### [1] [PRIME: Perception Feedback with Situational Memory Embeddings in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22040)
*Erik Deinzer,Naya Baslan,Luca Paparusso,Narunas Vaskevicius,Peter Knott,Luigi Palmieri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRIME 为自动驾驶 VLA 引入基于情境记忆的学习反馈机制，使感知查询受历史感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为条件化，以极低参数开销显著提升闭环驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 自动驾驶模型主要采用前馈式感知—推理—规划流程；尽管感知模块具备时序递归，但早期感知对下游推理与导航目标不可见，视觉输入处理缺乏目标导向，未能优先关注由先前决策驱动的线索。

Method: 提出 PRIME，一种学习反馈机制，将 VLA 感知查询条件化于新的情境记忆（Situational Memory）。其通过交叉注意力聚合 L 步窗口内过去感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为的潜在表示，实现意图驱动的感知注意力；最多仅增加 29.7M 参数，占 7.3B 基座模型的 0.41%。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 闭环基准上，PRIME 取得 82.47 的驾驶得分（较 ORION 提升 4.73）和 60.00% 的成功率（提升 5.38 个百分点），为基于 Think2Drive 演示训练的已发表 VLA 中报告的最高驾驶得分。

Conclusion: PRIME 以极小参数开销将下游意图反馈注入感知，验证了意图驱动感知注意力在闭环自动驾驶 VLA 中的有效性。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving operate primarily through feedforward inference across the perception--reasoning--planning hierarchy. While modern architectures maintain temporal recurrence within the perceptual module, early perception remains blind to downstream reasoning and navigation goals, processing visual inputs agnostically without prioritizing cues informed by prior decisions. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces PRIME, a learned feedback mechanism that conditions the VLA perceptual queries on a novel Situational Memory. By aggregating latent representations of past perception, reasoning, navigation goals, and predicted behaviors across an L-step window via cross-attention, PRIME enables intent-driven perceptual attention at minimal computational cost, adding only a maximum of 29.7M parameters (0.41% of the 7.3B-parameter base model). Evaluated on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, PRIME achieves a state-of-the-art Driving Score of 82.47 (+4.73 over ORION) and a Success Rate of 60.00% (+5.38 percentage points), the highest reported Driving Score among published VLAs trained on Think2Drive demonstrations.

</details>


### [2] [SFVO: Decoupled Confidence-Guided Stereo-Flow Visual Odometry with Bidirectional PnP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21754)
*Kai Zhang,Guoyang Zhao,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SFVO是一种对应关系驱动的双目视觉里程计框架，基于预训练立体匹配与光流模型，通过解耦旋转和平移置信度，实现鲁棒、准确且泛化性强的位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习视觉里程计多采用单目方案，存在尺度模糊问题；双目视觉里程计虽能提供真实尺度，但因计算成本高和建模复杂而研究较少。近期立体匹配与光流估计的进展使稠密视觉对应更准确可靠，但其互补的几何信息尚未在视觉里程计中得到充分利用。

Method: 提出SFVO，直接基于预训练立体匹配和光流模型估计立体与时序对应关系。不直接从图像学习位姿，而是将学习到的对应关系映射为几何约束，并预测哪些点可信。为提升基于视觉对应几何约束的可靠性，引入分别针对旋转和平移的解耦置信度图，以更好对齐视觉对应特征与6自由度变换。

Result: 在户外和室内数据集上的大量实验表明，SFVO能够实现鲁棒且准确的位姿估计，并具有强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SFVO验证了利用预训练对应模型与解耦置信度进行双目视觉里程计的可行性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Deep learning-based visual odometry (VO) has achieved significant progress, yet most existing methods focus on a monocular approach, which suffers from scale ambiguity. Stereo VO provides real metric by its nature, but remains less studied in deep learning VO due to its high computational cost and modeling complexity. Recent advances in stereo matching and optical flow estimation have made dense visual correspondence increasingly accurate and reliable, but their complementary geometric information has not been fully exploited for VO. In this paper, we present SFVO, a correspondence-driven stereo VO framework that directly builds upon pretrained stereo matching and optical flow models. SFVO exploits pretrained stereo matching and optical flow models to estimate stereo and temporal correspondences. Instead of learning pose directly from images, SFVO maps learned correspondences into geometric constraints and predicts which points are trustworthy. To improve the reliability of visual correspondence-based geometric constraints, we introduce decoupled confidence maps for rotation and translation. This design better aligns the characteristics of visual correspondence and 6-DoF transformations. Extensive experiments on outdoor and indoor datasets demonstrate that SFVO achieves robust and accurate pose estimation with strong generalization capability. The code will be released.

</details>


### [3] [2D GauSS-MI: Efficient Active Scene Reconstruction with Balanced Visual and Geometric Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21516)
*Yuhan Xie,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于2D高斯泼溅（2DGS）的主动重建框架，通过在线增量RGB-D建图与2D GauSS-MI信息增益度量进行高效主动视角选择，在视觉与几何重建质量之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动重建需要在有限机载算力下高效选择视角；现有方法难以同时兼顾视觉/几何重建质量与实时计算效率。

Method: 构建面向增量RGB-D观测的高效在线2DGS建图流程；提出刻画单个2D高斯泼溅视角相关重建质量的概率可靠性模型；基于该模型提出2D GauSS-MI，利用2DGS显式表面朝向计算候选视角的期望信息增益，用于主动视角选择，并同时考虑视觉与几何重建质量。

Result: 在八个Replica场景上与三种SOTA基线对比，方法在视觉与几何重建质量之间取得良好平衡，计算开销显著降低，模型存储具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 2D GauSS-MI能有效指导主动视角选择，实现兼顾视觉/几何质量与计算效率的主动重建。

Abstract: Active reconstruction requires efficient active view selection to achieve high-quality reconstruction within limited onboard computational resources. Existing methods face challenges in adequately balancing visual and geometric quality with the computational efficiency required for real-time operation. In this work, we present an active reconstruction framework based on 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS). We develop an efficient online 2DGS mapping pipeline for incremental RGB-D observations and introduce a probabilistic reliability model that characterizes the view-dependent reconstruction quality of individual 2D Gaussian splats. Building on this model, we formulate 2D Gaussian Splatting Shannon Mutual Information (2D GauSS-MI), a mutual-information-based metric that exploits the explicit surface orientation of 2DGS to evaluate the expected information gain of candidate views. The proposed metric enables active view selection to account for both visual and geometric reconstruction quality. We evaluate the proposed system against three state-of-the-art baselines on eight Replica scenes. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves a favorable balance between visual and geometric reconstruction quality with substantially lower computational cost and competitive model storage.

</details>


### [4] [Adaptive World Memory 3D Foundation Model for Scalable 3D Mapping, Localization, and Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21502)
*Tianchen Deng,Guole Shen,Yilin Shen,Wenhua Wu,Yilin Fang,Ziqi Ma,Tianjun Zhang,Shenghai Yuan,Wolfram Burgard,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个以记忆为中心的3D基础模型，用于可扩展的机器人定位、重建与高斯渲染。核心是自适应世界记忆机制，结合Transformer门控更新与测试时时空调节，并统一位姿估计、密集点云重建和逼真渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型虽能从RGB图像进行泛化几何推理，但在持久记忆、可扩展性和可渲染场景建模方面仍受限。

Method: 核心为自适应世界记忆机制：用基于Transformer的门控更新结合测试时时空调节；学习门控控制循环记忆传播，时序状态演化与空间观测-状态一致性调节长图像序列上的token级更新与遗忘。为支持大规模建图，将记忆组织为局部子图，并集成渐进式建图与跟踪、回环检测及基于SL(4)的全局优化。高斯重建头将记忆增强特征解码为可渲染基元，在单一模型中统一相机位姿估计、密集点云重建与照片级渲染。

Result: 在公开基准和来自多种机器人平台的自采数据集上，相比现有3D基础重建与SLAM基线，轨迹精度、重建完整性和渲染质量均有提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明自适应记忆可作为持久机器人世界建模的基础；数据集和代码将公开。

Abstract: Recent 3D foundation models enable generalizable geometric reasoning from RGB images but remain limited in persistent memory, scalability, and renderable scene modeling. We present a memory-centric 3D foundation model for scalable robotic localization, reconstruction, and Gaussian rendering. Its core is an adaptive world memory mechanism that combines transformer-based gated updates with test-time temporal-spatial regulation. Learned gates control recurrent memory propagation, while temporal state evolution and spatial observation-state consistency regulate token-wise updates and forgetting over long image sequences. To support large-scale mapping, we organize memory into local submaps and integrate progressive mapping and tracking, loop closure, and SL(4)-based global refinement to maintain local accuracy and global consistency. A Gaussian reconstruction head decodes memory-enhanced features into renderable primitives, unifying camera pose estimation, dense point-cloud reconstruction, and photorealistic rendering within a single model. Experiments on public benchmarks and self-collected datasets from diverse robotic platforms demonstrate improved trajectory accuracy, reconstruction completeness, and rendering quality over existing 3D foundation reconstruction and SLAM baselines. These results support adaptive memory as a foundation for persistent robotic world modeling. The dataset and code will be made publicly available at \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [5] [MT-WAM: Reorienting the One-Pass Predictive Representation Toward Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21474)
*Yiguang Yang,Jiankun Peng,Xiaoming Wang,Yiran Zhang,Zhibo Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MT-WAM 在 Fast-WAM 的视频-动作协同训练框架上，增加未来二维点轨迹与视觉特征作为辅助监督，使视频主干为动作生成提供更优的动态与空间上下文；推理时不生成未来视频，仅使用每次重规划计算一次的 video/motion 缓存，从而在多个基准和真实任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fast-WAM 虽通过视频-动作协同训练提升控制，且推理时无需生成未来视频，但其未来观测预测目标并未显式优先学习控制所需的未来动态和视觉结构，导致视频表示对动作生成的指导不足。

Method: 保留原始训练目标，新增未来二维点轨迹和未来视觉特征的互补监督。从视频主干最后若干块复制出轻量双流分支，分别进行目标特定处理；用结构化注意力掩码阻止跨流注意力。运动流 token 为动作专家提供额外动态条件；未来视觉特征预测在能捕捉物体与空间结构的特征空间中进行监督，以训练视频主干在视觉条件变化下为动作生成提供更有信息量的视觉上下文，同时不把视觉特征流 token 加入动作条件。推理时，MT-WAM 每次重规划只计算一次 video 与 motion 缓存，并跳过未来视频预测。

Result: 无需额外具身策略预训练，MT-WAM 在 LIBERO 达 98.2%，在 LIBERO-Plus 达 73.7%，后者超过 Fast-WAM 23.8 个百分点。RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Rand 上 Random 成功率从 6.30% 提升至 19.40%；四个真实世界任务平均成功率从 67.0% 提升至 77.8%。

Conclusion: 显式监督未来运动轨迹与视觉特征，可在不生成未来视频、不增加推理视频预测负担的前提下，增强视频主干表示对动作生成的支持，并显著提升跨基准与真实场景的控制成功率。

Abstract: Fast-WAM shows that video-action co-training improves control without generating future video at inference, making the representation from a single video diffusion Transformer forward central to action generation. However, future-observation prediction does not explicitly prioritize the future dynamics and visual structure needed for control. We present MT-WAM, which retains the original training objectives and adds complementary supervision for future two-dimensional point trajectories and visual features. A lightweight dual-stream branch copied from the video backbone's final blocks provides target-specific processing, while a structured attention mask prevents cross-stream attention. Motion-stream tokens supply additional dynamics conditions to the action expert. Future visual-feature prediction provides supervision in a feature space that captures object and spatial structure. This supervision trains the video backbone to provide more informative visual context for action generation under changing visual conditions, without adding visual-feature-stream tokens to action conditioning. At inference, MT-WAM uses video and motion caches computed once per replan and skips future-video prediction. Without additional embodied policy pretraining, MT-WAM achieves 98.2% success on LIBERO and 73.7% on LIBERO-Plus, exceeding Fast-WAM by 23.8 percentage points on the latter. On RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Rand, Random success increases from 6.30% to 19.40%; across four real-world tasks, average success increases from 67.0% to 77.8%.

</details>


### [6] [A Scene Language Model for Open-Vocabulary Scene Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21400)
*Adam Lilja,Fabio Hübel,Siming He,Junsheng Fu,Claire Tomlin,Lars Hammarstrand,Jitendra Malik,Jonas Frey,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SceneLM，用单个视觉语言模型直接维护结构化的文本场景地图，实现轻量、开放词汇的 3D 场景建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇 3D 场景建图依赖人工设计的建图流程，并常存储嵌入或图像裁剪等特征丰富的物体表示，导致持久内存庞大且系统复杂。

Method: SceneLM 将完整场景表示为结构化的物体文本列表，作为模型唯一持久内存；对每张输入图像，模型读取当前场景状态并通过添加、编辑、删除物体来更新地图。为学习该行为，引入迭代式场景地图维护监督任务，以及无需人工标注、从图像自动生成训练数据的标注流程。

Result: 在语言引导检索基准和定位基准上，SceneLM 生成的场景地图性能与由专用感知和几何模块构建的完整建图系统相当，但场景表示紧凑 6-12 倍；进一步在四足机器人上验证了其可在边缘设备在线运行。

Conclusion: 持久化的开放词汇 3D 场景地图可由单个视觉语言模型仅使用轻量文本表示直接维护；训练与推理代码已公开。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene mapping aims to build a persistent representation of the objects in an environment. Existing systems typically rely on engineered mapping pipelines to associate observations, merge information across views, and maintain a consistent scene representation over time. Many additionally store feature-rich object representations, such as embeddings or image crops, increasing the size and complexity of the persistent memory. We introduce SceneLM, a Scene-Language Model that directly maintains a textual scene map. The full scene is represented as a structured text list of objects, which serves as the model's only persistent memory. For each input image, the model reads the current scene state and updates the map by adding, editing, and removing objects. To learn this behavior, we introduce supervision tasks for iterative scene map maintenance together with an automatic annotation pipeline that generates training data from images without human labels. We evaluate SceneLM on both a language-grounded retrieval benchmark and a localization benchmark. Across both benchmarks, the model produces a scene map that achieves competitive performance with complete mapping systems built from dedicated perception and geometric modules while producing a scene representation that is 6-12x more compact. We further show that SceneLM can be run online on an edge device through experiments on a quadruped. These results show that a persistent open-vocabulary 3D scene map can be maintained directly by a single vision-language model using only a lightweight text representation. Training and inference code is available on this https URL .

</details>


### [7] [Multi-viewpoint Geo-localization with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21219)
*Adam D. Hines,Michael Milford,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出事件相机视觉位置识别系统MegaEvent，通过Image-to-Event转换大规模帧数据集并微调事件ViT，显著提升视角变化下的定位鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人定位需容忍视角变化，事件相机虽受关注，但现有事件定位器对视角变化研究不足，且强调视角变化的挑战性事件数据集稀缺。

Method: 将五个大规模地理标签帧基定位数据集经I2E转换为合成事件流，用于微调预训练事件视觉Transformer骨干，并采用多损失函数训练，得到MegaEvent，以学习视角鲁棒的位置识别特征。

Result: 在三个现有事件定位数据集上平均Recall@1达82%，分别领先次优事件方法20个召回点和直接应用于事件帧的帧基VPR模型8至26个召回点；新数据集Springfield-Event-VPR包含3.7km步行路线、三种相机朝向、总长11.1km，MegaEvent比最强基线高9个召回点。

Conclusion: MegaEvent在视角变化下的事件视觉位置识别中表现优越，并提供了具有挑战性的新数据集与开源代码。

Abstract: Robot localization is an ongoing challenge that demands mapping and positioning systems that are tolerant to viewpoint change. Event cameras are attracting increasing interest and adoption in robotics; however, dealing with viewpoint variance is an under-investigated problem in existing event-based localizers. In addition, event-based datasets that emphasize viewpoint variance for challenging localization situations are scarce. Here, we introduce an event-based visual place recognition (VPR) system that performs robustly under viewpoint changes. We converted five large-scale geo-tagged datasets, conventionally used to train frame-based localization systems, into synthetic event streams using Image-to-Event (I2E) conversion, and used them to fine-tune a pre-trained event-based vision transformer backbone with a multi-loss function, yielding a system we call MegaEvent that learns viewpoint-robust features for place recognition. We achieved an average Recall@1 of 82% across three existing event-based localization datasets, leading the next best event-based method by 20 recall points, and frame-based VPR models applied directly to event frames by 8 to 26 recall points. We introduce a new, challenging dataset - Springfield-Event-VPR - which features a 3.7km walking route recorded in three camera orientations for a total of 11.1km, which MegaEvent outperforms the strongest baseline by 9 recall points. The code for MegaEvent is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [8] [AnyviewMeter: Adapting Robotic Reward Models with Camera Geometry and Multi-View Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20106)
*Yuang Tu,Runjia Tan,Yujie Yan,Jinghan Hu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnyviewMeter：一种基于相机几何条件化的机器人奖励模型适应框架，通过 Plücker 射线与同步块注意力提升单/多视角任务进度奖励预测对视角和遮挡的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励模型从视觉观测评估任务执行，但预测会随相机视角和遮挡变化，即使底层任务状态不变；因此将预训练奖励模型适配到局部任务时，必须考虑任务是如何被观测的。

Method: AnyviewMeter 将低秩微调与 token 对齐的 Plücker 射线及同步块注意力结合：射线条件化把相机几何注入视觉特征以及注意力查询和键，块注意力在预训练解码器内融合同步视角；该框架基于预训练 Robometer 模型进行参数高效适应，支持单视角奖励预测与联合多视角评估。

Result: 在 PickCube 上，单视角适应在每个相机组均改善进度预测，并在视场角变化下将平均绝对误差较 RGB 微调降低约 21%；在模拟操作任务中，联合多视角预测较平均单视角 RGB 预测将进度误差降低 41–69%，并在约 88% 的任务-相机组中改善时序排序；在固定相机与腕部相机的真实任务上，平均绝对误差较平均 RGB 微调降低约 21%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，相机几何与联合视觉证据是任务特定机器人奖励适应中的有效组成部分。

Abstract: Robotic reward models evaluate task execution from visual observations, but their predictions can change with camera viewpoint and occlusion even when the underlying task state is unchanged. Adapting a pretrained reward model to a local task therefore requires accounting for how that task is observed. We introduce AnyviewMeter, a geometry-conditioned adaptation framework for robotic reward models that represent task progress as a scalar reward signal. It combines low-rank fine-tuning with token-aligned Plucker rays and synchronous block attention: ray conditioning incorporates camera geometry into visual features and attention queries and keys, while block attention fuses synchronized views inside the pretrained decoder. The framework supports both single-view reward prediction and joint multi-view evaluation through parameter-efficient adaptation of a pretrained Robometer model. On PickCube, single-view adaptation improves progress prediction in every camera group and reduces mean absolute error under a changed field of view by approximately 21% relative to RGB fine-tuning. Across simulated manipulation tasks, joint multi-view prediction reduces progress error by 41-69% compared with averaging single-view RGB predictions and improves temporal ordering in approximately 88% of task-camera groups. On real tasks with fixed and wrist-mounted cameras, mean absolute error decreases by approximately 21% relative to averaged RGB fine-tuning. These results support camera geometry and joint visual evidence as useful components of task-specific robotic reward adaptation.

</details>

### [9] [A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Framework for Perceptual Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21221)
*Hongyan Wei,Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端可微的神经-软符号框架，将视觉感知与任务规划统一到单一计算图中，通过连续软符号状态、可微软-T_P转移算子和短时域动作logits优化，实现规划目标梯度反传至感知；在Blocksworld上显著优于LatPlan和推理模型基线，并验证了与下游机器人运动执行的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知规划任务需同时准确感知不确定场景并依据逻辑规则规划有效动作序列。传统方法将感知离散化为符号事实后再规划，丢弃感知不确定性，且切断任务级反馈对感知的更新。

Method: 构建通用全可微神经-软符号框架：维持连续软符号状态；将领域规则提升为可微软-T_P转移算子；在短规划时域上优化动作logits；规划目标梯度可更新感知参数，从而在规划过程中细化任务相关感知表示。

Result: 在Blocksworld上，方法解决LatPlan-40的40/40任务与PlanBench-600的596/600任务，优于LatPlan的33/40和推理模型基线的587/600，且计算量和时间显著更少；感知不确定性消融中，成功率从冻结感知的59%提升至83%；进一步的任务与运动仿真验证了解码任务计划与下游机器人运动执行的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将感知与规划纳入统一可微的软符号计算图可保留感知不确定性并实现任务级反馈，从而提升规划成功率与效率，并具备向机器人执行层衔接的潜力。

Abstract: Perceptual planning tasks require two key capabilities: accurately perceiving uncertain scenes and planning valid action sequences following logical rules. Conventional methods convert perception into discrete symbolic facts and then plan, discarding perceptual uncertainty and severing task-level feedback to perception. We introduce a generic, fully differentiable neuro-soft-symbolic framework that connects visual perception and task planning within a single computational graph. The framework maintains a continuous soft symbolic state, lifts domain rules into a differentiable soft-$T_P$ transition operator, and optimizes action logits over a short planning horizon. Gradients from the planning objective can also update the perception parameters, allowing task-relevant perceptual representations to be refined during planning. On Blocksworld, our method solves 40/40 LatPlan-40 tasks and 596/600 PlanBench-600 tasks, compared with 33/40 for LatPlan and 587/600 for the reasoning-model baseline, while requiring substantially less computation and time. In the perceptual-uncertainty ablation, our method improves the success rate from 59\% with frozen perception to 83\%. We further conduct task-and-motion simulations on Blocksworld scenes, providing an execution-level validation of the compatibility between decoded task plans and downstream robotic motion execution.

</details>

### [10] [WM-VS: Progress-Aligned World Models for Closed-Loop Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20892)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yixuan Zhou,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WM-VS：一种面向闭环视觉伺服、以目标为中心且对齐任务进度的世界模型框架，用于解决“预测动作合理”与“动作能否降低任务误差”之间的预测-控制不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环视觉伺服中的世界模型若仅预测动作是否合理，无法保证动作能减少任务误差，即存在预测-控制不匹配；因此需要预测动作后果是否推动误差收缩。

Method: 离线用目标区域 DINOv2 对应关系定义带符号的四维伺服坐标：平移、尺度与平面内旋转。阶段1将动作条件潜变量转移与该坐标对齐；阶段2冻结世界模型，训练反应式关节速度策略，结合动作模仿、后果监督以及偏向误差收缩的短时想象 rollout。部署仅用 RGB，反应式执行，不进行在线轨迹优化。

Result: 在真实 7-DoF eye-to-hand 系统上，WM-VS 在 30/30 次试验中使角点 RMSE 不超过初始值的 10%，并在最终有效帧保持该标准 25/30（83.33%）。移除未来误差对齐后保持率降至 26.67%。学习到的进度信号与未用于训练或控制的外部 AprilTag 角点误差一致，平均 Spearman rho = 0.8778。无需重训练，两个未见 3D 目标的平移误差分别降低 86.48% 和 90.27%，旋转误差分别降低 70.01% 和 65.70%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将动作后果与任务进度对齐，可有效支持重复闭环误差修正与迁移；代码和数据将开源发布。

Abstract: Closed-loop visual servoing requires predictions that indicate whether an action reduces task error, not only whether the action is plausible. We call this gap the prediction-control mismatch and introduce WM-VS, a target-centric progress-aligned world-model framework for closed-loop visual servoing. Offline target-region DINOv2 correspondences define a signed four-dimensional servo coordinate for translation, scale, and in-plane rotation. Stage 1 aligns action-conditioned latent transitions with this coordinate; Stage 2 freezes the world model and trains a reactive joint-velocity policy with action imitation, consequence supervision, and short imagined rollouts that favor error contraction. Deployment is RGB-only and reactive, without online trajectory optimization. On a real 7-DoF eye-to-hand system, WM-VS reaches a corner RMSE no larger than 10 percent of its initial value in 30/30 trials and retains this criterion at the final valid frame in 25/30 (83.33 percent). Removing future-error alignment reduces retention to 26.67 percent. The learned progress signal agrees with an external AprilTag corner error not used for training or control (mean Spearman rho = 0.8778). Without retraining, two unseen 3D targets achieve translation-error reductions of 86.48 percent and 90.27 percent and rotation-error reductions of 70.01 percent and 65.70 percent. These results link progress-aligned action consequences to repeated closed-loop correction and transfer. Code and data will be released as open source.

</details>


### [11] [DPed-VLN: A Benchmark for Socially Compliant Vision-and-Language Navigation in Dynamic Pedestrian Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21504)
*Haojie Dai,Xiangyi Wang,Liuyi Wang,Kai Sheng,Zongtao He,Chengju Liu,Wei Ye,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 DPed-VLN：基于 Habitat 3.0 的动态行人视觉语言导航基准，包含 33,093 个导航回合、全局与先验增强指令、ORCA 控制的仿真行人、社会约束专家路径及联合评估导航效率与社会安全的指标；同时提出 DPet 策略网络，并验证 LoRA 微调可提升 VLM 导航模型安全性，DPet-RL 在 SR、SPL、STL 上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN 研究多集中于静态室内环境，而真实机器人需在有人类活动的空间中依据语言导航，同时应对移动行人并满足社会安全约束。因此，需要一个能系统评估动态行人场景下导航效率与社会安全的基准，并支持对动态行人线索进行受控分析。

Method: 构建 DPed-VLN 基准：耦合 33,093 个导航回合，提供配对的全局指令与先验增强指令，采用 ORCA 控制的人形行人，生成社会约束专家路径，并使用同时衡量导航效率与社会安全的指标；将普通目标导向路径引导与暴露动态行人线索的先验增强指令分离。提出 DPet（Dynamic Pedestrian-aware Network）策略网络，结合强化学习与模仿学习训练。进一步通过 LoRA 微调将 NaVILA、StreamVLN 等代表性 VLM 导航模型适配到 DPed-VLN。

Result: 实验表明，LoRA 适配可提升零样本 VLM 基线在多个成功率和安全性指标上的表现，尤其显著降低 StreamVLN 的碰撞率。在所有评估方法中，DPet-RL 取得最高的 SR、SPL 和 STL。

Conclusion: DPed-VLN 为动态行人环境下的 VLN 提供了受控评测平台，表明结合行人感知的策略学习（DPet-RL）在导航效率与社会安全上优于现有 VLM 基线；LoRA 微调也能有效增强 VLM 导航模型在动态行人场景中的安全表现。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) has advanced rapidly in static indoor environments, but robots operating in human-populated spaces must ground language while responding to moving pedestrians and social-safety constraints. We present DPed-VLN, a Habitat 3.0 benchmark for dynamic-pedestrian VLN that couples 33,093 navigation episodes with paired global and prior-augmented instructions, ORCA-controlled humanoid pedestrians, socially constrained expert paths, and metrics that jointly assess navigation efficiency and social safety. DPed-VLN separates ordinary goal-oriented route guidance from prior-augmented instructions that expose dynamic-pedestrian cues for controlled analysis. To instantiate the benchmark, we introduce DPet (Dynamic Pedestrian-aware Network), a pedestrian-aware policy network trained with reinforcement learning and imitation learning. We further adapt representative state-of-the-art VLM-based navigation models, including NaVILA and StreamVLN, to DPed-VLN through LoRA fine-tuning. Experiments show that LoRA adaptation improves zero-shot VLM baselines in several success and safety metrics, especially reducing StreamVLN's collision rate. Among the evaluated methods, DPet-RL achieves the highest SR, SPL, and STL.

</details>


### [12] [FORTE: Task-Adaptive Force Capability Optimization for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21497)
*Xiao Wang,Heng Zhang,Gokhan Solak,Fei Zhao,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向任务的力能力优化框架：用VLM从图像与任务描述推断物体物理属性并生成任务力序列，以任务力不确定性球与动态残余力多面体（RFP）的有符号距离作为力能力指标，与可操作性等目标联合进行全身多目标轨迹优化，实现重载保力、轻载保灵活的任务自适应平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人物理交互控制既需运动学可行，也需足够的力交互能力；现有冗余分解方法常忽略任务特定力需求，或盲目最大化力能力，在无需大力余量时牺牲了可操作性。

Method: VLM从RGB图像与任务描述推断物体物理属性，生成捕捉重力与惯性需求的期望任务力序列；定义任务力不确定性球与动态残余力多面体（RFP）间的有符号距离作为面向任务的力能力指标，量化任务需求与机器人剩余驱动能力的兼容性；将该指标与可操作性、关节限位规避、轨迹平滑性和基座振荡抑制共同纳入全身多目标轨迹优化问题。

Result: 在移动机械臂执行举升与单点保持任务、不同负载条件下，所提方法对重载提供足够力能力，对轻载保持高可操作性；相较固定能力最大化基线（RFP内切半径、RFP锥）及仅可操作性优化，实现了任务自适应的力-灵活平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架可依据任务需求自适应权衡力能力与可操作性，核心实现已公开。

Abstract: Effective physical interaction control in robotic manipulation requires not only kinematically feasible motion but also sufficient force-interaction capability. Existing redundancy resolution methods often ignore task-specific force demands or maximize the force capability indiscriminately, sacrificing dexterity when large force margins are unnecessary. We propose a task-oriented force capability optimization framework for redundant mobile manipulators. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) infers object physical properties from an RGB image and a task description, generating a desired task-force sequence that captures gravitational and inertial demands. We then define a task-oriented force capability metric as the signed distance between a task-force uncertainty ball and the dynamic residual force polytope (RFP), quantifying compatibility between task demands and the robot's remaining actuation capacity. This metric is incorporated, alongside manipulability, joint-limit avoidance, trajectory smoothness, and base-oscillation suppression, into a whole-body multi-objective trajectory-optimization problem. Experiments on a mobile manipulator performing lifting and single-point-holding tasks under varying payload conditions demonstrate that the proposed method provides sufficient force capability for heavy loads while preserving high manipulability for light loads. This yields a task-adaptive balance that fixed capability-maximizing baselines (RFP inscribed radius, RFP cone) and manipulability-only optimization fail to achieve. The core implementation is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [13] [Adaptive Rollout Truncation Based on Epistemic Uncertainty for Efficient Offline World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21482)
*Nikodem Sebastian Zymla,Laurin Thiele,Johannes Pitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于认知不确定性的自适应 rollout 策略，用于离线世界模型训练：在自课程训练中，当认知不确定性超过预热阶段校准的阈值时提前终止自回归 rollout；在 ANYmal-D 与 ANT 上，基于集成的自适应截断在匹配或提升固定 horizon 训练及 RWM-U 基线预测精度的同时，显著减少累积 rollout 步数，ANYmal-D 上约减少 72% rollout 计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的机器人依赖精确神经世界模型预测未来状态。多步自回归训练虽能提升长时程预测，但固定 rollout horizon 会增加计算成本，并在模型尚不准确时放大早期训练误差；现有训练方案通常在优化全程使用相同 rollout 长度，未考虑模型当前预测可靠性。

Method: 面向离线世界模型训练，提出认知不确定性驱动的自适应 rollout 策略，并采用自动课程式训练。模型不再始终展开到固定 horizon，而是在自回归 rollout 中一旦认知不确定性超过由预热阶段校准的阈值便终止。研究两类不确定性估计器：共享循环骨干的五头集成与 Monte Carlo Dropout。采用两阶段预热流程，在启用自适应截断前稳定不确定性估计。

Result: 在 ANYmal-D 和 ANT 上，基于集成的自适应截断在预测精度上匹配或优于固定 horizon 训练及 RWM-U 基线，同时所需累积 rollout 步数显著更少。在 ANYmal-D 上，按该方法训练世界模型可在最终性能与基线相当的情况下，减少约 72% 的 rollout 计算量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，认知不确定性不仅可用于下游策略正则化，也可用于提升世界模型训练本身的计算效率，使离线世界模型训练在保持预测性能的同时减少 rollout 计算。

Abstract: Accurate neural world models are central to model-based robotics, where they enable robots to predict future states from previously observed trajectories. Multi-step autoregressive training improves long-horizon prediction, but fixed rollout horizons also increase computational cost and can amplify early training errors when the model is still inaccurate. Existing training schemes typically use the same rollout length throughout optimization, independent of the model's current predictive reliability. We propose an epistemic uncertainty-driven adaptive rollout strategy for offline world model training following an auto-curriculum training scheme. Instead of always unrolling to a fixed horizon, the model terminates autoregressive rollouts once epistemic uncertainty exceeds a threshold calibrated from a warm-up phase. We study two uncertainty estimators: a five-head ensemble with a shared recurrent backbone and Monte Carlo Dropout. A two-stage warm-up procedure stabilizes uncertainty estimates before we enable adaptive truncation. Experiments on ANYmal-D and ANT show that ensemble-based adaptive truncation matches or improves the prediction accuracy of fixed-horizon training and the RWM-U baseline while requiring substantially fewer cumulative rollout steps. Training a world model on ANYmal-D following the presented approach reaches comparable final performance with the baselines with roughly 72% less rollout computation. These results indicate that epistemic uncertainty is useful not only for downstream policy regularization, but also for making world model training itself more compute-efficient.

</details>


### [14] [Learning Distance-Conditioned Object Transport for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from a Single Motion Clip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21467)
*Yuhyeon Hwang,Daniel Sungho Jung,YongHyeok Seo,Mingi Jung,Chang Nho Cho,Jung-Hoon Hwang,Dongin Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出距离条件参考重组（DCRR），将演示终止段重定位到中间运输状态，生成距离条件监督并蒸馏为无参考策略，实现人形loco-manipulation的距离可控运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一重定向运动片段训练的策略主要复现演示终点的运输结果；源轨迹虽经过中间物体位移，但终止仅展示在终点，存在“终止-经过”差距，中间位移只是经过状态而非终止完成结果。需要从源运动中构造中间距离的终止监督，以实现指令依赖的运输距离调节。

Method: DCRR将演示终止片段重定位到中间运输状态，形成重组合参考；冻结的跟踪教师策略在闭环动力学中重放这些参考；保留轨迹按实际物体放置结果重新标注；蒸馏为无参考策略。该过程从源运动编码的交互行为中构造距离条件监督，并可进一步进行RL微调。

Result: 在Carry、Kick-Push、Crouch-Push、Drag四种模式下，DCRR-BC产生指令依赖运输，整体归一化距离MAE为0.15，优于仅源行为克隆的0.28；RL微调进一步改善训练仿真器中的指令响应与执行鲁棒性及sim-to-sim迁移；硬件实验展示四种交互模式的运输距离调制。

Conclusion: DCRR能有效利用源运动中的交互行为构建距离条件监督，实现人形loco-manipulation的可控运输距离，并在仿真与硬件实验中验证可行性。

Abstract: Motion tracking can reproduce humanoid loco-manipulation from a single retargeted motion clip, but a policy trained on a fixed reference primarily reproduces its demonstrated transport outcome. Although the source trajectory visits intermediate object displacements, transport termination is demonstrated only at its endpoint. We identify this mismatch as the termination-versus-passage gap: intermediate displacements are observed as passage states rather than termination-complete outcomes. We introduce Distance-Conditioned Reference Recomposition (DCRR), which relocates the demonstrated termination segment to intermediate transport states. A frozen tracking teacher replays the recomposed references under closed-loop dynamics, and the retained trajectories are relabeled by their achieved object placements and distilled into a reference-free policy. This procedure constructs distance-conditioned supervision from the interaction behavior encoded in the source motion. Across Carry, Kick-Push, Crouch-Push, and Drag, DCRR-BC produces command-dependent transport with an overall normalized distance mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.15, compared with 0.28 for source-only behavior cloning. RL fine-tuning further improves the command response and execution robustness in the training simulator and under sim-to-sim transfer. Finally, hardware experiments demonstrate transport-distance modulation across all four interaction modes.

</details>


### [15] [AtomEgo: Exploring Ego-Robot Integration for Embodied Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21461)
*Di Wu,Dongchen Zheng,Junhe Sheng,Zhongxing Wei,Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Xiaoquan Sun,Junyang Zheng,Zhuoyang Song,Jiaxing Zhang,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 AtomEgo，对自我中心人类数据与机器人数据联合预训练进行系统研究，基于约2659小时语料和可扩展流水线，发现数据规模与对齐质量共同决定能力增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型受限于机器人演示数据的规模与多样性，因此需要利用大规模第一人称人类交互数据；但人机在具身形态和动作空间上存在显著鸿沟，如何有效将此类数据融入具身模型预训练尚不明确。

Method: 构建约2659小时语料与可扩展数据处理流水线；在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）和世界-动作模型架构上，研究三种代表性范式：带领域特定动作头的联合共训练、通过具身对齐的渐进式自我中心到机器人迁移、联合视频-动作建模；通过多任务真机实验和语言条件跨具身表征分析进行评估。

Result: 结果揭示一个简单原则：Data Scale * Alignment Quality -> Capability Gain；自我中心数据能够提升泛化能力，但其价值取决于数据对齐与利用的有效程度。

Conclusion: 该原则可为可扩展的自我中心-机器人预训练提供实践指导。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are constrained by the limited scale and diversity of robot demonstrations, motivating the use of large-scale egocentric human interaction data. However, how to effectively incorporate such data into embodied-model pre-training remains unclear because of substantial embodiment and action-space gaps between humans and robots. We present AtomEgo, a systematic study of ego--robot co-training supported by a curated corpus of approximately 2,659 hours and a scalable data processing pipeline. Across vision--language--action and world--action model architectures, we investigate three representative paradigms: joint co-training with domain-specific action heads, progressive ego-to-robot transfer through embodiment alignment, and joint video--action modeling. We evaluate these paradigms through multi-task real-robot experiments and language-conditioned cross-embodiment representation analysis. Our results reveal a simple principle: Data Scale * Alignment Quality --> Capability Gain; egocentric data can improve generalization, but their value depends on how effectively they are aligned and utilized. This principle can provide practical guidance for scalable ego--robot pre-training.

</details>


### [16] [Robotic Multiphase Interaction: Manipulating Coupled Liquid and Solid Dynamics with a World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21448)
*Yixuan Feng,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出机器人多相交互（RMI）设定：液体进入多孔材料并与可变形固体骨架发生机械耦合，机器人操作会同时改变孔隙体积、排出/重分布液体并影响抓取稳定性；作者以含水海绵为首个案例，构建耦合液-固动力学环境，并用动作条件世界模型与扩散策略/整流流匹配生成动作，显著降低保水预测与终端水量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作多针对固体，或仅处理空间分离的液-固任务；而液体浸入多孔可变形固体后，操作会耦合改变孔隙体积、液体分布与抓取稳定性，且液体溢出会带来家庭与制造场景的安全风险。因此需要研究并建模这种机器人多相交互。

Method: 以含水海绵为RMI首个实例，采用基于SPH的隐式不可压缩多孔流作为动力学引擎；通过库仑接触记忆、混合速度与力调节、以及抬升稳定性门控实现机器人操作，使指令与耦合液-固状态变化相连。使用动作条件世界模型预测候选指令下状态演化，并以时序UNet结合Diffusion Policy或整流流匹配生成动作。

Result: 世界模型相比基线将保水量预测误差降低超过60%；在世界模型从策略提案中选出的最佳动作序列，进一步将预测终端水量误差降低约一半。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对耦合液-固状态建模有助于机器人预测其动作如何同时影响多孔物体及内部液体，从而提升RMI任务中的操作预测与决策能力。

Abstract: This work presents \textit{Robotic Multiphase Interaction (RMI)}, a setting in which liquid enters a porous material and interacts mechanically with its deforming solid skeleton. Manipulation can therefore change pore volume, expel or redistribute retained liquid, and alter grasp stability at the same time. Spilled liquid can also create safety risks in domestic and manufacturing settings. This differs from most manipulation of solid objects and from tasks that involve both liquid and solid while keeping the phases spatially separate. We study a sponge filled with water as the first RMI example. We use implicit incompressible porous flow with smoothed particle hydrodynamics as the dynamics engine and enable robotic manipulation by adding Coulomb contact memory, hybrid velocity and force regulation, and a stability gate for lifting. The resulting environment connects robot commands to changes in the coupled liquid and solid state. A world model conditioned on actions predicts how this state evolves under candidate commands, while a temporal UNet generates actions using either Diffusion Policy or rectified flow matching. Our world model reduces retained water prediction error by more than $60\%$ compared with the baseline. The best action sequence selected by the world model from policy proposals further reduces the predicted terminal water error by about half. These improvements show that modelling the coupled liquid and solid state helps the robot predict how its actions affect both the porous object and the liquid held inside.

</details>


### [17] [FootQuery: Future-Touchdown-Guided Retrieval from Depth History for Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21447)
*Tao Dong,Jia Yu,Yuxuan Fan,Linna Zhao,Jiaqi Gong,Andong Yang,Chao Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FootQuery：利用足部预测落点查询历史深度，实现复杂地形上的感知式人形机器人运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在复杂地形上需要提前预判落脚点，但着地时目标区域可能已不可见；相机覆盖有限和自遮挡使得必须从早期观测中检索相关地形信息。

Method: 该框架用本体感觉预测下一步落脚位置及其不确定性，并结合逐足特征查询稀疏采样的历史深度帧。训练时将实际接触投影到历史图像中，以监督接触可见区域的特征检索。检索到的逐足特征与全局视觉记忆融合生成控制动作，同时采用渐进式力辅助课程支持早期探索，并用事件一致的踏步中线塑形促进协调的楼梯接触。部署时仅需本体感觉和机载深度图像。

Result: 仿真中，完整框架在最困难的楼梯、间隙和平台任务上优于各组件消融版本；在Unitree G1真机实验中，单一策略可连续通过户外楼梯，以及包含上下楼梯、平台和间隙的室内路线。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，围绕预期接触来组织视觉历史，是复杂地形下感知式人形机器人运动的有效思路。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion over complex terrain requires anticipating footholds that may no longer be visible at touchdown. Limited camera coverage and self-occlusion make it necessary to retrieve relevant terrain information from earlier observations. We present FootQuery, a perceptive locomotion framework that queries depth history using each foot's predicted next touchdown. The policy predicts touchdown locations and uncertainty from proprioception and uses these distributions, together with per-foot features, to query sparsely sampled historical depth frames. During training, realized contacts are projected into historical images to supervise retrieval at the regions where those contacts were visible. The retrieved per-foot features are fused with global visual memory to generate control actions. A progressive force-assistance curriculum supports early exploration, while event-consistent tread-midline shaping encourages coordinated stair contacts. Deployment requires only proprioception and onboard depth images. In simulation, the complete framework outperforms its component ablations on the most challenging tested stairs, gaps, and platforms. Real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 demonstrate continuous traversal with a single policy across outdoor stairs and indoor routes combining stair ascent and descent, platforms, and gaps. These results support organizing visual history around anticipated contacts for perceptive humanoid locomotion.

</details>


### [18] [A Unified Dynamic Force Guidance Framework for Performance-Optimized Kinesthetic Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21416)
*Chunxin Li,Jianhua Wu,Zhenhua Xiong,Xiangyang Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线动态力引导方法，结合可变导纳控制与虚拟力，在拖动示教中约束并优化机器人操作性能，实验证明可提升工作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人常用于频繁换产的工业场景，拖动示教虽直观，但用户易忽略机器人构型，导致操作性能下降。操作性能指机器人生成运动的能力，可由雅可比矩阵最小奇异值量化。

Method: 提出在线动态力引导方法，集成性能约束与优化机制：可变导纳控制使操作性能保持在预设阈值以上；虚拟力主动引导用户将机器人拖向性能更优的构型。在6自由度协作机器人上实验，对比任务空间三条典型路径，并通过轨迹回放分析操作性能与工作效率的关系。

Result: 所提方法有效提升了机器人操作性能，并由此提高了工作效率。

Conclusion: 该方法对降低工业生产节拍时间具有重要价值。

Abstract: Collaborative robots are increasingly deployed in industrial scenarios characterized by frequent product changeovers. As an intuitive programming method, kinesthetic teaching facilitates rapid robot deployment. However, users may overlook the configuration of the robot during kinesthetic teaching, leading to degradation in operational performance. Operational performance refers to the capability of the robot to generate motion and can be quantified by the Minimum Singular Value of the Jacobian matrix. To address this issue, this paper proposes an online dynamic force guidance method that integrates performance constraint and optimization mechanisms. Specifically, variable admittance control maintains the operational performance of the robot above a predefined threshold, while a virtual force actively guides the user to drag the robot towards configurations with improved performance. Experiments are conducted on a 6-DOF collaborative robot, comparing three typical paths in the task space. To evaluate the quality of the taught trajectories, trajectory playback experiments are conducted to analyze the relationship between the operational performance of the robot and the work efficiency. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively enhances the operational performance of the robot and consequently improves the work efficiency, holding significant value for reducing production takt time in industrial deployment.

</details>


### [19] [Stabilizing Trajectory Outputs in End-to-End Autonomous Driving via SC-IMM Based Teacher Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21404)
*Siewoo Kim,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于场景条件交互多模型（SC-IMM）的离线教师信号生成与学习方法，用于稳定端到端轨迹输出；在不改变推理结构和waypoint控制器的情况下，显著提升闭环驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于waypoint的模仿学习主要最小化坐标级误差，难以捕捉场景相关的路径-速度变化以及waypoint输出在时间上的不稳定性。

Method: 将专家轨迹转换为路径-速度状态，并以场景线索为条件执行IMM更新，生成路径-速度教师标签和模式后验概率；这些信号作为辅助监督加入原始轨迹损失进行训练，推理结构与waypoint控制器保持不变。

Result: 在CARLA Town12的100条短路线闭环评测中，相比基线驾驶得分提升28.0%，Collision/km降低62.3%，同时jerk和轨迹变化指标也有所改善。

Conclusion: 嵌入场景条件运动模型线索的离线教师信号能够引导轨迹输出驾驶模型实现更稳定的闭环行为。

Abstract: End-to-End autonomous driving models commonly predict future waypoints from sensor inputs and convert them into vehicle control commands through a downstream controller. However, conventional waypoint-based imitation learning mainly minimizes coordinate-level errors, making it difficult to capture scene-dependent path-speed changes and temporal instability across waypoint outputs. In this paper, we propose an offline teacher-signal generation and learning method for trajectory-output stabilization based on a Scene-Conditioned Interacting Multiple Model (SC-IMM) to mitigate this issue. The proposed method converts expert trajectories into path-speed states and performs IMM updates conditioned on scene cues to generate path-speed teacher labels and mode posterior probabilities. The generated signals are added to the original trajectory loss as auxiliary supervision during training, while the inference structure and waypoint controller remain unchanged. In closed-loop evaluation on 100 short routes in CARLA Town12, the proposed method improved the driving score by 28.0% and reduced Collision/km by 62.3% compared with the baseline, while also improving jerk and trajectory-variation metrics. These results demonstrate that offline teacher signals embedding scene-conditioned motion-model cues can guide trajectory-output driving models toward more stable closed-loop behavior.

</details>


### [20] [MarineCraft: Enabling Rapid Prototyping of Underwater Robots via Modular Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21396)
*Yuta Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MarineCraft 是一种模块化水下机器人工具包，通过自包含防水推进模块实现快速结构重构，免除集中布线和重复密封，可在数分钟内组装测试多种几何形态，并在 2.5 米深度可靠无泄漏运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人开发常受防水和布线复杂性阻碍，显著拖慢快速原型迭代过程，因此需要降低开发门槛并加速设计周期。

Method: 提出 MarineCraft 模块化工具包，其核心是集成功率、无线通信和驱动的自包含防水推进模块；通过结构重配置组装不同机器人几何，无需集中布线和反复密封。

Result: 实验结果表明，该可重构架构支持快速迭代设计周期，同时在上至 2.5 米深度保持可靠运行和无泄漏性能。

Conclusion: 该工具包通过将模块化转化为快速物理原型的手段，有效降低了水下机器人开发的门槛。

Abstract: Underwater robot development is often hindered by the complexities of waterproofing and wiring, which significantly delay the rapid prototyping process. This paper presents MarineCraft, a modular toolkit designed to accelerate the development cycle through structural reconfiguration. The system features self-contained, waterproof propulsion modules that integrate power, wireless communication, and actuation. By eliminating centralized wiring and the need for repeated sealing, MarineCraft allows diverse robot geometries to be assembled and tested in minutes rather than days. Experimental results demonstrate that this reconfigurable architecture enables fast, iterative design cycles while maintaining reliable operation and leak-free performance at depths of up to 2.5 meters. Our toolkit effectively lowers the barrier to underwater robotics by transforming modularity into a vehicle for rapid physical prototyping.

</details>


### [21] [AVT-Fabric: Active Visuo-Tactile Perception via Adaptive Evidence Selection for Efficient Robotic Fabric Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21377)
*Chang Gao,Zhuo Chen,Suhang Xia,Jihong Zhu,Jiankang Deng,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AVT-Fabric，一种以RGB优先、按比较难度自适应调用触觉证据的机器人织物比较框架，在7B MLLM上实现98.0%准确率，并提升效率与真实系统部署表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人织物比较需要主动融合视觉外观与触觉线索，现有方法可能依赖固定或被动推理，难以兼顾准确率与效率。

Method: AVT-Fabric采用RGB优先框架，通过双尺度门控评估答案token置信度和原始logit分离度，决定是否需要额外带力标签的GelSight触觉观测；用紧凑文本记忆保存执行历史，并通过多数投票整合所选预测。

Result: 在400个留出比较上，7B MLLM达到98.0%准确率，比90B MLLM-Fabric基线的94.0%高4.0个百分点，平均仅使用5个阶段中的1.60个；比匹配被动推理提升9.25个百分点，模型侧延迟降低61.8%，并优于仅RGB方法。四个额外MLLM骨干支持其泛化性、准确性和效率。真实机器人系统上达到78.1%成对排序准确率，在8个应用场景中7个正确选择织物。

Conclusion: AVT-Fabric表明，自适应证据选择能够同时提升机器人视觉-触觉推理的准确率与效率。

Abstract: Robotic fabric comparison needs to actively combine visual appearance and tactile cues. Here, we present AVT-Fabric, an RGB-first framework that allocates tactile evidence according to the difficulty of each comparison. A dual-scale gate evaluates answer-token confidence and raw logit separation to determine whether another force-tagged GelSight observation is needed. Compact textual memory preserves the executed history, and majority voting consolidates the selected predictions. On 400 held-out comparisons, AVT-Fabric achieves 98.0% accuracy with a compact 7B Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM), surpassing the 94.0% reported by the 90B MLLM-Fabric baseline by 4.0 percentage points while processing only 1.60 of five available stages on average. It improves on matched passive inference by 9.25 percentage points and reduces model-side latency by 61.8%, while also improving on RGB-only accuracy. Four additional MLLM backbones support the generalizability, accuracy, and efficiency of the framework. This framework is also deployed on a real robotic system, achieving 78.1% pairwise ranking accuracy and correct fabric selection in seven of eight application scenarios. AVT-Fabric demonstrates that adaptive evidence selection can improve both the accuracy and efficiency of robotic visuo-tactile reasoning.

</details>


### [22] [ProTracer: Proprioception-Guided Failure Diagnosis in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21369)
*Chang Dong,Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,King Hang Wong,Lingqiao Liu,Francois Fraysse,Feras Dayoub,Minh Hoai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProTracer，一个无需训练、结合 VLM 与本体感觉信号的机器人操作失败分析框架，涵盖二分类失败检测、失败分类、解释生成及失败起点定位，并引入 FailTime 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作失败分析需要细粒度时序定位；现有方法往往难以同时利用本体感觉的时序精度与 VLM 的多模态推理能力，且缺少同步视觉-本体感觉观测的评估基准。

Method: ProTracer 利用本体感觉动力学识别动作边界，将机器人状态转化为结构化自然语言描述，并与视觉观察一起交由 VLM 联合分析；无需额外训练。作者还构建 FailTime 基准，包含同步视觉与本体感觉观测，用于评估传统失败诊断及失败起点定位。

Result: 实验表明，ProTracer 在传统失败诊断任务和新增的失败起点定位任务上均取得强性能，突显本体感觉推理对细粒度时序失败分析的重要性。

Conclusion: 无需训练的 VLM+本体感觉框架可有效完成机器人操作失败分析，并验证了本体感觉信号在细粒度时序失败定位中的关键价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive framework for robot manipulation failure analysis that includes binary failure detection, failure categorization, explanation generation, and the additional capability of failure onset localization, which aims to identify the earliest moment at which a robot execution deviates from a valid task-completion trajectory and is ultimately followed by task failure. To address these tasks, we propose ProTracer, a training-free framework that leverages existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) together with proprioceptive signals for failure analysis. Our method uses proprioceptive dynamics to identify temporally informative action boundaries and converts richer robot-state signals into structured natural-language descriptions that can be jointly analyzed together with visual observations by the VLM. This design combines the temporal precision of proprioceptive signals with the multimodal reasoning capabilities of modern VLMs without requiring additional model training. We further introduce FailTime, a benchmark with synchronized visual and proprioceptive observations for evaluating conventional failure diagnosis tasks as well as failure onset localization. Experiments demonstrate that ProTracer achieves strong performance across both conventional failure diagnosis tasks and the newly introduced failure onset localization task, highlighting the importance of proprioceptive reasoning for fine-grained temporal failure analysis.

</details>


### [23] [MicroHookACT: Monocular Microscopic Vision Guided Visuomotor Policy for Flexible Microelectrode Hooking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21365)
*Yitong Chen,Fangbo Qin,Yang Wang,Ruihua Hu,Kui Zhang,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MicroHookACT——一种基于模仿学习的视觉运动策略，在单目显微视觉下实现自动化3D针环钩取，整体成功率最高达96.7%，平均执行时间11.5秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针环自动钩取是柔性微电极（FME）植入的关键步骤，需在单目显微视觉下实现微米级精准控制与接触操作。

Method: 1）单向钩取策略，利用离焦线索与光轴引导，实现免触诊精确对齐与富接触穿线；2）基于冻结ViT主干的动作监督目标注意力模块，从人类演示中直接学习聚焦微针尖与微环，无需人工视觉标注；3）以注意力为中心的全局粗特征与局部细特征，根据预测动作进度动态加权，使单一ACT策略适应操作中变化的离焦模糊与视觉需求。在60个人类演示上训练，并在5种不同难度设置下评估。

Result: MicroHookACT框架取得最高整体成功率96.7%，平均执行时间11.5秒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，视觉运动策略学习具备在变化操作条件下实现微米级控制的潜力。

Abstract: Automated needle-loop hooking is a critical step in flexible microelectrode (FME) implantation. This paper presents MicroHookACT, an imitation learning-based visuomotor policy for automated 3D hooking under monocular microscopic vision. First, a unidirectional hooking strategy exploits defocus cues and optical-axis guidance to enable palpation-free precise alignment and contact-rich threading. Second, an action-supervised object attention module built on a frozen ViT backbone learns to focus on the micro-needle tip and micro-loop directly from human demonstrations, without requiring manual visual annotations for training. Third, attention-centered global coarse and local fine features are dynamically weighted according to predicted action progress, enabling a single ACT policy to adapt to changing defocus blur and visual requirements throughout the operation. In the experiments, visuomotor policies were trained on 60 human demonstrations and evaluated under five setups with varying difficulties. Our MicroHookACT framework achieved the highest overall success rate of 96.7\% with an average execution time of 11.5 s. These results demonstrate the potential of visuomotor policy learning for micron-level control under varying operating conditions.

</details>


### [24] [FAN: Foresight Action Normalization for Continual Adaptation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21358)
*Yijun Hong,Jiarun Zhu,Xiaoquan Sun,Le Xu,Qijun He,Xin Jin,Mingqi Yuan,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文系统研究持续VLA适配中被忽视的动作归一化问题，评估五种归一化策略在四个真实任务流中的表现，提出一致性、覆盖、因果（3C）原则与FAN方法，实现最高性能和稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽在多样化机器人操作任务中成功，但长期真实部署需持续获取新技能并保留旧能力；现有持续VLA适配方法如经验回放和强化微调忽略了动作归一化这一决定策略感知与执行物理动作坐标系统的基础机制。

Method: 在单臂和双臂操作的四类真实任务流中系统评估五种归一化策略，揭示现有协议因任务间坐标漂移、运动覆盖有限或训练-测试坐标不匹配而导致严重失败模式；据此提出3C设计原则，并引入Foresight Action Normalization（FAN），在持续学习前从小型任务无关校准集一次性估计归一化统计量，并在整个适配过程中冻结。

Result: 在所有评估任务流中，FAN取得最高性能，并表现出一致的稳健性。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建稳定动作表示、实现有效终身VLA适配提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pre-trained on large-scale, closed datasets have demonstrated remarkable success across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, their long-term real-world deployment necessitates continuously acquiring new skills while retaining previously learned capabilities. While pioneering works have explored continual VLA adaptation using techniques such as experience replay and reinforcement fine-tuning, they overlook a foundational mechanism: action normalization, which determines the underlying coordinate system in which policies perceive and execute physical actions. To bridge this gap, we systematically evaluate five normalization strategies across four real-world task streams covering single-arm and bimanual manipulation. Our analysis reveals that existing protocols induce severe failure modes due to inter-task coordinate drift, limited motion coverage, or train-test coordinate mismatches. Motivated by these insights, we formulate three core design principles: consistency, coverage, and causality (3C), and introduce foresight action normalization (FAN). FAN estimates normalization statistics once from a small, task-independent calibration set prior to continual learning and freezes them throughout adaptation. Across all evaluated streams, FAN achieves the highest performance and demonstrates consistent robustness, providing insightful guidance for building stable action representations in achieving effective lifelong VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [25] [The EventCV Library for Event-Based Robotic Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21330)
*Adam D. Hines,Michael Milford,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EventCV 是一个开源、可扩展的 Rust 库，提供 OpenCV 风格 Python 绑定，旨在降低事件相机在机器人流程中的使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备微秒级异步响应、高动态范围和低功耗等优势，适合快速运动或困难光照下的机器人应用；但实际集成仍很困难：缺少即插即用驱动、事件流文件格式不兼容、多数项目依赖定制研究级代码。

Method: 提出 EventCV，包含去噪滤波、几何变换、数据增强、角点检测、无监督特征学习、对比度最大化运动估计、视频到事件模拟器以及 ONNX 推理，并集成 Neuromorphic Drivers 以支持事件相机流的实时处理。

Result: 现有工具包未覆盖如此广泛的操作；EventCV 在表示构建和文件解码上比当前可用库快 1.1x 到 3.7x；在 Jetson Orin AGX 上部署，并展示了目标检测、端侧模型推理和定位三个机器人案例。

Conclusion: EventCV 为事件相机在机器人栈中的落地提供了统一、高效且易用的开源工具链，可降低入门与集成成本。

Abstract: Event cameras detect per-pixel brightness changes asynchronously on microsecond timescales, with high dynamic range and low power draw. These are desirable properties for robots that move fast or work in difficult lighting conditions. However, integrating an event camera into a real-world robotic pipeline still requires substantial effort: plug-and-play drivers do not exist, event streams are recorded in a variety of incompatible file formats, and most projects rely on custom research-grade code. Here, we present EventCV, an open-source and extensible Rust library with OpenCV-style Python bindings that lowers the entry barrier to working with event cameras. EventCV provides a wide range of features: denoising filters and geometric transforms, augmentations, corner detection and unsupervised feature learning, contrast-maximization motion estimation, a video-to-events simulator, and Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) inference for deployment in robotic stacks. EventCV integrates the Neuromorphic Drivers package, allowing an event camera stream to be processed directly in real time. No existing toolkit covers this range of operations in one package, and EventCV builds representations and decodes files 1.1x to 3.7x faster than the currently available libraries. We deploy EventCV on a Jetson Orin AGX and present three robotics case studies spanning object detection, on-device model inference, and localization. Project webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [26] [SeeQ: Training Generalist Value Functions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22085)
*Saksham Singh,Zheyuan Hu,Max Sobol Mark,Jeffrey Yu,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SeeQ（Subtask-elicited Q-functions），学习当前激活子任务的Q函数，以缩短价值预测时域并缓解长时程机器人任务中的稀疏奖励与信用分配难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂、长时程、多阶段或需反复尝试的任务上仍很脆弱。Q函数可通过对候选动作排序或指导策略改进来提升性能，但基于稀疏任务级奖励学习Q函数面临信用分配时域长、Bellman备份困难、数据覆盖要求广等问题。

Method: SeeQ不学习整体任务级Q值，而是学习当前活跃子任务的Q值，从而缩短预测时域并可用TD目标有效训练。训练时利用离线机器人数据中的子任务级标注进行分解，并支持从广泛且可能次优的数据中学习。为在测试时避免人工标注或模块化子任务预测系统，其Q函数架构基于视觉-语言骨干，先自回归地用自然语言预测当前子任务，再估计该子任务价值。模型在多样开源机器人操作数据上预训练，并在下游任务上微调。

Result: 在两个双臂机器人平台的四个真实世界操作任务上，SeeQ价值函数显著提升了best-of-N策略引导性能。

Conclusion: 子任务级Q学习结合自然语言子任务自回归预测，可有效利用离线机器人数据并改善长时程操作策略，同时无需测试时人工子任务标注或独立子任务预测模块。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle on complex, long-horizon tasks that comprise multiple stages or require repeated attempts and deliberation on the same underlying stage before success. Q-value functions can improve these policies by ranking candidate actions or guiding policy improvement, but learning from sparse task-level rewards entails long credit-assignment horizons, difficult Bellman backups, and broad data-coverage requirements. We introduce SeeQ (Subtask-elicited Q-functions), which instead learns Q-values for the currently active subtask. This shortens the value-prediction horizon and enables effective learning with temporal-difference (TD) objectives. During training, subtask-level annotations present in offline robot data provide the decomposition and enable learning from broad, potentially suboptimal robot datasets. To eliminate the need for human annotations or modular subtask prediction systems at test time, our Q-function architecture is trained to autoregressively predict the active subtask in natural language before estimating its value. We instantiate SeeQ using a base vision-language backbone, pretrain it on diverse open-source robot manipulation data, and finetune it on downstream tasks. Across four real-world manipulation tasks on two bimanual robot platforms, the SeeQ value function substantially improves best-of-N policy steering.

</details>


### [27] [LIMBO: Learning and Internalizing Model-Free Barrier Objectives for Agile and Safe Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22075)
*Jake Gonzales,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Aaron D. Ames,Lillian J. Ratliff,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIMBO提出一种合成状态-动作控制屏障函数（Q-CBF）并将其安全结构蒸馏到任务策略中的框架，用于高维全身安全控制；在29自由度人形机器人上实现躲避球与低障碍下运动，并无需部署时在线安全滤波器即可迁移到硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身安全控制需在高维非线性动力学下同时协调避碰与平衡，安全证书难以设计且难以跨行为复用；现有Q-CBF合成难以扩展到完整控制维度。

Method: LIMBO从黑盒转移和基于状态的失败规范中学习安全证书，学习对象是冻结基础控制器周围的残差动作，从而在完整控制维度上使Q-CBF合成可行，并将证书置于任务策略的控制空间。合成阶段，学习到的安全值驱动风险引导采样，靠近估计的可恢复性边界；任务学习阶段，它作为教师提供动作级安全反馈，得到鲁棒任务策略，减少部署时对在线安全滤波器的需求。

Result: 在29自由度人形机器人上验证了躲避球避让和低障碍下运动。除将学习Q-CBF扩展到全身控制外，风险引导边界采样为探索可恢复性边缘提供了有理论依据的方法；在同一安全规范下，仅改变采样集中度即可产生从蹲伏到新颖后仰下腰式limbo动作的不同策略。两种设置中，学习策略均无需在线安全滤波即可迁移到硬件。

Conclusion: 学习安全合成可扩展到敏捷全身控制，并表明通过风险引导采样可有效探索可恢复性边界、产生多样且可部署的安全策略。

Abstract: Safe whole-body control requires coordinating collision avoidance and balance under high-dimensional, nonlinear dynamics--making safety certificates difficult to design and reuse across behaviors. We present LIMBO, a framework for synthesizing a state-action control barrier function and distilling its safety structure into a task policy. LIMBO learns the safety certificate from black-box transitions and a state-based failure specification over residual actions around a frozen base controller, making Q-CBF synthesis tractable in the full control dimension while placing the certificate in the task policy's control space. During synthesis, the learned safety value drives risk-guided sampling near the estimated boundary of recoverability; during task learning, it serves as a teacher that provides action-level safety feedback, yielding a robust task policy and alleviating the need for an online safety filter at deployment. We demonstrate LIMBO on a 29-degree-of-freedom humanoid performing dodgeball avoidance and locomotion beneath low obstacles. Beyond scaling learned Q-CBFs to whole-body control, we show that risk-guided boundary sampling provides a theoretically grounded way to explore the edge of recoverability. Under the same safety specification, ceteris paribus, varying the sampling concentration produces strategies ranging from crouching to a novel backward-leaning limbo maneuver. In both settings, the learned policies transfer to hardware without online safety filtering, showing that learned safety synthesis scales to agile whole-body control.

</details>


### [28] [Duty Factor Predicts Robust Constrained Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Gait Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22073)
*James Zhu,David Ologan,George Ortiz,Thomas Chun Fai Lee,Selvin Garcia Gonzalez,Ardalan Tajbakhsh,Pinhas Ben-Tzvi,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究表明，占空比是比名义步态类型更有效的四足机器人运动鲁棒性预测与调节参数，并在多种控制架构和实体实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人需要在扰动和受限地形中保持鲁棒运动。步态类型（如行走、小跑）常被用来描述运动，但同一歩态内占空比、速度、站距等参数仍可变，因此步态类型并不能唯一决定运动特性，需要研究这些步态参数与鲁棒性的关系。

Method: 作者采用三种四足运动控制方法开展研究：1）全身轨迹优化结合LQR反馈；2）学习型运动控制器；3）质心模型预测控制（centroidal MPC），并在物理四足机器人上进行狭窄地形实验验证。

Result: 结果显示：占空比比名义步态类型更能预测局部误差收敛性；在学习型控制器中，占空比可作为低维参数用于调节狭窄地形环境下的运动鲁棒性；这些趋势在质心MPC框架下仍然存在，并通过实机狭窄地形实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 占空比为跨步态类型和控制架构理解与选择鲁棒四足运动提供了一个简单而有效的基础。

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots are increasingly deployed in environments where locomotion must remain robust to disturbances and constrained terrain. Gait type, such as walking or trotting, is commonly used to characterize quadrupedal locomotion. However, gait type does not uniquely define locomotion, as parameters such as duty factor, speed, and stance width can vary within a single gait type. In this work, we investigate the relationship between these gait parameters using three distinct quadrupedal locomotion control approaches. First, using whole body trajectory optimization with LQR feedback, we show that duty factor is a stronger predictor of local error convergence than nominal gait type. Second, we investigate duty factor selection with a learned locomotion controller, suggesting how duty factor may serve as a low-dimensional parameter for adapting locomotion robustness in narrow-terrain environments. Finally, we show that these trends persist under a centroidal model predictive control framework and validate them through narrow-terrain experiments on a physical quadruped. These results show that duty factor provides a simple and effective basis for understanding and selecting robust quadrupedal locomotion across gait types and control architectures.

</details>


### [29] [Gripper-Aware Automatic Dense Packing of Irregular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22062)
*Tianhao Qin,Connor McCann,Berk Calli,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向真实机械臂的闭环不规则物体密集装箱流程，集成感知、抓取器感知放置优化与力引导执行，并在Franka Emika Panda上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库自动密集装箱需求广泛，但现有不规则物体装箱研究多局限于理想接触的仿真，将物体视为孤立刚体，抓取器仅作事后可行性检查，且未处理执行中的感知与接触漂移。

Method: 优化器将物体与抓取器建模为分层球树组成的单一复合体，在GPU上以CMA-ES搜索五自由度放置，竖直坐标依据当前高度图解析确定；执行阶段采用力监测竖直下降并在首次接触停止；释放后通过压实推消除抓取器感知规划残留的横向间隙；每次放置后重新感知容器以避免漂移累积。

Result: 在Franka Emika Panda机器人上对3D打印的平板、曲面和凹形物体及YCB物体子集进行验证；消融实验分离抓取器感知优化、压实推和网格几何对端到端成功率、达到密度和计算成本的贡献；并与高度图最小化方法这一先前不规则物体装箱工作代表基线进行基准比较。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将抓取器纳入规划、力引导执行与重复感知结合，可面向真实操作缓解仿真到现实差距和漂移累积问题，推进机械臂不规则物体密集装箱。

Abstract: Automatic dense packing is widely desired in warehouse operations but remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Existing work on irregular-object packing largely targets simulation with idealized contact, treating the object as an isolated rigid body. The gripper often enters as a discrete, post-hoc feasibility check, if considered at all, and the perception and contact drift accumulated during execution are not addressed. We present a closed-loop pipeline that integrates perception, gripper-aware placement optimization, and force-guided execution on a real manipulator. The optimizer represents the object together with the gripper as a single composite body of hierarchical sphere trees. It searches over five degrees of freedom on a GPU within a CMA-ES framework, with the vertical coordinate grounded analytically against the current heightmap. During execution, a force-monitored vertical descent stops on first contact. A post-release consolidation push then closes the residual lateral clearance that gripper-aware planning leaves behind. The container is re-perceived between placements so that drift does not accumulate. We validate the system on a Franka Emika Panda robot packing a 3D-printed set of flat, curved, and concave objects, and a YCB object subset. An ablation study isolates the contribution of gripper-aware optimization, the consolidation push, and mesh-derived geometry to end-to-end success, achieved density, and computational cost. We further benchmark against the heightmap-minimization method as a baseline representative of prior irregular-object packing work.

</details>


### [30] [SkelWAM: A Skeleton-Guided World-Action Model for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21983)
*Pengjun Niu,Yujia Xie,Rui Peng,Hang Zhao,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SkelWAM，一种骨架引导的世界-动作模型，通过共享25维几何状态实现单源跨本体操作，在LIBERO-Cross10上显著超过基线，并成功迁移到连续体机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机器人本体复用操作经验对扩展机器人学习和减少重复任务数据采集很重要；但本体变化会改变视觉外观、动作维度与语义，以及实现同一工具位姿所需的全身构型。

Method: SkelWAM以显式几何表示耦合感知与控制：将手臂中心线几何、工具中心点(TCP)位姿和平行夹爪命令统一为共享25维状态，用于规范化的第三人称与腕部观测及未来全身动作目标。模型以预测性视觉监督训练，采用视频-动作混合Transformer预测规范化骨架动作块，再由本体特定的约束解码器转换为关节或连续体机器人控制；无需一对一关节对应，也不使用目标任务演示或目标策略更新。同时提出LIBERO-Cross10单源跨本体迁移基准，涵盖10个任务、10个目标本体和4个形态组。

Result: 在LIBERO-Cross10的1000个回合中，由Franka训练的SkelWAM成功率达43.3%，比评估中最佳基线高36.2个百分点；并将JAKA mini2训练的策略部署到Feagine A03连续体机器人，完成三项桌面操作任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明，SkelWAM能实现无需目标演示与策略更新的跨本体操作迁移，并具备真实世界连续体机器人操作的潜力。

Abstract: Reusing manipulation experience across robot embodiments is important for scaling robot learning and reducing repeated task-specific data collection. However, changes in embodiment alter visual appearance, action dimensionality and semantics, and the whole-body configurations that can realize the same tool pose. We present SkelWAM, a skeleton-guided world-action model that couples perception and control through one explicit geometric representation for single-source cross-embodiment manipulation. Arm centerline geometry, tool-center-point (TCP) pose, and parallel-jaw commands form a shared 25-D state. The same definition underlies canonical third-person and wrist observations and future whole-body action targets. Trained with predictive visual supervision, a video-action mixture of transformers predicts canonical skeleton action chunks, which embodiment-specific constrained decoders convert into joint or continuum-robot controls. This formulation requires no one-to-one joint correspondence and uses no target-task demonstrations or target policy updates. We introduce LIBERO-Cross10, a source-only cross-embodiment transfer benchmark covering ten tasks and ten target embodiments across four morphological groups. On this benchmark, Franka-trained SkelWAM achieves 43.3% success over 1,000 episodes, exceeding the best-performing evaluated baseline by 36.2 percentage points. We further deploy a JAKA mini2-trained policy on the Feagine A03 continuum robot for three tabletop manipulation tasks, illustrating the approach's potential for real-world cross-embodiment manipulation. Project page: this http URL

</details>


### [31] [CARF: Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21982)
*Shuqi Zhao,Bang Du,Cheng-En Wu,Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CARF框架，通过对比式吸引-排斥机制从机器人不完美数据中学习：吸引策略模仿渐进片段，排斥策略避开失败关键片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只利用失败轨迹中仍有任务进展的片段，而忽视直接导致失败的失败关键行为。作者认为二者提供非对称监督：渐进片段应被模仿，失败关键片段应被明确避免。

Method: CARF引入基于进展的重要性评分器，仅用成功专家演示及其扰动结果训练，估计每一步对任务完成的贡献并识别失败轨迹中有信息区域。该评分指导统一的流匹配目标，使策略被吸引向渐进行为、被排斥离开失败关键行为，同时排除模糊片段。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，CARF在多种失败场景下持续优于竞争基线；消融实验进一步验证了所提评分机制和吸引-排斥机制的有效性。

Conclusion: CARF能更全面地利用不完美机器人数据，并避免来自模糊失败片段的不可靠监督。

Abstract: Robot demonstration collection often produces imperfect or failed trajectories in addition to successful demonstrations. Existing methods typically exploit failed trajectories by identifying segments that still make progress toward task completion, but largely overlook \textit{failure-critical behaviors} that directly lead to task failure. Here we argue that these two types of segments provide fundamentally asymmetric supervision: progressive segments should be imitated, whereas failure-critical segments should be explicitly avoided. Based on this observation, we propose CARF, a Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-guided framework for learning from imperfect robot data. CARF introduces a progress-based importance scorer, trained solely on successful expert demonstrations and its perturbation results, to estimate step-wise contributions toward task completion and identify informative regions in failed trajectories. These scores guide a unified flow-matching objective that attracts the policy toward progressive behaviors and repels it from failure-critical ones, while excluding ambiguous segments. This enables more comprehensive utilization of imperfect data and avoids unreliable supervision from ambiguous failure segments. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines across diverse failure scenarios, with ablations further validating the effectiveness of the proposed scoring and attraction-repulsion mechanisms. Our website is this https URL .

</details>


### [32] [GALA: Geometry-Aware Latent Action Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Model Pretraining across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21948)
*Yichen Liu,Puzhen Yuan,Xiang Zhu,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GALA通过几何感知潜在动作建模，将图像潜在动作与3D末端执行器几何运动结合，并引入UEMR统一表示，以提升多具身VLA预训练中细粒度跨具身动作泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多具身VLA学习受限于不同末端执行器动作空间异构；现有基于图像的LAM虽能学习具身无关动作表示，但难以捕捉细粒度末端执行器关节运动，尤其人手和灵巧手的手指级几何变化。直接引入点云又会得到细粒度但共享语义有限的动作表示，妨碍跨具身预训练。

Method: 提出GALA：几何感知潜在动作建模框架，在图像潜在动作基础上增强3D末端执行器几何运动；设计统一末端执行器运动表示UEMR，在保留细粒度运动信息的同时提升跨具身泛化性；融合捕捉场景级动态的视觉潜在动作与捕捉共享细粒度末端执行器关节运动的几何潜在动作，为多具身数据（包括无动作自我中心人类视频）上的VLA预训练提供有效监督。

Result: 在细粒度运动探测、跨具身检索和下游VLA评估中验证了GALA的有效性；取得68.3%的RoboCasa-GR1成功率和75.5%的真实世界成功率。代码、附录和演示已公开。

Conclusion: GALA能够有效建模跨具身的可泛化细粒度运动，缓解末端执行器异构问题，并提升多具身VLA预训练及下游任务性能。

Abstract: Learning large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models from multi-embodiment datasets remains challenging due to heterogeneous action spaces across end effectors. Although latent action models (LAMs) can learn embodiment-agnostic action representations from diverse video data, existing image-based LAMs often fail to capture fine-grained end-effector articulation, particularly finger-level geometric changes in human and dexterous robot hands. To address this limitation, we propose GALA, a Geometry-Aware Latent-Action modeling framework that augments image-based latent actions with 3D end-effector geometric motion. However, naively incorporating point clouds yields fine-grained action representations with limited shared semantics, hindering cross-embodiment pretraining. To address this issue, we introduce the Unified End-effector Motion Representation (UEMR), which preserves fine-grained motion information while improving the cross-embodiment generalizability of latent actions. Building upon UEMR, GALA combines visual latent actions that capture scene-level dynamics with geometric latent actions that capture shared fine-grained end-effector articulation, providing effective supervision for VLA pretraining from multi-embodiment data, including action-free ego-centric human videos. Experiments on fine-grained motion probing, cross-embodiment retrieval, and downstream VLA evaluation demonstrate GALA's effectiveness in modeling generalizable fine-grained motions across embodiments, achieving 68.3% RoboCasa-GR1 success rate and 75.5% real-world success rate. Code, appendix, and demos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [33] [When Should a Failing Robot Ask? Initiating Corrective Human-Robot Dialogue from Audited Sensor Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21942)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文构建机器人故障纠正对话仿真基准，注入已知故障原因并测量各传感器可诊断性；评估六个开源视觉语言模型在三动作决策（自行行动、查询自身传感器、询问人类）中的表现，发现其行为受提示表面影响而非证据和成本驱动，但一次人类询问可将准确率提升至回答者可靠性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务失败后需决定：基于自身诊断行动、查询其他机载传感器，还是打断人类求助。该决策需知道传感器证据对故障原因的揭示程度以及机器人自身诊断的可靠性。

Method: 构建仿真基准，故障真实原因通过注入已知，显式检查数据泄漏，测量相机图像与力数据等传感器对各类故障的可诊断性；测试六个开源视觉语言模型。将问题建模为三动作决策问题，其最优策略由实测准确率决定；并改变回答选项顺序、提示变体、示例、问题成本、将力数据作为文本输入等条件。

Result: 部分故障仅力数据可诊断（力数据0.99，图像方法不高于0.55）。模型行为跟随提示表面：拒绝选项从最后移到最前，使六个模型-家族对中的三对拒绝率从78-100%降至0-6%。仅凭帧的准确率在所有提示变体下都不高于多数类基线，陈述置信度不携带正确性信息。将力数据以十行文本输入后，六个模型中四个首次出现高于基线的诊断。模型不遵循最优策略，询问率对四倍问题成本变化不敏感。一次人类询问仍可将其从基线提升至约回答者自身可靠性（询问时0.70-0.81）。

Conclusion: 是否询问人类应基于实测准确率和明确成本，而非模型置信度；许多失败源于缺失传感器数据，而非模型能力不足。

Abstract: A robot that fails at a task faces the first decision in corrective dialogue: act on its own diagnosis, consult another onboard sensor, or interrupt a person. Choosing well requires knowing how much the robot's sensors reveal about the cause and how reliable the robot's own diagnosis is. We build a simulated benchmark in which every failure's true cause is known, because we injected it, and measure what each sensor reveals, with explicit checks against data leakage. Some failures are diagnosable from camera images; others only from the robot's force data (0.99 from force data, no image method above 0.55). We then test six open vision-language models. Their behavior tracks the surface of the prompt, not the evidence: moving the refusal option from last to first in the answer list collapses refusal rates from 78-100% to 0-6% in three of the six swept model-and-family pairs. Accuracy from frames stays at or below a majority-class baseline under every prompt variant, with or without worked examples, and stated confidence carries no information about correctness. Handing the same models the force data as ten lines of text produces the first above-baseline diagnoses, in four of the six models: much of the failure reflects missing sensor data, not missing ability. We pose the choice as a three-action decision problem, act, consult your own sensors, or ask a human, whose optimal policy follows from measured accuracy. The models do not follow it, and their ask rates ignore a fourfold change in question cost. One question to a human still lifts them from that baseline to roughly the answerer's own reliability (0.70-0.81 when they ask). The decision to ask should be tied to measured accuracy and stated costs, not to the model's confidence.

</details>


### [34] [MAAP: Multi-Agent Active Perception for Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21929)
*Bruno N.Y. Chen,Li Kang,Heng Zhou,Xiufeng Song,Zhemeng Zhang,Jiahua Ma,Yiran Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAAP与RAIL：让多机械臂在执行操作时利用腕部相机充当移动视角，实现协作式主动感知；RAIL按角色条件化动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体操作中每个机械臂都带腕部相机并移动，但现有方法未充分利用这些任务驱动的多视角；主动感知通常仍依赖专用传感智能体。

Method: MAAP将每个机械臂设计为双重用途：既执行操作，又通过腕部相机提供移动视角。RAIL是角色感知模仿学习控制器，在预测各臂动作块的同时预测其当前角色，并以角色为条件生成动作，从而在同一网络中表示角色相关动作。

Result: 四个仿真任务中，平均成功率从固定相机的56.5%提升至单个主动腕部视角62.5%、全部腕部视角70.0%，MAAP+RAIL达79.2%；RAIL增益主要在三臂Microwave任务，相同多腕输入下成功率从47%升至82%。双臂平台上，MAAP+RAIL在20次放置试验中成功14次，而固定视角ACT为0次。

Conclusion: 协作操作本身即可作为一种主动感知机制，多机械臂的移动腕部视角能有效提升操作成功率。

Abstract: Multi-agent manipulation naturally produces multiple task-driven viewpoints: every arm carries a wrist camera and moves through the scene while acting. Yet these observations are typically underutilized, and active perception in manipulation is still often treated as requiring a dedicated sensing agent. We introduce MAAP (Multi-Agent Active Perception), in which every arm is dual-purpose: it executes manipulation actions and, through the wrist camera it carries, simultaneously serves as a moving viewpoint for the team. We pair this with RAIL (Role-Aware Imitation Learning), a controller that predicts each arm's current role alongside its action chunk and conditions action generation on it, representing role-dependent actions within one network. Across four simulated tasks, widening the perception regime lifts average success from 56.5% with a fixed camera to 62.5% with one active wrist view and 70.0% with all of them, while MAAP+RAIL reaches 79.2%. RAIL's additional gain is concentrated on the three-arm Microwave task, where success rises from 47% to 82% on identical multi-wrist inputs. On a dual-arm platform, MAAP+RAIL succeeds in 14 of 20 placement trials compared with 0 of 20 for fixed-view ACT. Collaborative manipulation can thus serve as an active perception mechanism in its own right.

</details>


### [35] [CommitFlow: Semantic Commitment Verification and Local Correction for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21908)
*Zixiang Zhao,Yansong Feng,Yang Yang,Chaoyu Wang,Haoran Xiao,Hui Zhang,Chuang Cheng,Jianjun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CommitFlow 闭环执行框架，通过语义承诺监控与局部校正，在不微调基座策略的情况下缓解 VLA 长时程执行中的语义-物理状态失配，提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略在长时程任务中可能在所需物理效果尚未建立时即进入下一阶段，导致语义承诺（阶段需建立或维持的物理条件）与实际物理状态失配；仅凭动作命令无法确认条件，局部偏差会传播并造成任务失败。

Method: CommitFlow 保持基座策略冻结，结合承诺监控与局部校正：SCM 对比阶段需求与当前状态证据，在条件未满足或违反时阻止依赖动作；BoundaryFlow 基于当前状态与基座动作生成局部校正；RGC 选择满足约束的最小校正强度。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 十个常见任务上，CommitFlow 平均成功率达 75.9%，较基座策略 pi0.5 提升 22.7%；跨策略实验也显示一致增益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，CommitFlow 能提升长时程机器人执行的可靠性，并具备跨策略泛化潜力。

Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced rapidly, long-horizon execution may still progress to the next task stage before the required physical effect has been established. We call this a mismatch between semantic commitments, physical conditions that a stage must establish or maintain, and the actual physical state. Because an action command alone cannot confirm such a condition, local deviations can propagate and cause task failure. To address this problem, we present CommitFlow, a closed-loop execution framework that combines commitment monitoring with local correction while keeping the base policy frozen. CommitFlow integrates three components. A Semantic Commitment Monitor (SCM) compares stage requirements against current state evidence and holds back dependent actions when a required condition is unmet or violated. BoundaryFlow then generates a local correction conditioned on the current state and base action, and Relation and Gain Calibration (RGC) selects the smallest correction strength that satisfies the relevant constraints. Across the ten common RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark tasks, CommitFlow achieves a mean success rate of 75.9 percent, improving on the base policy pi0.5 by 22.7 percent. Cross-policy experiments show consistent gains, pointing toward reliable long-horizon robot execution.

</details>


### [36] [VIRGA: Virtual-Agent-Intermediated Riemannian Geometry for Active-Sensing Air-Ground Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21883)
*Fenghe Guo,Runjie Shen,Chenyang Sun,Junrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VIRGA是一种神经几何协调框架，通过双LiDAR观测构建有界源特定黎曼场，并借助虚拟智能体与互惠弹性反馈实现无人机-无人车-云台闭环协调，在仓库与洞穴场景中安全完成观测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当无人机需持续被无人车搭载的云台LiDAR观测时，空地自主性变得更具挑战；平台需在单一闭环内协调异构运动、有限感知与动态任务主导权，同时避障并保持观测。

Method: VIRGA将双LiDAR观测转化为有界源特定黎曼场，通过虚拟智能体与互惠弹性反馈耦合；平台感知执行映射将共享协调参考转为可行的UAV、UGV与云台指令，并施加主动观测安全约束。

Result: 与三个基线对比：Ray-RMP黎曼响应最快但耦合空地任务中间隙不足；密集解析黎曼场改善几何避障但评估成本高，难以稳定维持视场；ColAG延迟最低但存在安全与观测违规。VIRGA安全完成所有配对仓库条件，并在无需重训练的长距离洞穴压力测试中于不规则狭窄几何中持续协调。消融实验证实在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介与互惠反馈的贡献。

Conclusion: VIRGA通过神经几何协调与虚拟智能体反馈，在复杂受限环境中实现了安全、可观测且无需重训练的空地协同，验证了各模块的有效性。
