Abstract: Air-ground autonomy becomes harder when the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) must remain observable by a gimbal light detection and ranging (LiDAR) mounted on the unmanned ground vehicle (UGV). The platforms must avoid dynamic obstacles while coordinating heterogeneous motion, limited sensing, and changing task initiative within one closed loop. This paper presents VIRGA, a neural geometric coordination framework that turns dual-LiDAR observations into bounded source-specific Riemannian fields and couples them through a virtual agent with reciprocal elastic feedback. Platform-aware execution maps convert the shared coordination reference into feasible UAV, UGV, and gimbal commands while enforcing active-observation safeguards. Evaluation against three complementary baselines reveals distinct limitations. An adapted Ray-RMP controller provides the fastest Riemannian response but produces insufficient clearance in the coupled air-ground task. A dense analytical Riemannian field improves geometric avoidance, yet its high evaluation cost prevents stable field-of-view maintenance. An adapted ColAG controller achieves the lowest latency but still incurs safety and observability violations. VIRGA completes all paired warehouse conditions safely, while a long-range cave stress test without retraining demonstrates sustained coordination in irregular and confined geometry. Ablations confirm contributions from online geometric evaluation, virtual-agent mediation, and reciprocal feedback.

</details>


### [37] [PopNavShift: Stress-Testing Social Navigation under Behavioral Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21838)
*Kaizhen Tan,Diyu Zheng,Tim Guangyu Wu,ChengHe Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PopNavShift 匹配仿真框架，在行人群体分布偏移下压力测试社会导航策略；发现仅改变时间压力即可大幅改变控制器排名，且行人负担比机器人通行时间更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会导航算法通常在固定行人行为分布下评估，但行人对机器人的反应因个体和社会情境而异，忽略群体差异会低估策略脆弱性。

Method: PopNavShift 用 MatrAIx Persona 1M 中 600 条合成人物记录提示 Gemini 3.7 Flash，并将回答确定性映射为有界运动参数，构建群体条件化行人运动画像；在 8 种群体条件、7,488 次匹配机器人运行中比较反应式避让、提前让行与互惠避碰三种策略，并对同一 202 个人物进行仅改变时间压力的匹配干预。

Result: 仅改变时间压力时，基于机器人通行时间的控制器排名反转 8.6%，基于平均行人延误为 22.4%，基于最差十分位延误为 23.9%；跨群体条件，这种敏感性对行人负担高于机器人通行时间，并在空间受限场景中增强，且在第二种行人动力学模型下保持定性一致。

Conclusion: 社会导航策略应在多种行为群体上评估，并同时考虑机器人性能与行人负担。

Abstract: Social-navigation algorithms are often evaluated under a fixed pedestrian-behavior distribution, despite substantial variation in pedestrian responses to robots across individuals and social contexts. We introduce PopNavShift, a matched simulation framework for stress-testing social-navigation strategies under pedestrian population shifts. PopNavShift constructs population-conditioned pedestrian motion profiles by prompting Gemini 3.7 Flash with 600 synthetic persona records from MatrAIx Persona 1M and deterministically mapping the responses into bounded motion parameters. It then compares three representative navigation strategies, reactive avoidance, early yielding, and reciprocal collision avoidance, across eight population conditions and 7,488 matched robot runs. In a matched intervention on the same 202 personas, changing only time pressure reverses 8.6% of controller rankings based on robot travel time, but 22.4% based on mean pedestrian delay and 23.9% based on worst-decile delay. Across population conditions, this sensitivity is greater for pedestrian burden than for robot travel time and increases in spatially constrained settings; the same qualitative pattern persists under a second pedestrian dynamics model. These findings support evaluating navigation strategies across behavioral populations using both robot performance and pedestrian burden.

</details>


### [38] [LunaDrive: A Delay-Compensated High-Voltage GaN FET-Based Motor Driver for Dynamic Robots with Flat BLDC Motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21818)
*Sota Yuzaki,Temma Suzuki,Hiromi Tada,Masanori Konishi,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一款面向高功率扁平BLDC电机的GaN FET驱动器，并研究了超出额定工作范围的高速区延迟补偿；实验实现96 V下30 A连续电流、80 A峰值电流和3110 Hz最高电频率，并用24S LiPo电池完成高速举升实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态机器人需要快速能量输出和高瞬时转速，但许多商用伺服电机按48 V或更低电压设计，限制了最大转速；同时电机驱动器中的半导体存在电压额定值与电流容量之间的权衡，传统Si MOSFET难以兼顾高压与大电流。GaN FET虽有潜力，但可安装在扁平BLDC电机后侧的紧凑驱动器仍有限。

Method: 采用GaN FET开发适用于高功率扁平BLDC电机的电机驱动器，并研究在超过额定工作范围的高速区域中的延迟补偿效果；通过实验测试驱动器的连续电流、峰值电流和最高电频率，并使用24S LiPo电池（100 V）进行高速负载举升实验。

Result: 在96 V工作且附加散热片时实现30 A连续电流；确认峰值电流达80 A，最高电频率为3110 Hz；24S LiPo电池（100 V）驱动的高速负载举升实验表明其适用于动态机器人操作。

Conclusion: 基于GaN FET的紧凑电机驱动器能够实现高压、大电流和高速运行，并可通过高速区延迟补偿提升超出额定范围时的性能，展现出应用于动态机器人驱动的可行性。

Abstract: The performance improvement of high-power flat BLDC motors has accelerated the development of dynamic robots. However, many commercially available servo motors assume operating voltages of 48 V or lower, which limits the maximum rotational speed. Dynamic robots require rapid energy generation, so this voltage constraint restricts motion performance. Therefore, driving motors beyond the rated voltage is desirable to increase the instantaneous maximum speed. On the other hand, semiconductor devices used in motor drivers have a trade-off between voltage rating and current capacity. Conventional drivers using Si MOSFETs have difficulty achieving both high-voltage and high-current operation. Although GaN FETs are promising, compact drivers that can be mounted on the rear side of flat BLDC motors remain limited. In this study, a motor driver for high-power flat BLDC motors using GaN FETs is developed. The effect of delay compensation in the high-speed region beyond the rated operating range is also investigated. In the experiments, under 96 V operation, a continuous current of 30 A was achieved with a heat sink attached. A peak current of 80 A and a maximum electrical frequency of 3110 Hz were confirmed. A high-speed load lifting experiment driven by a LiPo battery 24S (100 V) was also conducted, demonstrating applicability to dynamic robot operation.

</details>


### [39] [A Sim-to-Real Integration Pipeline for Training and Deployment of Chunk-Based VLA Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21817)
*Mathilde Kappel,Clémence Grislain,Mohamed Chetouani,Olivier Sigaud,Louis Annabi,Fa\"ız Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux,Mahdi Khoramshahi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源sim-to-real实验协议：在仿真中生成专家轨迹，于真实Franka FR3上开环回放并采集真实视觉与本体感觉观测，转换为VLA训练格式；同一部署栈复用于闭环策略评估，从而缓解真实数据采集瓶颈并直接测量sim-to-real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通常预测末端执行器位姿空间中的动作块，训练与评估依赖大规模真实世界演示数据；在真实硬件上采集此类数据主要依靠人工遥操作，成本高、耗时长且难以扩展。

Method: 在仿真中生成专家轨迹，并在真实Franka FR3装置上开环回放；记录对应的真实视觉与本体感觉观测，转换为兼容VLA训练的格式。随后复用同一部署栈，以闭环方式在该装置上评估训练后的策略，使数据采集与评估共享相同硬件配置。每次真实记录均与产生它的仿真轨迹配对，以直接测量sim-to-real gap。

Result: 该协议产出配对真实-仿真数据与直接sim-to-real差距测量结果；收集的数据集已发布在Hugging Face，并公开pipeline源代码。

Conclusion: 该开源sim-to-real实验协议可降低VLA真实数据采集瓶颈，统一数据采集与策略评估的硬件配置，并为量化与复用sim-to-real迁移提供基础设施。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a prominent paradigm for mapping multimodal inputs, including semantic instructions, visual observations of the scene, and proprioceptive observations, to robot actions. Most state-of-the-art models predict actions in the end-effector pose space as sequences of action chunks. Training and evaluating these models requires large-scale collections of real-world demonstrations, pairing robot actions with the corresponding visual and proprioceptive observations. Collecting such data on real hardware typically relies on human teleoperation, making the process costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. We present an open-source sim-to-real experimental protocol that addresses this bottleneck: expert trajectories generated in simulation are replayed open-loop on a real Franka FR3 setup, where the corresponding real visual and proprioceptive observations are recorded and converted into a format compatible with VLA training. The same deployment stack is then reused, in closed-loop, to evaluate a trained policy on that setup, so that data collection and evaluation share an identical hardware configuration. Because each real recording is paired with the simulated trajectory that produced it, the protocol also yields a direct measurement of the sim-to-real gap. We release the collected datasets on Hugging Face together with the pipeline source code this https URL .

</details>


### [40] [Contact-Rich Motion Planning via GPU-Parallel Mode Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21803)
*Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CoMET：利用 GPU 并行轨迹优化与贪心接触模式扩展，重新审视接触丰富运动规划中的广泛接触模式评估，并在平面推动和双臂非抓取操作中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富运动规划因组合式接触决策而计算困难，现有方法多通过搜索启发式或优化重构来避免对接触模式序列进行广泛评估。

Method: CoMET 将 GPU 并行轨迹评估与贪心接触模式扩展结合，对接触模式进行显式广泛评估，并使用高吞吐轨迹评估器支持并行优化。

Result: 在平面推动基准上，CoMET 在解质量和规划时间上与基于优化、采样和树搜索的基线方法相当，在几乎所有实例上以更少评估和更短规划时间达到全枚举参考水平；消融表明性能提升主要来自高吞吐轨迹评估器。在双臂非抓取操作中，随模式空间增大，GPU 友好的局部模式扩展比所测试的自适应树搜索具有更高规划成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，广泛的显式接触模式评估为接触丰富运动规划提供了一种简单而有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich motion planning (CRMP) is essential for robotic manipulation and locomotion, yet remains computationally challenging due to combinatorial contact decisions. Existing methods typically avoid broad evaluation of contact-mode sequences through search heuristics or optimization reformulations. We revisit broad evaluation in light of modern GPU hardware and introduce Contact-Mode Expansion with parallel Trajectory optimization (CoMET), which combines GPU-parallel trajectory evaluation with greedy contact-mode expansion. On planar pushing benchmarks, CoMET is competitive with optimization-based, sampling, and tree-search baselines in solution quality and planning time, matching the full-enumeration reference on nearly all instances with fewer evaluations and shorter planning times. Ablations suggest that much of the performance gain comes from the high-throughput trajectory evaluator. In bimanual nonprehensile manipulation, GPU-friendly local mode expansion achieves higher planning success than the tested adaptive tree search as the mode space grows. These results demonstrate that broad explicit mode evaluation provides a simple yet effective alternative for CRMP.

</details>


### [41] [AcousticDiffusion: Semantically Conditioned Audio-Guided Diffusion Policy for Search-and-Rescue Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21792)
*Iana Zhura,Didar Seyidov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Hajira Amjad,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AcousticDiffusion：一种语义条件化、音频引导的扩散策略，用于在视觉退化或遮挡环境中让人形/救援机器人朝人类呼救者导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉接触退化或遮挡的救援场景中，机器人需要具备朝向人类呼叫者导航的能力，而仅依赖视觉或传统声源定位往往不够可靠。

Method: 使用冻结的预训练音频识别器处理 10.24 秒窗口，并通过语音门控与遇险感知优先级将识别输出转化为源级导航角色；将麦克风阵列到达方向（DOA）递归整合为以机器人为中心的贝叶斯鸟瞰图信念场；通过自运动补偿对齐连续观测，在保留方位引起的距离不确定性的同时逐步约束声源位置；以语义信念、近期声学观测、音频特征和机器人状态为条件，用扩散模型生成路点轨迹。

Result: 在基于录制音频的合成导航验证集上，平均端点方位误差为 11.20°，91.78% 的轨迹与呼叫者方位偏差在 30° 以内；干扰源拒绝率为 89.20% 至 98.99%，在 91.07% 的时间窗中优先选择标记为 HELP 的呼叫者而非竞争说话人。在 ZSL-1 四足机器人上在线部署且无需额外重训练时，平均方位误差为 64.9°，优于 A* 的 98.2° 和 RRT 的 90.4°，平均规划计算时间为 6.07 ms；尽管声学定位不完美，平均最终声源距离由使用 ODAS 引导的经典规划器的 3.96 m 降至 2.48 m，提升 37.4%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将不确定的声学观测转化为更接近人类呼叫者的导航行为，展示了音频引导扩散策略在救援机器人人向导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Navigating toward human callers is an important capability for rescue robots operating where visual contact is degraded or occluded. We present AcousticDiffusion, a semantically conditioned, audio-guided diffusion policy for human-directed navigation. A frozen pretrained audio recognizer processes 10.24 s windows, with speech gating and distress-aware prioritization converting recognition outputs into source-level navigation roles. Microphone-array direction-of-arrival measurements are recursively integrated into a robot-centric Bayesian bird's-eye-view belief field. Ego-motion compensation aligns successive observations, progressively constraining source position while preserving bearing-induced range uncertainty. The semantic belief, recent acoustic observations, audio features, and robot state condition a diffusion model that generates waypoint trajectories. On a synthetic-navigation validation set using recorded audio, AcousticDiffusion achieves a mean end-point bearing error of 11.20 degrees, with 91.78% of trajectories aligned within 30 degrees of the caller. Distractor rejection ranges from 89.20% to 98.99%, and the policy favors a HELP-designated caller over a competing speaker in 91.07% of windows. Deployed online on a ZSL-1 quadruped without additional retraining, it achieves a mean bearing error of 64.9 degrees, compared with 98.2 degrees for A* and 90.4 degrees for RRT, with a mean planner compute time of 6.07 ms. Despite imperfect acoustic localization, the reported mean final source distance is reduced from 3.96 m for the classical planners using ODAS-derived (Open embedded Audition System) guidance to 2.48 m, a 37.4% improvement. These results demonstrate the framework's ability to translate uncertain acoustic observations into closer approaches to human callers.

</details>


### [42] [From Pretraining to Proficiency: Real-World Subtask RL for Long-Horizon Manipulation with Minimal Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21788)
*Sichang Su,Benjamin Yang,Zhiyun Deng,Boyuan Liang,Yip Fun Yeung,Zelin Wang,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PARTS 是一种真实世界机器人子任务强化学习微调框架，利用冻结预训练策略提供标称动作，并通过残差修正与子任务成功验证器集中解决长时程任务中的瓶颈，从而在少量真实 rollout 和较少人工介入下显著提升全任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人基础策略常能完成长时程任务的大部分步骤，却在少数关键子任务上反复失败；收集完整任务示范进行 SFT 需要操作员重复已掌握行为，成本高。现有强化学习微调方法在稀疏奖励下难以解决长时程任务，因此需要一种聚焦瓶颈子任务、可自主训练的真实世界 RL 微调方法。

Method: 提出 PARTS（Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks）。训练时冻结预训练策略并让其提供全流程标称动作；由智能体生成的选择器与成功验证器决定何时激活残差修正，并提供局部子任务结果奖励。该奖励使方法在完整任务成功稀少时仍能从成功子任务中学习。训练结合在线 RL 与成功重加权再训练，每次再训练后的残差策略重新部署以收集新经验；人类仅在初始化时识别瓶颈并在需要时进行物理重置。

Result: 在双臂 YAM 和单臂 Franka 任务上，PARTS 将完整任务成功率分别从 32% 提升至 61%、从 50% 提升至 95%，每项任务平均仅需数十分钟真实世界 RL rollout。与现有真实世界 RL 微调方法相比，在相同机器人 rollout 预算下将全任务成功率提高超过 25%，且所需人工介入更少。

Conclusion: PARTS 通过针对瓶颈子任务的残差强化学习、局部成功奖励和成功重加权再训练，有效缓解稀疏奖励下长时程真实机器人策略微调难题，在提升全任务成功率的同时降低示范与人工成本。

Abstract: A pretrained robot foundation policy may execute most of a long-horizon task yet repeatedly fail at a few critical subtasks. Collecting additional full-task demonstrations for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) requires operators to repeat behaviors the policy already performs well. Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning offers a promising path to bridge this gap, but existing approaches struggle to solve long-horizon tasks using only sparse rewards. We present PARTS (Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks), a real-world subtask RL framework that concentrates practice at these bottlenecks while allowing training rollouts to proceed with minimal human intervention. The frozen pretrained policy supplies nominal actions throughout execution, while agent-generated selectors and success verifiers activate residual corrections and provide local outcome rewards. These rewards support learning from successful subtasks even when complete-task successes are scarce. Training combines online RL with success-reweighted retraining, and each retrained residual policy is redeployed to collect further experience. Humans identify bottlenecks during setup and perform physical resets when needed. On bimanual YAM and single-arm Franka tasks, PARTS improves complete-task success from 32% to 61% and from 50% to 95%, respectively, using tens of minutes of real-world RL rollouts per task on average. Compared with existing real-world RL fine-tuning methods, PARTS raises full-task success by more than 25% under the same robot-rollout budget while requiring less human involvement.

</details>


### [43] [Compact but Moving: Intervention-Relevant Geometry in Recurrent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21787)
*Yuming Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 高维循环世界模型中的紧凑反事实干预虽会迅速离开固定入口子空间，但其非线性修正可由沿事实循环雅可比链传输的低秩几何捕捉，并表现为状态依赖的移动局部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的世界模型即使循环状态高维，也可能存在紧凑干预；但干预进入模型后如何传播、是否保持低维或固定结构尚不清楚。论文旨在检验紧凑反事实修正的持久性、可预测性，以及其是否意味着存在固定或动态封闭的低维状态。

Method: 在受控循环世界模型中，基于此前发现的 checkpoint 特定 rank-4 一次性反事实速度干预接口开展研究；用事实循环雅可比链将入口方向传输到后续时间步，构造低秩切线预测修正并重启 rollout；在独立训练的 structured-GRU 与参数匹配、以特权紧凑修正初始化的 LSTM 中复现；进一步定义完整循环载体上的有限时域未来响应算子，并通过 patching、方向面板排序、全幅度 assay 检验未来敏感方向、局部算子排序能力及切向残差/响应重构。

Result: 修正会迅速离开固定 rank-4 入口子空间；但经雅可比链传输得到的低秩图像仍能捕捉大部分非线性修正，使用切线预测修正重启后也保留显著的反事实未来功能。该传输/功能模式在多个 structured-GRU 和参数匹配 LSTM 中重复出现。未来响应算子显示，patching 会将主要未来敏感方向移向匹配的原生反事实组织；局部算子能在 patched 与原生反事实基点上准确排序有限扰动效应。全幅度实验发现事实终点切向残差显著，并在 3 个 checkpoint 中的 2 个支持响应重构。

Conclusion: 紧凑干预结构可以作为嵌入高维循环动力学中的移动、状态依赖局部几何而持续存在；这并不意味模型存在固定或动态封闭的低维状态。

Abstract: Learned world models may have compact interventions even when their recurrent state is high-dimensional, but it is unclear what happens to such a correction after it enters the model. We study this question in a controlled recurrent world model where prior work identified a checkpoint-specific rank-4 interface for one-shot counterfactual velocity interventions. The correction rapidly leaves this fixed entry subspace during autonomous rollout. Nevertheless, a low-rank image obtained by transporting the entry directions through the factual recurrent Jacobian chain continues to capture most of the nonlinear correction. Restarts using the tangent-predicted correction preserve substantial counterfactual future function. This transport/function pattern recurs across independently trained structured-GRU models and a parameter-matched LSTM initialized with a privileged compact correction. We further characterize a finite-horizon future-response operator over the full recurrent carrier. Patching shifts its leading future-sensitive directions toward the matched native-counterfactual organization, and the local operator accurately ranks finite perturbation effects over the registered direction panels at the patched and native-counterfactual basepoints. A separate full-amplitude assay finds substantial factual-endpoint tangent residuals and supports response reconfiguration in two of three checkpoints. Together, these results show that compact intervention structure can persist as a moving, state-dependent local geometry embedded in high-dimensional recurrent dynamics, without implying a fixed or dynamically closed low-dimensional state.

</details>


### [44] [TRACE: Coverage Path Planning for Unknown Environments Using Hierarchical Coverage Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21777)
*Zongyuan Shen,Haodong Liu,Gao Wang,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向未知环境实时覆盖的在线覆盖路径规划算法TRACE，基于层次化覆盖树、增量全局巡回和局部规划器实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知环境中实时覆盖路径规划需应对增量感知导致的剩余未覆盖空间碎片化问题，避免频繁全局重规划并保持覆盖过程的连续性与效率。

Method: TRACE构建层次化覆盖树以全局表示未覆盖空间的动态连通性；当新障碍或已覆盖单元使未覆盖空间分裂时，递归扩展对应树节点以显式表示并组织各区域；基于更新后的树维护增量全局巡回，仅局部细化受影响部分并保留未变区域的访问顺序；局部规划器在全局巡回引导下生成往复覆盖路径，并切换为全局巡回感知规划以高效完成目标区域；理论上分析计算复杂度、完整覆盖性质并推导增量全局巡回细化的近似界。

Result: 通过大量高保真仿真与移动机器人实机实验评估，并与六种现有CPP方法对比，在覆盖时间、路径长度、重叠率和转向次数上均有显著改进。

Conclusion: TRACE能够有效实现未知环境中的实时覆盖，在保持完整覆盖的同时降低重规划负担并提升覆盖效率。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel online coverage path planning (CPP) algorithm, called TRACE, for real-time coverage of unknown environments. TRACE is built upon a hierarchical coverage tree that provides a global representation of the evolving connectivity of the uncovered space. As the environment is incrementally revealed and covered, newly discovered obstacles and covered cells may fragment the remaining uncovered space into disconnected regions. TRACE recursively expands the corresponding tree nodes to explicitly represent these regions and organize them for subsequent coverage planning. Based on the updated tree, an incremental global tour is maintained to guide the coverage process. TRACE locally refines only the affected portions while preserving the visiting order of unchanged regions, thereby reducing the computational burden of global replanning and maintaining a consistent coverage progression. Guided by the global tour, a local planner generates back-and-forth coverage paths and switches to global-tour-aware planning to efficiently complete the target regions. Theoretical analysis establishes the computational complexity and complete coverage property of TRACE, and derives an approximation bound for the incremental global tour refinement. The performance of TRACE is evaluated through extensive high-fidelity simulations and real-robot experiments using a mobile robot. Comparative evaluations against six existing CPP methods demonstrate significant improvements in coverage time, path length, overlap ratio, and number of turns.

</details>


### [45] [Scaling Vision-Language Reward Learning for Robot Manipulation in Parallel Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21767)
*Lobna Joualy,Eric Demeester,Nikolaos Tsiogkas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RAPID 系统，通过 GPU 并行采样、单请求偏好标注与代表性图像采样等组件，大幅加速基于视觉语言模型的偏好奖励学习：端到端训练提速 8.0 倍、API 调用减少 95.5%，最终成功率由 86.3% 提升至 98.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用视觉语言模型（VLM）替代人工标注者进行基于偏好的奖励学习虽可行，但串行 API 请求与单环境数据采集导致训练缓慢且成本高昂。

Method: RAPID（Reward learning with Adaptive Parallel Image Diversity）将 GPU 并行 rollout 与数据感知的策略更新相结合，并引入单请求偏好标注、自动奖励稳定化和代表性图像采样。在 IsaacLab 的五个 Franka Panda 操作任务上进行评估。

Result: 在匹配的两阶段提示下，并行 rollout 与自适应更新使平均运行时间从 9.18 小时降至 3.13 小时；启用全部组件后训练仅需 1.15 小时，每次运行的 API 调用从 19,840 降至 896，最终总成功率从 86.3% 升至 98.7%，实现 8.0 倍端到端加速和 95.5% 的 API 用量削减。使用 Gemma 3 12B 与 GPT-4.1 mini 的离线评估表明，单请求提示能降低两个模型的标注延迟与成本。

Conclusion: RAPID 有效解决了 VLM 偏好奖励学习中训练慢、成本高的问题，可显著提升训练效率与任务成功率，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can replace human annotators in preference-based reward learning, but sequential API requests and single-environment data collection make training slow and costly. We present RAPID (Reward learning with Adaptive Parallel Image Diversity), a system that couples GPU-parallel rollout with data-aware policy updates, single-request preference labeling, automatic reward stabilization, and representative image sampling. We evaluate these components on five Franka Panda manipulation tasks in IsaacLab. Parallel rollout and adaptive updates provide the first substantial reduction in training time: under matched two-stage prompting, mean runtime falls from 9.18 to 3.13 hours. With all RAPID components enabled, training completes in 1.15 hours using 896 rather than 19,840 API calls per run, and aggregate final success rises from 86.3\% to 98.7\%. This represents an 8.0$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 95.5\% reduction in API usage. An offline evaluation with Gemma~3 12B and GPT-4.1 mini demonstrates that single-request prompting reduces labeling latency and cost across both models. Code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [46] [CRISP: Contact-Rich Robotic Simulation Platform with Extensive Geometries and Contact Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21761)
*Somang Lee,Sunkyung Park,Jinhee Yun,Seoki An,Dongjun Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CRISP——面向复杂多接触仿真的高保真物理引擎，支持多样几何表示、基于优化的碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日接触求解，并在机器人操作场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真机器人仿真需要精确的几何与接触建模，以及鲁棒的碰撞检测和接触求解；现有模拟器在几何表示、接触建模或数值求解的精度与鲁棒性上存在不足，难以支持紧公差机器人操作等接触密集任务。

Method: CRISP支持多种几何表示，采用基于优化的精确碰撞检测，并将接触建模与鲁棒的增广拉格朗日接触求解器结合，从而在复杂几何上高效、一致地获取接触信息，并在避免问题性松弛的情况下准确求解多接触约束。

Result: 作者将CRISP与当前最先进平台进行对比以验证其物理保真度，并通过复杂机器人操作场景展示其能力；CRISP已公开提供。

Conclusion: CRISP通过集成多样几何表示、优化碰撞检测和增广拉格朗日接触求解，提升了接触密集与尖锐交互仿真的保真度和鲁棒性，并可作为公开平台用于复杂机器人操作研究。

Abstract: We present CRISP (Contact-RIch Simulation Platform), a high-fidelity physics engine tailored for complex multi-contact simulations such as tight-tolerance robotic manipulation. Achieving high physical fidelity in robotic simulation requires both expressive modeling of geometry and contact interactions, as well as accurate numerical resolution via robust collision detection and contact solvers. However, existing simulators often either rely on limited support for geometric representations and simplified modeling of contact interactions, or employ numerical resolution methods whose accuracy or robustness is inherently constrained. Accordingly, we develop a new simulator that supports diverse geometric representations with accurate optimization-based collision detection, and combines contact modeling with robust augmented Lagrangian-based contact solvers. This integration enables efficient and consistent detection of contact information across complex geometries while accurately resolving multi-contact constraints without problematic relaxations, which is essential for simulating contact-intensive and sharp interactions. We validate the physical fidelity of our simulator against state-of-the-art platforms and further demonstrate its capabilities through complex robotic manipulation scenarios. CRISP is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [47] [PSR: Predictive Sensorimotor Representation Learning for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21753)
*Shengbao Li,Peng Xu,Chao Tang,Hao Wei,Jiaheng Wang,Hong Yin,Jiangtao Chen,Jinxuan Zhu,Zhong Zhou,Mengfan Wang,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预测性感驱表征（PSR）学习框架，并将其集成到VLA模型中形成PSR-VLA，在六个真实世界富接触操作任务上取得91.7%总体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富接触操作需要策略超越视觉观测，对接触力、机器人构型和交互历史进行推理以生成精确动作；现有方法仅被动依赖力反馈，无法主动预测未来接触动力学，限制了高精度动作生成能力。

Method: PSR框架通过预训练多模态Transformer，联合预测未来交互动力学，学习层次化预测表征；该表征随后增强视觉运动策略的动作流，使策略在多个深度上利用接触相关线索。进一步在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中实例化为PSR-VLA。

Result: 在六个真实世界富接触操作任务中，PSR-VLA总体成功率达91.7%，分别比π0.5、ForceVLA-π0.5和ForceVLA2-π0.5高出30.0、22.5和19.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，所提出的PSR能有效实现力感知的富接触操作。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to generate precise actions by reasoning over contact forces, robot configurations, and interaction histories beyond visual observations. Existing methods passively condition on force feedback rather than actively predicting future contact dynamics, limiting their ability to generate high-precision actions. To address this problem, we introduce Predictive Sensorimotor Representation (PSR) learning, a framework that learns a hierarchy of predictive representations from multimodal sensorimotor signals and integrates them into the action stream of a visuomotor policy. Specifically, during a pretraining stage, a multimodal Transformer is trained to learn a hierarchy of predictive representations by jointly forecasting future interaction dynamics. The learned hierarchy subsequently augments the action stream, enabling the resulting policy to exploit contact-relevant cues at multiple depths. We further instantiate PSR within a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model, resulting in PSR-VLA, and evaluate it on six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experimental results show that PSR-VLA achieves 91.7% overall success, improving over $\pi_{0.5}$, ForceVLA-$\pi_{0.5}$, and ForceVLA2-$\pi_{0.5}$ by 30.0, 22.5, and 19.2 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed PSR for force-aware, contact-rich manipulation. Videos of the tasks and stability tests are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [48] [ForceTwin: Physics-informed Digital Twins for Robotic Manipulation from Instrumented Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21751)
*Tim Engelbracht,René Zurbrügg,Mayank Mittal,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum,Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceTwin 通过带力感知夹爪的人类交互数据，辨识铰接物体的物理数字孪生，建模惯性、摩擦、阻尼及状态相关机构力，并用于提升阻抗控制与真实世界门穿越策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作铰接物体不仅需要运动学，还需要惯性、摩擦以及弹簧、闭门器等机构属性；这些物理属性无法从外观直接观测，视觉相同的门可能操纵难度差异很大。现有数字孪生流程主要恢复运动学或依赖视觉语言先验赋予静态物理参数，容易产生物理不合理估计，且状态相关机构动力学未被识别，也无法在标准资产格式中表示。

Method: 使用手持力传感夹爪探测物体，获取同步位姿与交互力，估计铰接结构及参数化动力学，包括惯性、库仑摩擦、粘性阻尼，并用结构化神经残差捕捉状态相关机构力。

Result: ForceTwin 将惯性参数误差较 VLM 先验近乎减半；作为 Spot 与 Franka FR3 阻抗控制的前馈动力学模型，在 9 个物体-本体对中达到 87% 目标完成率，而 VLM 先验为 60%、仅运动学孪生为 57%，在强机构导致基线停滞的物体上增益最大；进一步用辨识出的孪生训练全身门穿越策略并部署到真实世界。

Conclusion: ForceTwin 能从仪器化人类交互中辨识物理信息数字孪生，更准确表示状态相关机构动力学，并显著提升下游控制与真实世界部署性能。

Abstract: Manipulating objects requires understanding not only their motion, but also the physical properties that determine it. For articulated objects, these include inertia, friction, and mechanisms such as springs or door closers, whose effects can vary with configuration and velocity. Such properties are not directly observable from appearance: visually identical doors may require very different effort to manipulate. Existing digital-twin pipelines recover primarily kinematics or assign static physical parameters from visual and language priors, which can yield physically implausible estimates. As a result, state-dependent mechanism dynamics remain unidentified and are not represented in standard asset formats. We present ForceTwin, a system for identifying physics-informed digital twins of articulated objects from instrumented human interaction. A person probes an object using a handheld force-sensing gripper, providing synchronized poses and interaction forces from which we estimate the articulation, parametric dynamics including inertia, Coulomb friction, viscous damping, and a structured neural residual capturing state-dependent mechanism forces. ForceTwin nearly halves the inertial-parameter error of a VLM prior. As a feedforward dynamics model for impedance control on a Spot and a Franka FR3, ForceTwin achieves 87% goal completion across nine object-embodiment pairs, compared with 60% using VLM-prior and 57% using kinematics-only twins, with the largest gains on objects whose strong mechanisms cause both baselines to stall. We further use the identified twins to train whole-body door-traversal policies and deploy them in the real world. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [49] [Understanding Engagement and Intrusiveness in Assistive Human-Robot Interaction Using Individual Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21744)
*Valerio Bo,Lavinia Hriscu,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探讨机器人辅助中接近距离、干预频率与个体特质如何共同影响用户参与度和侵入感；通过32人实验发现总体参与度稳定，但情感反应和感知侵入感随人格特质显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在陌生任务中，用户需理解任务要求并与机器人协调，机器人辅助尤为重要。既有研究对机器人空间距离与用户参与的关系结论不一：更近可能增强互动，也可能令人感到侵入。作者认为侵入感不仅取决于空间距离，还取决于机器人干预频率，并受人格和人口学等个体特质强烈影响。

Method: 招募32名参与者，与两个辅助机器人交互。两个机器人提供相似的任务支持，但采用不同的干预策略。研究比较不同条件下的用户参与度、情感反应和感知侵入感，并考察人格特质如何调节交互动态。

Result: 总体参与度在不同机器人行为条件下保持稳定；但情感反应和感知侵入感随人格特质显著变化。人格特质对交互动态的影响因机器人行为不同而不同。

Conclusion: 有效的人机交互应考虑个体差异，根据用户独特的情感与行为特征定制机器人行为，以维持用户参与度并尊重用户的个体差异。

Abstract: Robot assistance is particularly crucial in unfamiliar tasks, where users must understand task requirements while coordinating with the robot. Previous research offers mixed evidence on the role of robot proxemics in user engagement: some studies suggest closer proximity enhances interaction, while others report it can feel intrusive. In this work, we argue that perceptions of intrusiveness depend not only on proxemics but also on the frequency of robot interventions, and are strongly influenced by individual traits such as personality and demographics. We conducted an experiment with 32 participants who interacted with two assistive robots that provided similar task support but differed in their intervention strategies. Results indicate that overall engagement remains stable across conditions, yet affective responses and perceived intrusiveness vary significantly with personality traits. Moreover, personality shapes interaction dynamics differently depending on the robot's behavior. These findings emphasize that effective human-robot interaction should account for individual differences, tailoring robot behavior to maintain engagement while respecting each user's unique affective and behavioral profile.

</details>


### [50] [Sandwich-Residuals: Parameter-Efficient Test-time Adaptation of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21740)
*Krishnam Soni,Aditya Sehgal,Vedant Dave,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Sandwich-Residuals：冻结预训练潜在世界模型，仅在线学习预测器周围的小型残差修正，利用自监督预测误差优化，无需奖励、标签或源域数据；在 AdaJEPA 基准和 DINO-WM 3-D 操作任务上以极少参数实现高效测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型虽可通过预测动作后果进行规划，但在测试时条件与训练分布不一致时预测会不可靠。现有测试时自适应方法通常需更新预训练模型的大量参数，并需选择适配哪些内部组件，成本高且复杂。

Method: 保持预训练世界模型冻结，仅在预测器周围学习小型残差修正；残差通过模型自身的自监督预测误差在线优化，不依赖奖励、标签或源域数据。在 AdaJEPA 基准的 21 种条件下评估，并在复合偏移及 DINO-WM 的 3-D 操作任务中验证。

Result: 在 AdaJEPA 上达到冻结模型 1.3 倍成功率，保留最强 AdaJEPA 变体 95% 的性能，同时适配参数少 97–99%。在复合偏移下提升至冻结模型 1.9 倍成功率，并与内部模块适配方法相当；在 DINO-WM 3-D 操作任务上同样验证该自适应原则。

Conclusion: 世界模型的有效测试时自适应不一定需要修改预训练内部权重；仅学习轻量残差修正即可在分布偏移下显著提升规划性能并大幅降低适配参数量。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning by predicting the effects of actions in a learned representation space, but their predictions can become unreliable when test-time conditions differ from training. Existing test-time adaptation methods address this by updating parts of the pretrained model, often modifying millions of parameters and requiring a choice of which internal components to adapt. We introduce Sandwich-Residuals, a lightweight alternative that keeps the pretrained world model frozen and learns only small residual corrections around the predictor. The residuals are optimized online using the model's self-supervised prediction error and require no rewards, labels, or source-domain data. Across 21 conditions on the AdaJEPA benchmark, our method achieves $1.3\times$ the success rate of the frozen model while retaining 95% of the performance of the strongest AdaJEPA variant and adapting 97-99% fewer parameters. Under compound shifts, this advantage increases to $1.9\times$ the success rate of the frozen model, while remaining comparable to internal block adaptation. We further demonstrate the same adaptation principle on a DINO-WM model for 3-D manipulation. These results suggest that effective test-time adaptation of world models does not necessarily require modifying their pretrained internal weights.

</details>


### [51] [When Should Robots Intervene? Balancing Engagement and Intrusiveness in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21734)
*Lavinia Hriscu,Valerio Bo,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在任务导向的人机交互中，机器人应通过自适应、情境敏感的干预策略来平衡用户参与度与感知侵入性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计有效的任务导向人机交互，需要兼顾用户参与度和机器人介入的社会可接受性；本文关注不同机器人干预策略如何影响用户体验、互动动态、感知侵入性和支持感。

Method: 比较两种策略：持续寻求互动的机器人策略，以及根据用户状态和任务情境选择性干预的情境感知策略；两者均依赖身体朝向和注意信号等多模态行为线索。通过32名参与者在模拟医院环境中的任务进行用户研究评估。

Result: 更高的互动频率不一定带来更好的参与度；感知支持与侵入性之间存在系统性权衡，并受物理距离、用户努力等因素影响。

Conclusion: 有效的人机交互参与依赖于自适应、情境敏感的干预策略。

Abstract: Designing effective Human-Robot Interaction in task-oriented settings requires carefully balancing user engagement with socially acceptable levels of robot intrusiveness. In this paper, we examine how different robot intervention strategies shape user experience, interaction dynamics, perceived intrusiveness, and sense of support. We compare two approaches: a continuous engagement-seeking robot strategy, and a context-aware strategy that selectively intervenes based on the user's state and task context. Both approaches rely on multimodal behavioral cues, including body orientation and attentional signals, to guide robot actions. We evaluate these strategies in a user study with 32 participants performing a task in a simulated hospital environment. Our findings show that higher interaction frequency does not necessarily lead to better engagement. Instead, we observe a systematic trade-off between perceived support and intrusiveness, influenced by factors such as physical proximity and user effort. These results provide empirical evidence that effective engagement in HRI depends on adaptive, context-sensitive intervention policies.

</details>


### [52] [Visual Proactivity: Enhancing Human-Robot Collaboration Through Intent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21729)
*Valerio Bo,Edison Bejarano,Anaís Garrell,Alberto Sanfeliu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“视觉主动性”方法，让机器人通过视觉反馈表达自身意图，并在人-机器人交接场景的 user study 中验证其能提升协调性与协作自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从重复任务转向人机协作时，理解人类意图很重要；已有研究多关注机器人推断和响应人类意图，较少关注机器人如何主动传达自身意图。

Method: 提出 visual proactivity：一种简单有效的视觉主动性方法，使机器人通过视觉反馈传达意图、影响人类行为并提升透明度和流畅性；在人到机器人交接场景中设计并评估 reactive、anticipatory、proactive 三类机器人行为，并通过用户研究验证人类感知。

Result: 用户研究表明，有效的视觉主动性可促进更好的人机对齐与协调，并验证了人类对 reactive、anticipatory、proactive 行为的感知差异。

Conclusion: 视觉主动性有助于机器人传达意图、提升协作透明度和流畅性，为更直观的人机协作奠定基础。

Abstract: As robots transition from performing repetitive tasks to collaborating with humans, understanding human intent becomes crucial to effective interaction. Anticipation enables robots to predict human actions, while proactivity allows them to take initiative and guide human behavior toward optimal outcomes. Although research has largely focused on how robots infer and respond to human intentions, less attention has been paid to how robots communicate their own intent. This paper introduces visual proactivity, a novel, simple yet effective approach that enables robots to communicate their intentions through visual feedback, influencing human behavior and enhancing transparency and fluency. We develop and evaluate proactive robotic behaviors in a human-to-robot handover scenario, where a user study validates human perception of reactive, anticipatory, and proactive behaviors. The results demonstrate that effective visual proactivity fosters better alignment and coordination, paving the way for more intuitive human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [53] [ZeroTouch: Tactile-Supervised Visual Contact Estimation for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21726)
*Dmitriy Kosenkov,Daniia Zinniatullina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Iana Zhura,Mikhail Derevianchenko,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZeroTouch 是一种仅在训练阶段使用触觉监督、部署时无需触觉硬件的机器人抓取框架，可从腕部 RGB、夹爪状态和局部重力方向预测接触形变、六轴力/力矩及抓取相关压缩目标，并在物理实验中显著优于 OpenVLA 和 SmolVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠抓取需要估计动态物理交互并选择与抓取相关的压缩目标；触觉传感器虽能直接测量交互，但部署时需要专用硬件。

Method: 提出 ZeroTouch：以触觉测量作为特权监督，仅训练阶段使用；部署时从腕部 RGB 观测、夹爪状态和局部重力方向预测密集接触形变、瞬时六轴力/力矩以及抓取相关期望压缩目标。

Result: 完整架构将法向力 MAE 从仅状态基线的 2.017 N 降至 0.531 N；每条件 20 次物理试验中，在未见物体、已见物体/未见抓取、内容/负载偏移下成功率分别为 95%、80%、90%，而 OpenVLA 为 25%、40%、55%，SmolVLA 为 10%、25%、35%。

Conclusion: ZeroTouch 表明无需部署触觉硬件，仅通过触觉监督训练即可从视觉等信号实现高成功率抓取，并显著优于现有 VLA 基线。

Abstract: Reliable robotic grasping benefits from estimating the evolving physical interaction and selecting a grasp-dependent compression target. Tactile sensors provide direct interaction measurements but require dedicated hardware at deployment. We introduce ZeroTouch, a tactile-supervised framework that predicts dense contact deformation, the instantaneous six-axis wrench, and a grasp-dependent desired compression target from wrist RGB observations, gripper state, and local gravity direction. Tactile measurements are used only as privileged supervision during training and are not required at deployment. On the full validation set, the complete architecture reduces normal-force MAE from 2.017 N for a state-only baseline to 0.531 N. In physical evaluation with 20 trials per condition, ZeroTouch achieves 95% success on an unseen object, 80% in a seen-object/unseen-grasp condition, and 90% under a content/load shift. Under the same evaluation protocol, OpenVLA achieves 25%, 40%, and 55%, while SmolVLA achieves 10%, 25%, and 35%, respectively.

</details>


### [54] [A Novel Path-Tracking Algorithm for Automated Tractor-Trailer Forward and Backward Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21718)
*Alexandre Lombard,Florent Perronnet,Nicolas Gaud,Abdeljalil Abbas-Turki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向单挂车铰接车辆的几何式路径跟踪算法，通过挂车级横向控制与牵引车转向角短时域预测调整，实现前进和倒车稳定跟踪，且无需逐车标定或训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全自主拖拉机-挂车系统在物流、农业和工业场景中日益普及，精确鲁棒的路径跟踪至关重要；但牵引车与挂车之间的铰接关系引入额外非线性，使横向和纵向控制显著复杂化，倒车工况尤为困难。

Method: 提出一种无需逐车标定或训练的几何控制方法：在挂车层面施加横向控制律，并对牵引车转向角进行短时域预测调整，从而保证车辆在前进和后退运动中均可稳定收敛至期望路径。

Result: 在高保真物理仿真中，该控制器在精度、稳定性和抗扰动鲁棒性方面能够匹配或优于经典方法及当前先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法可有效解决单挂车铰接车辆的路径跟踪问题，尤其适用于前进与倒车机动，并展现出较强的实用部署潜力。

Abstract: Fully autonomous tractor--trailer systems are increasingly deployed in logistics, agriculture, and industrial environments, where precise and robust path-tracking capabilities are essential. However, the articulation between the tractor and the trailer introduces additional nonlinearities and significantly complicates lateral and longitudinal control, particularly during reversing maneuvers. This paper introduces a novel path-tracking algorithm specifically designed for articulated vehicles with a single trailer. The proposed method combines a lateral control law applied at the trailer level with a short-horizon predictive adjustment of the tractor steering angle, ensuring stable convergence toward the desired path in both forward and backward motion. The approach is geometry-based and requires no per-vehicle calibration or training. Simulation studies in a high-fidelity physics simulator demonstrate the ability of the controller to match or outperform classical and state-of-the-art methods in terms of accuracy, stability, and robustness to disturbances.

</details>


### [55] [AgenticSwarm: Semantic Perception and Adaptive Task Allocation for Heterogeneous Multi-UAV Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21716)
*Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Faryal Batool,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Valerii Serpiva,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AgenticSwarm，一个面向异构多无人机任务的智能体框架，将语义感知、约束任务分配与自适应重规划结合，以应对环境变化和执行中断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境中的多无人机任务要求系统同时理解周围场景与人类操作员意图，并在任务条件变化时保持可行的任务分配。

Method: 智能体解析航拍图像和自然语言指令，构建与任务需求、能力约束和任务依赖关联的接地任务表示；该信息增强约束任务分配，纳入障碍感知路径可行性、能耗和受保护返航要求。执行中若发生无人机故障、电量下降或任务修改，则从当前系统状态重建剩余任务，同时保留已完成工作和侦察进展。

Result: 在五个 Gazebo 环境和室内真实测试环境中评估。相较 Grounding DINO+SAM 2.1 感知基线，基于 SAM3 的流程将类别感知召回率提高 25.2 个百分点，语义标签准确率提高 29.5 个百分点。消融剩余任务重规划后，平均重复工作从 0% 增至 61.7%，事件后恢复时间增加 58.6%。

Conclusion: AgenticSwarm 能将语义推理、约束分配和自适应多无人机任务执行连接起来；残留任务重规划对减少重复工作和加快事件后恢复具有关键贡献。

Abstract: Multi UAV missions in complex environments require the system to understand both the surrounding scene and the intent of a human operator while maintaining feasible task allocation as mission conditions change. This paper presents AgenticSwarm, an agentic framework for semantic perception and adaptive task allocation in heterogeneous multi UAV missions. An agent interprets aerial imagery and natural language instructions to construct a grounded mission representation that links perceived objects and regions with task requirements, capability constraints, and mission dependencies. This information augments a constrained task allocation process in which obstacle aware path feasibility, energy consumption, and protected return home requirements are incorporated before assignment. During execution, changes such as UAV failure, battery degradation, or task modification trigger residual mission reconstruction from the current system state, while completed work and reconnaissance progress are retained. AgenticSwarm is evaluated across five diverse Gazebo environments and an indoor real test environment, demonstrating its ability to connect semantic reasoning with constrained allocation and adaptive multi UAV mission execution. Compared with a Grounding DINO+SAM~2.1 perception baseline, the SAM3-based pipeline improves class-aware recall by 25.2 percentage points (pp) and semantic label accuracy by 29.5 pp. Ablating residual mission replanning increases mean repeated work from 0% to 61.7% and post-event recovery time by 58.6%, highlighting the contribution of adaptive replanning to mission execution.

</details>


### [56] [NeuRIO: A Streaming Neural Estimator for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Multi-Robot Relative Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21707)
*Zhehan Li,Jiadong Lu,Shengwei Ren,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuRIO 是一种仅用已识别的机器人间方位、距离与 IMU 测量的流式神经估计器，实现无锚点 6-DoF 相对惯性里程计，并具备零样本 sim-to-real 迁移与良好可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人相对定位通常依赖锚点、优化方法计算量随团队规模增长，且难以适应不同团队规模、时变观测图和真实传感器退化；作者希望实现免锚点、可实时扩展并可直接迁移到真实场景的相对惯性里程计。

Method: NeuRIO 将测量统一到重力对齐坐标系，把机器人建模为节点、相互观测建模为因子，用注意力做空间推理、GRU 做时间建模；作为图网络，它在整个网络中共享节点级和因子级算子，从而支持不同团队规模与时变观测图。训练在仿真中进行，耦合多种运动模式、设备级传感器特性和多样化、真实建模且时间持续的传感器损坏，以实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。

Result: 在 24 条真实序列上，位置 RMSE 为 14.1 cm，旋转 RMSE 为 3.9°；计算可扩展性强，仿真中最多 400 个机器人时更新耗时低于 20 ms，而优化方法在仅 24 个机器人时已超过 20 ms。此外，即使只用有限团队规模训练，NeuRIO 无需改变架构或参数即可直接迁移到未见过的更大团队。

Conclusion: NeuRIO 表明基于图神经网络的流式估计可在免锚点、多机器人 6-DoF 相对惯性里程计中兼顾真实场景精度、零样本 sim-to-real 迁移、实时性与团队规模泛化能力。

Abstract: We present NeuRIO, a streaming neural estimator for anchor-free 6-DoF relative inertial odometry using only identified inter-robot bearings, ranges, and IMU measurements. NeuRIO canonicalizes measurements into gravity-aligned coordinates, represents robots as nodes and mutual observations as factors, and uses attention for spatial reasoning and GRUs for temporal modeling. As a graph network, NeuRIO applies shared node-wise and factor-wise operators throughout the network, enabling it to handle different team sizes and time-varying observation graphs. NeuRIO is trained on a simulator that couples various motion patterns, device-level sensor characteristics, and diverse, realistic modeled, and temporally persistent sensor corruptions. In this way, NeuRIO achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. Across $24$ real-world sequences, NeuRIO achieves $14.1\,\mathrm{cm}$ position RMSE and $3.9^\circ$ rotation RMSE. More importantly, NeuRIO demonstrates strong computational scalability, maintaining an update cost below $20\,\mathrm{ms}$ with up to $400$ robots in simulation, while optimization-based methods exceed $20\,\mathrm{ms}$ at only $24$ robots. Moreover, even trained on limited team sizes, NeuRIO transfers directly to unseen larger teams without architectural or parameter changes.

</details>


### [57] [RAYA: Learning Where and When to Intervene for Robot Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21690)
*Ishaan Mahajan,Charles Chen,Frederike Dümbgen,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAYA 将学习到的有限时域可恢复性裕度嵌入带硬约束的最优控制器，并用有界学习调度器动态调整任务权重，使机器人在动作选择时兼顾可恢复性，从而提升仿真与真实飞行中的生存率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全机制常在预测失败后才反应，但此时名义规划可能已耗尽恢复所需的控制权限，固定任务优先级也会阻碍剩余响应。关键洞察是：可恢复性与任务目标都应在控制器内部、动作选择过程中被决定，而非事后否决。

Method: 提出 RAYA 混合学习-解析框架：在硬约束最优控制器中引入学习到的有限时域可恢复性裕度，使动作选择受可恢复性影响；并配对有界学习调度器，在可恢复性下降时移动任务权重以促进恢复。学习组件可零样本迁移。

Result: 每个控制器进行 7,200 次仿真，覆盖四旋翼与自动驾驶基准；RAYA 不仅提高生存率，还能零样本迁移到未见轨迹、扰动、对象/模型偏移和摩擦布局。其嵌入式实现部署于 35g Crazyflie 四旋翼；在风扰且存在气动失配或未建模 40% 电机指令损失的 40 次硬件飞行中，三个基线全部失败，RAYA 完成 10/10 六周期任务。

Conclusion: 将可恢复性作为控制器内的一等约束并动态调节任务优先级，可显著提升安全恢复能力，且学习组件可迁移到真实嵌入式平台。

Abstract: A robot can predict failure and still be unable to prevent it. By the time a safety mechanism reacts, the nominal plan may already have spent the control authority that recovery requires, and fixed task priorities may block whatever response remains. Our key insight is that both aspects are decided inside the controller. Recoverability must inform actions while they are chosen rather than veto them afterward, and task objectives must be adapted as recoverability shrinks. Building on this, we present RAYA, a hybrid learned-analytic framework that places a learned finite-horizon recoverability margin inside an optimal controller with hard constraints and pairs it with a bounded learned scheduler that shifts task weights to facilitate recovery. Across 7,200 simulation episodes per controller spanning quadrotor and autonomous-vehicle benchmarks, RAYA not only improves survival rates, but also transfers the learned components zero-shot to unseen trajectories, disturbances, plant shifts, and friction layouts. We developed an embedded realization of RAYA and deployed it on-board a 35g Crazyflie quadrotor. Across 40 combined hardware flights under wind with either aerodynamic mismatch or an unmodeled 40% motor-command loss, each of three baselines fails in all trials, while RAYA completes 10/10 six-cycle missions. Project Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [58] [Outcome-Conditioned End-Effector Geometry Across Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21659)
*Xingyu Lin,Zhuang Li,Zhongrun Wu,Shouquan Zhou,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过1.5万次LIBERO闭环rollout分析四种VLA策略的末端执行器几何一致性，发现双成功策略对的距离显著小于仅单成功对，但低跨策略距离不等于策略可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅以任务成功率无法判断不同动作接口的VLA策略是否在物理执行上达成一致，因此需要直接比较跨策略的末端执行器轨迹几何。

Method: 对四种VLA策略在LIBERO中执行15,000次闭环rollout；主分析在干净条件下构建3,600个配置匹配且因此非独立的策略对。以归一化动态时间规整距离度量末端执行器几何，比较双成功、仅单成功与双失败策略对，并在所有任务、策略对和九种采样/带限表示中检验。另进行72动作窗口、端点与时长调整、复合视觉压力扰动，以及与同任务示教轨迹的对照。

Result: 双成功策略对的中位归一化DTW距离为0.0120 m，仅单成功对为0.0380 m；该排序在所有任务、所有策略对和九种表示中成立，但比值随表示变化数倍，故只报告方向而非固定倍数。双失败对分离更大，但因支持稀疏且不均，仅作探索性结果。成功执行内部，替换伙伴带来的分离大于初始状态变化。匹配基线下仍存在可测且异质的残余策略差异。成功执行与同任务示教轨迹的距离，约等于示教轨迹彼此之间的距离。72动作窗口保留排序但降低幅度；端点与时长调整后，混合结果相对双成功仍为正系数，但幅度依赖设定。复合视觉压力下，策略排名与策略对组成同时改变。

Conclusion: 跨策略末端执行器几何与任务成功存在关联，但低跨策略距离并不意味可互换；结果支持任务相关几何，却无法区分训练数据重叠与任务约束。视觉压力会同时改变策略排名和比较组成，提示评估需关注条件与表示敏感性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies solve the same manipulation task through different action interfaces, but task success alone does not establish whether their physical executions agree. We study cross-policy end-effector geometry in 15,000 closed-loop LIBERO rollouts from four policies. The primary clean-condition analysis forms 3,600 configuration-matched, and therefore dependent, policy pairs. Both-success pairs have a median normalized dynamic time warping distance of 0.0120 m versus 0.0380 m when exactly one policy succeeds. This ordering holds in every task, every policy pair, and nine sampling and band-limited representations; however, the ratio varies severalfold across representations, so we report the direction rather than a fixed multiple. Both-failure pairs are more separated again but rest on thin, uneven support, so we report them as exploratory. Within successful executions, partner replacements separate more across tasks than across initial states. A matched baseline still reveals measurable, heterogeneous residual policy differences, so a low cross-policy distance does not imply interchangeability. Successful executions sit about as far from same-task demonstrations as those demonstrations sit from each other, compatible with task-associated geometry without separating training-data overlap from task constraints. A common 72-action window preserves the ordering but reduces its magnitude; endpoint and duration adjustment likewise leaves a positive mixed-outcome coefficient relative to both-success pairs, though its magnitude is specification-dependent. Under composite visual stress, policy rankings and pair composition change together.

</details>


### [59] [SynthDemo-RL: Breaking the Zero-Reward Barrier in VLA Adaptation with LLM-Guided Synthetic Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21650)
*Hiroaki Kingetsu,Hiroaki Kurihara,Kaoru Yokoo,Kenji Fukumizu,Manohar Kaul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SynthDemo-RL，通过自动教师生成成功轨迹、蒸馏 VLA 学生并用稀疏二值奖励 PPO 精调，在无新人类演示下显著提升扰动任务成功率，并验证仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型微调通常依赖人类遥操作演示；使用稀疏二值奖励的强化学习在成功轨迹稀少时面临严重探索难题，直接 PPO 难以覆盖零成功率任务。

Method: SynthDemo-RL 采用教师-学生框架：自动教师利用模拟器特权状态生成成功操作轨迹；VLA 学生通过监督微调从这些轨迹中蒸馏；随后用带二值任务成功奖励的 PPO 精调学生。引入奖励覆盖率，即固定评测协议下至少观测到一次成功的任务比例，作为平均成功率的补充指标。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 上，原始 LIBERO 微调的 pi_0.5 策略在 57 个计分任务中有 27 个成功率为 0%。相同 PPO 配方与算力下直接 PPO 仅挽救其中 10 个，仍有 17 个为 0%；SynthDemo-RL 每任务仅用 50 条合成轨迹且无新人类演示，挽救全部 27 个任务，Position 和 Task 轴平均成功率分别达 97.8% 和 97.1%。在标准 LIBERO 上无人类演示达 96.0%，与每任务 50 条人类演示训练的 pi_0.5 相差 1.7 个百分点。另在 RoboTwin 2.0 验证，MuJoCo 孪生中训练的策略轨迹可在物理机器人上开环执行。

Conclusion: SynthDemo-RL 利用自动合成演示解决稀疏奖励探索瓶颈，在无新人类演示条件下实现高成功率，并展现出跨基准与仿真到真实部署的有效性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly relies on human teleoperation demonstrations, while reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse binary rewards faces an exploration challenge when successful trajectories are rarely sampled. We propose SynthDemo-RL, a teacher-student framework in which an automated teacher converts simulator-privileged state into successful manipulation trajectories, a VLA student is distilled from them by supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and PPO with binary task-success rewards refines the student. We study reward coverage, the fraction of tasks for which at least one success is observed under the fixed evaluation protocol, as a complement to the average success rate. On LIBERO-PRO, a public benchmark of perturbed LIBERO tasks for which no demonstrations exist, 27 of 57 scored tasks are at exactly 0% success for a pi_0.5 policy fine-tuned on the original LIBERO tasks. Direct PPO from this policy, under the same PPO recipe and the same RL compute as SynthDemo-RL's refinement stage, rescues 10 of these 27 tasks and leaves 17 at 0%. SynthDemo-RL, with 50 synthesized trajectories per task and no new human demonstrations, rescues all 27 and reaches average success rates of 97.8% and 97.1% on the Position and Task axes of LIBERO-PRO, respectively. On standard LIBERO, the same pipeline reaches 96.0% with no human demonstrations, within 1.7 points of pi_0.5 trained on 50 human demonstrations per task. We further validate the pipeline on RoboTwin 2.0 and verify that trajectories from a policy trained in a MuJoCo twin execute open-loop on a physical robot.

</details>


### [60] [Towards Fine-Grained Object Manipulation: SAM3-Guided Visuomotor Policy with Persistent Memory Learning and Focused Visual Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21621)
*Haolong Meng,Fangbo Qin,Mengchen Bai,Houwu Wang,Cirong Liu,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAM3引导的视觉运动框架，通过FOM-SAM3持久对象记忆和FSAE聚焦空间-外观编码，实现机器人对细粒度物体的可靠操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度物体操作要求机器人从视觉相似物体和干扰中区分指定目标；现有场景级视觉条件缺乏显式物体选择，类别级指导又无法可靠区分同类细粒度物体。

Method: 方法包括：1）FOM-SAM3：在冻结SAM3下，从有限多视角注册图像学习可重用FO记忆token，通过one-vs-rest学习编码持久记忆，定位目标FO并拒绝相似替代，存入记忆库；2）FSAE：结合FO内局部外观特征与显式边界框坐标，为DP和ACT等动作策略提供条件。

Result: 在FO-30数据集（30个物理对象、4个粗类别）上验证FOM-SAM3；在三个真实机器人FO操作任务中，FOM-SAM3引导的策略对干扰物鲁棒，能区分相似FO，并可扩展到新FO。

Conclusion: SAM3引导的视觉运动框架通过持久对象记忆和聚焦视觉条件，有效提升细粒度物体操作的区分、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Fine-grained object (FO) manipulation requires robots to distinguish a specified FO from visually similar objects and execute actions reliably despite scene distractors. However, scene-level visual conditioning lacks explicit object selection, while category-level guidance cannot reliably distinguish FOs within the same category. We present a SAM3-guided visuomotor framework that addresses these challenges through persistent object memory and focused visual conditioning. First, we introduce FO Memory-driven SAM3 (FOM-SAM3), which learns reusable FO memory tokens from limited multi-view registration images while keeping SAM3 fully frozen. Through one-vs-rest learning, these tokens encode persistent memories for localizing target FOs and rejecting similar alternatives, which can be stored in a memory bank. Second, we propose Focused Spatial-Appearance Encoding (FSAE), which combines in-FO local appearance features with explicit bounding-box coordinates to condition action policies including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT). The effectiveness of the proposed FOM-SAM3 was validated on the FO-30 dataset comprising 30 physical objects across four coarse categories. Across three real-robot FO manipulation tasks, our FOM-SAM3-guided policies demonstrated robustness against distractors, discrimination ability among similar FOs, and extendibility to new FOs.

</details>


### [61] [CounterPlay: Counterfactual Post-Training for Self-Play Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21617)
*Jiarong Wei,Yin Wu,Runkai He,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CounterPlay提出一种反事实自我博弈后训练方法，通过失败回溯、奖励条件化重试与安全验证，在极少仿真预算下显著提升驾驶策略的闭环表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高吞吐自我博弈训练的驾驶策略闭环鲁棒，但随训练规模增大单位仿真收益递减；常见场景早已学会，后续rollout反复遇到未解决的失败。现有后训练多在已访问状态评估替代动作或延续，而成功恢复可能需要更早改变驾驶风格。

Method: CounterPlay从失败任务回溯，在备用驾驶风格下重试。具体包括：1）利用策略价值估计选择更早存储状态进行失败驱动回溯；2）通过奖励条件化让单一策略以从谨慎到激进的候选风格重试；3）仅保留任务完成且相对事实分支未使其他车辆新增或提前碰撞/驶出道路的重试；通过新鲜随机性验证后，将重试蒸馏回部署条件下的策略。

Result: 在BehaviorBench的Interactive和Random划分、全部八种交通场景中取得SOTA；仅用1B后训练transition，为锚点100B自我博弈预算的1%。相对锚点提升覆盖三种评估驾驶风格。解决锚点大量超时案例，并在任务完成与安全间取得平衡，优于继续自我博弈或采用更激进风格。

Conclusion: CounterPlay表明，针对失败的反事实回溯与风格条件化重试后训练，可在极低仿真成本下有效修正驾驶策略的稀疏失败并兼顾安全。

Abstract: Self-play in high-throughput simulators yields driving policies with robust closed-loop performance, but improvement per unit of simulation diminishes as training scales. Policies learn to handle common situations early, while further rollouts repeatedly encounter unresolved failures. Post-training offers an opportunity to target these failures, but existing methods primarily evaluate alternative actions or continuations at visited states, although successful recovery may require changing driving style earlier. We propose CounterPlay, a counterfactual self-play post-training approach that backtracks from failed tasks and retries them under alternate driving styles. CounterPlay rests on three key components. First, failure-driven backtracking uses the policy's value estimates to select an earlier stored state from which to retry the task. Second, reward conditioning enables a single policy to retry the task from this state using candidate styles ranging from cautious to aggressive. Third, CounterPlay retains task-completing retries only if no other vehicle incurs a new or earlier collision or off-road event relative to the factual branch. Retries that pass verification with fresh randomness are then distilled into the policy under its deployment condition. On BehaviorBench, CounterPlay achieves state-of-the-art scores on both the Interactive and Random splits across all eight traffic regimes using 1B post-training transitions, which is just 1% of the anchor's 100B self-play training budget. Improvements over the anchor hold across all three evaluated driving styles. CounterPlay resolves a substantial fraction of the anchor's timeout cases on BehaviorBench and achieves a balance between task completion and safety that neither continued self-play nor adopting a more aggressive driving style attains.

</details>


### [62] [Potential-Field Action Representation for Reinforcement Learning in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21609)
*Xinyu Liu,Gökhan Solak,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PA-RL：用人工势场作为强化学习动作表示，策略不直接输出笛卡尔运动，而是调节势场参数，再由势场生成状态相关导引方向并通过笛卡尔阻抗控制器执行；在轴孔插入任务中取得100%仿真成功率，并实现9/9真机零微调插入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型强化学习可用于接触丰富的机器人操作，但通常需同时学习任务策略和底层运动生成。直接笛卡尔命令接口要求策略在每个决策步生成运动，将任务级适应与连续底层控制耦合，增加学习负担；因此动作表示对探索和物理执行至关重要。

Method: PA-RL将人工势场作为动作表示：策略调节类能量势场的参数，势场根据状态生成导引方向，并由笛卡尔阻抗控制器执行。在具有非线性动力学和接触不连续性的轴孔插入任务中，与笛卡尔速度、笛卡尔位姿和可变阻抗动作空间在相同RL算法下对比。

Result: PA-RL是唯一在给定训练时间内达到100%评估成功率的方法，最佳基线为92.6%；相较最佳基线，关节力矩变化降低55.4%，笛卡尔加速度变化降低70.8%，且奖励中未显式加入运动质量惩罚。仿真训练策略无需微调即完成9/9真机插入。

Conclusion: 结果表明，势场动作接口能有效提升接触丰富操作任务的学习成功率与运动质量，并具备从仿真到真机部署的可行性。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning can acquire contact-rich robotic manipulation skills through trial-and-error interaction, but it often requires the policy to learn both task strategy and low-level motion generation. In this setting, the action representation is critical because it determines how policy outputs are converted into robot motion, shaping both exploration and physical execution. Direct Cartesian command interfaces require the policy to generate motion at every decision step, coupling task-level adaptation with continuous low-level control and increasing the learning burden. We propose PA-RL, a reinforcement-learning framework that uses artificial potential fields as the action representation. Instead of commanding motion directly, the policy adapts the parameters of an energy-like potential field, which generates a state-dependent guidance direction executed through a Cartesian impedance controller. We evaluate PA-RL on peg-in-hole insertion, a representative contact-rich task with nonlinear dynamics and discontinuous contact transitions. In simulation, PA-RL is compared with Cartesian velocity, Cartesian pose, and variable-impedance action spaces using the same RL algorithm. PA-RL is the only method to reach a 100% evaluation success rate within the allotted training time, while the best baseline reaches 92.6%. It also reduces joint-torque variation by 55.4% and Cartesian acceleration variation by 70.8% relative to the best baseline, without explicit motion-quality penalties in the reward. The simulation-trained policy further completes 9/9 real-robot insertions without fine-tuning, demonstrating the deployment feasibility of the learned potential-field interface.

</details>


### [63] [A High-Payload Wall-Climbing Robot Using Passive Bistable Suction Cups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21584)
*Andrew Nguyen,Mingyuan Li,Daniel Bruder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种采用被动双稳态吸盘的高负载壁面爬行机器人，通过履带机构自动接合/脱开吸盘，实现光滑表面垂直、侧向和倒置爬行，并能拖曳7.940 kg负载，负载自重比达2.25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壁面爬行机器人可用于窗户清洗、检测、维护和建筑等危险或高劳动强度任务的自动化；主动粘附负载能力高但需持续供能，被动吸盘无需供能却负载受限，因此需要兼顾被动粘附与高负载能力。

Method: 设计新型高负载壁面爬行机器人，采用被动双稳态吸盘，无需被压向壁面即可产生粘附；通过履带式系统自动使双稳态吸盘接合与脱开，从而在光滑表面实现移动。

Result: 机器人可在玻璃、木材、金属和涂漆表面垂直爬壁，也可侧向和倒置爬行；能够拖曳7.940 kg负载，负载自重比为2.25。

Conclusion: 表明被动双稳态吸盘结合履带式自动接合/脱开机构，可实现无需持续供能的高负载壁面爬行，适用于光滑表面作业。

Abstract: Wall-climbing robots capable of scaling vertical surfaces could help automate hazardous or labor intensive tasks such as window washing, inspection, maintenance, and construction. Active adhesion methods achieve higher payload capacities, but require power to maintain their grip. Passive adhesion devices such as suction cups are an attractive option for such robots because they do not require power to maintain their grip, but they are limited by their payload capacity. This work presents a novel high-payload wall-climbing robot that utilizes passive bistable suction cups to generate adhesion without needing to be pushed into the wall. The robot features a track-based system that automatically engages and disengages bistable suction cups to achieve locomotion on smooth surfaces. The robot is able to achieve vertical wall climbing on glass, wood, metal, and painted surfaces, sideways and upside-down climbing, and is able to tow a payload of 7.940 kg (with a payload-to-weight ratio of 2.25).

</details>


### [64] [Tilt as a Certified Resource: Preserving Motor Wrench-Rate Authority on Articulated Multirotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21580)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文针对全驱动多旋翼在扰动下快速调制扳手的能力，提出以伺服倾转作为主动形态资源，并构造仅考虑电机的 readiness 证书（可达 wrench-rate 集对数体积），作为 CBF 嵌入统一物理命令 QP。仿真表明该方法可避免经典分配器发散和未认证 articulated 分配器违反安全下限，在强阵风下约束状态并保持车辆 authority。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全驱动多旋翼不仅需跟踪标称 wrench，还需保留在扰动下快速调制 wrench 的 readiness。经典 effort-minimizing 分配忽略该动态限制；最大化 readiness 会产生拓扑不连通最优面并要求物理不可实现的执行器速率；固定几何对称平台上还存在零和退化，仅靠电机转速重分配无法在提升 authority 的同时不牺牲 wrench 跟踪。因此需用主动 morphology 打破退化。

Method: 将 servo tilt 视为几何资源，提供静态旋翼不具备的 authority-recovery 方向。构造 configuration-dependent、motor-only 的 readiness certificate，即可达 wrench-rate set 的 log-volume，并显式排除伺服容量，以避免 ghost capacity fallacy：证书不应错误计入慢速机械运动学极限，而应在电机饱和时准确崩溃。该证书作为 Control Barrier Function 约束，嵌入 Unified Physical-Command Quadratic Program，作用于 motor torques 与 servo setpoints。

Result: 在 severe gust disturbances 下对 articulated octorotor 进行闭环仿真：classical allocators 发散，uncertified articulated allocators 违反 readiness safety floor，而提出的 CBF filter 能约束系统状态并保持 vehicle authority。

Conclusion: 主动形态与仅电机 readiness 证书的 CBF-QP 方法可在强扰动下维持全驱动多旋翼的 wrench 调制权限，避免虚假容量与安全下限违反，优于经典分配器和未认证 articulated 分配器。

Abstract: Fully-actuated multirotor aerial vehicles must not only track nominal wrenches but retain the "readiness" to modulate them rapidly under disturbances. Classical effort-minimizing allocators ignore this dynamic limit, whereas maximizing readiness leads to topologically disconnected optimal sheets demanding physically impossible actuator rates. Enforcing a readiness safety floor on fixed-geometry symmetric platforms further encounters a zero-sum degeneracy: motor-speed redistribution cannot improve authority without conceding wrench tracking. This paper uses active morphology to break the degeneracy, treating servo tilt as a geometric resource supplying authority-recovery directions unavailable to static rotors. We construct a configuration-dependent, motor-only readiness certificate - the log-volume of the reachable wrench-rate set - that explicitly excludes servo capacity, preventing a "ghost capacity fallacy" in which the certificate would falsely credit slow mechanical kinematic limits instead of collapsing accurately at motor saturation. The certificate is enforced as a Control Barrier Function (CBF) within a Unified Physical-Command Quadratic Program acting on motor torques and servo setpoints. Closed-loop simulations of an articulated octorotor under severe gust disturbances show classical allocators diverging and uncertified articulated allocators violating the safety floor, while the proposed CBF filter bounds the system state and preserves vehicle authority.

</details>


### [65] [SABER: Learning Attention-based Semantic Affordance for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21572)
*Hari Prasanth Palanivelu,Samuel Sze,Kennard Garrison Johannes,Albertus Hendrawan Adiwahono,Meng Yee,Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SABER是一种无规划器的强化学习策略，通过统一地形可供性图联合推理地形几何与语义接触许可，使四足机器人在工业环境中避开几何可行但不宜踩踏的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知运动策略主要整合地形几何，却很少融入地形语义；管道、草地、易碎箱等区域几何上可通过，但接触可能损坏设备、导致机器人失稳或危及场地，工业场景对落足安全性要求更高。

Method: 提出SABER无规划器RL策略，输入统一地形可供性图，每格编码局部3D几何和语义接触成本。在交叉注意力中引入可学习的有符号语义偏置：加于注意力logits、由接触成本门控，并按距最近足距离重新加权标记单元，使危险仅在能影响下一落足点时重塑注意力。策略选择允许支撑面上的落足点，并在摆动相全程避让禁触区域。

Result: 系统消融表明，仅移除语义偏置就会使禁触接触增加55%，而速度跟踪性能不变；在Unitree B2上完成实机验证，于室内外环境及四类语义障碍上实现sim-to-real语义接触选择。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将几何信息与语义接触许可联合建模，可有效提升四足机器人在复杂工业环境中的安全落足与接触选择能力。

Abstract: Perceptive legged locomotion has advanced rapidly by integrating terrain geometry into learned policies, yet the integration of terrain meaning remains sparse: a pipe, a patch of grass, or a fragile box may be geometrically traversable while being inappropriate for contact. In industrial environments, where legged robots increasingly operate, a single misplaced step can damage fragile equipment, destabilize the robot, or endanger the site. To address this, we introduce SABER, a planner-free reinforcement-learning policy that jointly reasons about terrain geometry and semantic contact permission. The policy consumes a unified terrain-affordance map, where each cell encodes local 3D geometry and a semantic contact cost. We augment cross-attention with a learned, signed semantic bias: an additive term on the attention logits, gated by the contact cost, that reweights flagged cells by their distance from the nearest foot. A hazard therefore reshapes attention where it can still affect the next foothold, and its influence fades where it cannot. The resulting policy selects footholds on permitted support and keeps the leg clear of forbidden regions throughout the swing phase. We perform a systematic ablation that isolates the contribution of each architectural component; removing the semantic bias alone increases forbidden contacts by 55% while velocity tracking is unchanged. We validate the policy on a Unitree B2, demonstrating sim-to-real semantic contact selection across indoor and outdoor environments and four semantic obstacle classes.

</details>


### [66] [Skel-WAM: A Hand-Skeleton-Conditioned World Action Model for Human-to-Robot Manipulation Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21514)
*Zetao Cai,Yaping Li,Yiqun Wang,Xinyu Zhan,Yuyin Yang,Haoxiang Ma,Kailin Li,Tao Lu,Jiangmiao Pang,Linning Xu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Skel-WAM 是一种世界动作模型，通过统一手部骨架运动接口弥合人类视频与机器人演示之间的具身差异，实现人机数据联合训练并提升机器人操作性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据采集成本高且任务分布覆盖有限；人类视频虽低成本且互补，但存在视觉外观和动作空间上的具身鸿沟，难以直接用于机器人学习。

Method: 提出 Skel-WAM，以统一手部拓扑对齐人与机器人运动：使用骨架叠加将运动锚定到场景，并用结构化 2.5-D 关键点编码显式手部运动学。Video Expert 与 Keypoint Expert 通过 Mixture-of-Transformers 联合学习视觉和骨骼动态；独立的机器人训练 Action Expert 将预测映射为可执行控制。该分离设计使人类视频无需机器人动作标签即可与机器人演示共同监督共享动态。

Result: 在四个真实世界双臂任务和七个仿真任务中，Skel-WAM 平均成功率分别为 79.86% 和 63.29%，超过最强基线 22.22 和 8.28 个百分点。人机协同训练在机器人训练数据未覆盖的任务变体上将真实世界成功率从 38.89% 提升至 86.11%，提升超过一倍。

Conclusion: 共享骨骼接口能够有效联合学习人类与机器人数据，并利用互补的人类演示扩展机器人任务覆盖范围，显著提升真实与仿真环境中的操作成功率和泛化性能。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations are expensive to collect and often provide limited distributional coverage of task variations. Human videos offer a low-cost source of complementary manipulation experience, but learning from them requires bridging embodiment gaps in visual appearance and action spaces. We introduce Skel-WAM, a world action model that bridges these differences through a unified hand-skeleton motion interface. The key insight is to align human and robot motion through a common hand topology, combining skeleton overlays that ground motion in the scene with structured 2.5-D keypoints that encode explicit hand kinematics. Video and Keypoint Experts jointly learn visual and skeletal dynamics through a Mixture-of-Transformers, while a separate robot-trained Action Expert maps these predictions to executable controls. This separation enables human and robot demonstrations to directly supervise shared dynamics without requiring robot action labels for human videos. Across four real-world bimanual tasks and seven simulated tasks, Skel-WAM achieves average success rates of 79.86% and 63.29%, surpassing the strongest baseline by 22.22 and 8.28 percentage points, respectively. Human-robot cotraining more than doubles real-world success on task variations absent from robot training data, from 38.89% to 86.11%. These results demonstrate that a shared skeletal interface enables joint learning across human and robot data and expands robot task coverage through complementary human demonstrations.

</details>


### [67] [2nd Place Solution to the HANDS 2026 Workshop Challenge-Dexterous Grasp Motion Track: Single-Shot Trajectory Warping for Grasp Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21511)
*Muneeb A. Khan,Woojin Kim,Shinwoo Kim,Muhammad Munsif,Binod Bhattarai,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HANDS 2026 / ECCV 2026 灵巧抓取运动赛道亚军方案：不逐步生成抓取运动，而是对一条成功 GraspM3 演示进行 12 维形变编辑，并用一步 PPO 在 4,824 个训练对象上并行训练形变策略，最终在私有测试集简单赛道达 94.61%、困难赛道达 57.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 12 自由度 LinkerHand O6，在随机初始手位下为未见物体生成物理可行的 reach-and-lift 轨迹。逐步策略每步约需做出 70 个 12 维决策，误差随时间累积，且测试物体与物理动态可能偏离训练分布。

Method: 核心是演示编辑而非逐步生成：策略仅观测物体一次，输出对单条成功 GraspM3 演示的 12-DoF 形变（warp），随后开环回放。使用 one-step PPO 在所有 4,824 个训练对象上并行训练该形变策略。

Result: 在私有测试集上，简单赛道成功率为 94.61%，为所有提交中最高；困难赛道成功率为 57.18%。该方案获得 HANDS 2026 挑战赛 Dexterous Grasp Motion track 第二名。

Conclusion: 通过单次物体观测生成演示形变并开环执行，可避免逐步策略的误差累积；结合大规模并行 one-step PPO 训练，该方案在灵巧抓取运动生成任务中取得高成功率并具备泛化能力。

Abstract: This report describes our 2nd place solution to the HANDS 2026 workshop challenge (Dexterous Grasp Motion track) in conjunction with ECCV 2026. In this challenge, we address grasp motion generation for the 12-DoF LinkerHand O6, aiming to produce physically plausible reach-and-lift trajectories for unseen objects from randomized initial hand poses in simulation. This task is particularly challenging because each grasp requires a per-step policy to make approximately $70$ twelve-dimensional decisions, with errors accumulating over time, while test objects and physical dynamics may differ from those encountered during training. To address these challenges, we propose editing a single successful GraspM3 demonstration instead of generating the motion step by step: a policy observes the object once and outputs a 12-D warp of the demonstration, which is then replayed open-loop. Moreover, we train the warp policy with one-step PPO over all $4{,}824$ training objects in parallel. As a result, our method achieved success rates of $94.61\%$ on the easy track, the highest of all submissions, and $57.18\%$ on the hard track of the private test set.

</details>


### [68] [LEMCA: LLM-Guided Synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21319)
*Arjun Krishna,Vincent Pacelli,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LEMCA，利用大语言模型在进化循环中自动合成资源高效的模式切换控制器，避免传统单体式控制器在简单阶段浪费资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然物理控制任务（如驾驶、物体操作）的感知与计算复杂度随时间剧烈变化，机器人控制本应动态切换不同资源分配的控制模式；但模式切换控制器（MSC）设计依赖专家、成本高，导致现代架构常退化为按最难阶段永久分配资源的浪费型单体结构。

Method: LEMCA将MSC设计表示为可解释程序，在进化循环中迭代优化；提出兼容MSC的自动控制器合成扩展（如仿真强化学习）来评估设计适应度；并利用大语言模型的语义先验、推理与编码能力，迭代编辑控制器模式、对应的感知-计算资源分配以及模式切换。

Result: 在多种控制基准上，LEMCA持续发现超越单体设计帕累托前沿的策略，通过在“简单”任务阶段回收浪费的资源实现更优性能与效率权衡。

Conclusion: LEMCA提供了一种自动化、低人力投入的途径，用于合成资源高效的模式切换控制架构，表明LLM引导的程序进化可有效解决MSC设计难题。

Abstract: Physical control tasks in the natural world, such as driving or object manipulation, frequently exhibit dramatic variations in sensory and compute complexity over time. Correspondingly, a natural resource-efficient choice for robot control is to dynamically switch between control modes with varying resource allocations. However, such "mode-switching controllers" (MSCs) have historically required laborious, expert-driven design and synthesis for each new task. Driven by these design difficulties, modern robotic control architectures often fall back to a wasteful "monolithic" one-size-fits-all structure, where resource allocation is permanently anchored to the hardest, most resource-intensive task phases. To facilitate the design of performant yet efficient MSCs, we propose LLM-Guided synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures (LEMCA). LEMCA represents MSC designs as interpretable programs to be iteratively refined in an evolutionary loop. To evaluate design fitness, we propose MSC-compatible extensions of automated controller synthesis approaches, such as reinforcement learning in simulation. LEMCA then leverages the semantic priors, reasoning, and coding capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to iteratively edit controller modes, their corresponding sensory-compute resource allocations, and mode transitions. Our experiments across diverse control benchmarks show that LEMCA consistently discovers strategies that surpass the Pareto frontier of monolithic designs by reclaiming wasted resources during "easy" task phases. LEMCA thus presents an automated, low-effort path to synthesize resource-efficient MSC designs.

</details>


### [69] [NaViRrator: Robot Navigation from Human-Readable Maps through a Learned Visual Route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21316)
*Ayun Lee,Jiseon Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NaViRRator 框架，将人类可读地图上的起点和终点转换为导航指令，以驱动预训练 VLN 策略；核心 RouteScribe 通过 SGL-CFM 生成显式路线脚手架，再由 VLM 转写为指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可读地图便于直观指定机器人目的地，但其示意几何与机器人第一视角观测难以直接对应。目标是在不重训预训练 VLN 策略的情况下，把地图上的起止位置转化为可执行的导航指令。

Method: NaViRRator 将地图到语言模块与导航执行解耦。核心 RouteScribe 先在地图图像坐标中生成显式路线脚手架，再交由预训练 VLM 转为导航指令；脚手架由 start-goal line conditional flow matching (SGL-CFM) 构建，将直线起终点路点序列变形为地图条件路线。执行时 VLN 策略仅接收指令和第一视角观测，地图与脚手架保留在上游，因此可替换执行器而无需重训地图到语言模块。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，该方法在成功率和 SPL 上优于直接地图到指令生成、基于 A* 的脚手架以及高斯源条件流匹配。定性结果显示，其显著转弯更清晰，并更好地保持预期操作序列。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以路线为基的语言可作为人类可读地图与预训练导航策略之间的模块化接口。

Abstract: Human-readable maps provide an intuitive interface for specifying robot destinations, but connecting their schematic geometry to egocentric observations remains challenging. We present NaViRRator, a framework that translates user-specified start and goal locations on such maps into navigation instructions for a pretrained vision-and-language navigation (VLN) policy. Its core method, RouteScribe, separates route inference from verbalization by first generating an explicit route scaffold in map-image coordinates, which a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) converts into a navigation instruction. We construct the scaffold with start--goal line conditional flow matching (SGL-CFM), which deforms a straight start--goal waypoint sequence into a map-conditioned route. During execution, the VLN policy receives only the instruction and egocentric observations, while the map and scaffold remain upstream, allowing executor replacement without retraining the map-to-language modules. Real-world experiments show higher success rates and success weighted by path length (SPL) than direct map-to-instruction generation, A*-based scaffolding, and Gaussian-source conditional flow matching. Qualitative results further show clearer salient turns and better preservation of the intended maneuver sequence, supporting route-grounded language as a modular interface between human-readable maps and pretrained navigation policies.

</details>


### [70] [Stability-aware Residual Reinforcement Learning Framework for Robotic Manipulator Disturbance Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21307)
*Jihong Kim,Joonhyuk Kwon,Hwa Soo Kim,TaeWon Seo,Hyung-Tae Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种残差强化学习DOB框架，将解析观测器与RL策略结合，并用ISS导出状态相关动作约束，在6自由度机械臂上实现扰动估计与跟踪精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器和扰动观测器虽用于机械臂精密跟踪，但受参数不确定性、非线性摩擦和复合扰动影响，难以建模与补偿全部残差。

Method: 提出解析观测器+RL策略的残差DOB框架：确定性基线负责可靠区域，RL策略补偿模型未捕获残差；估计器网络将观测历史与特权扰动上下文对齐，按扰动状态组织隐空间以快速适应扰动切换；基于输入到状态稳定性(ISS)分析推导状态相关动作上界并约束RL策略，使闭环跟踪误差在任意策略输出下限制于认证包络内。

Result: 在6自由度机械臂实验中，扰动估计与跟踪性能均持续提升；真实硬件零样本sim-to-real迁移下跟踪误差降低27.8%，在训练未见的基座振动扰动下降低38.0%。

Conclusion: 该残差RL DOB框架结合解析模型与学习补偿，并通过ISS动作约束提供稳定性保证，展现出对未训练扰动的泛化能力和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Although conventional controllers and disturbance observers (DOBs) are the standard for precision tracking in manipulators, they suffer from parameter uncertainty, nonlinear friction, and compound disturbances. This study proposes a residual reinforcement learning DOB framework that pairs an analytical observer with an RL policy. The deterministic baseline operates within a reliable region, whereas the RL policy explicitly targets the residuals that the model cannot capture. To make this compensation disturbance-aware, an estimator network aligns the observation history with a privileged disturbance context, organizing the latent space by disturbance regime and enabling rapid adaptation across disturbance transitions. To guarantee stability, we derived and enforced a state-dependent action bound on the RL policy from an input-to-state stability (ISS) analysis such that the closed loop provably confines the tracking error to a certified envelope for arbitrary policy outputs. Experiments on a 6-DOF manipulator demonstrated consistent improvements in disturbance estimation and tracking, including a 27.8% tracking-error reduction on real hardware under zero-shot sim-to-real transfer and a 38.0% reduction under a base-vibration disturbance that was not observed during training.

</details>


### [71] [LOInK: Learned Optimal Inverse Kinematics via Structured Neural Surrogate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21275)
*Michael Somerfield,Damian Abood,Ruigang Wang,Ian R. Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LOInK，一种学习近最优逆运动学解的方法：通过双Lipschitz可逆映射将构型空间映射到解耦的任务/潜空间，并将代价最小解置于原点，从而用基于算子分裂的网络反演高效采样近优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆运动学通常需要在满足任务约束的同时优化给定代价函数，传统约束优化可能较慢且计算昂贵；现有生成式方法往往需要大量采样并评估候选解。因此，作者希望学习一种能直接、高效生成近优解的表示。

Method: 在由采样构型、关联任务变量及给定代价函数组成的数据上训练，学习从构型空间到解耦任务/潜空间的双Lipschitz可逆映射；该潜空间被结构化，使代价最小化解位于原点；进而通过基于算子分裂的网络反演算法高效采样代价最小化解。

Result: 在三个问题上验证：3自由度机械臂示例；四足攀爬机器人上生成近最优解，平均比约束优化方法快31倍，最高快100倍；模拟软体驱动器纯数据驱动示例中，LOInK能显式生成高质量解，而现有生成式方法需要多样采样和候选解评估。

Conclusion: LOInK能够高效生成近最优逆运动学解，在机器人及软体驱动器等任务中优于约束优化和现有生成式方法，尤其适用于需要快速获得高质量解的场景。

Abstract: We introduce Learned Optimal Inverse Kinematics (LOInK), a method to generate approximately optimal solutions to an inverse kinematics problem.
