When trained on data consisting of sampled configurations and associated task variables and a given cost function, LOInK learns a bi-Lipschitz invertible mapping from configuration space to a decoupled task/latent space, and moreover, the latent space is structured so as to place cost-minimizing solutions at the origin. This enables efficient sampling of cost-minimizing solutions via a network-inversion algorithm based on operator splitting. We demonstrate the proposed approach on three problems: an illustrative three degree-of-freedom manipulator problem; a quadrupedal climbing robot for which LOInK can generate near-optimal solutions on average 31 times faster and up to 100 times faster than a constrained optimization approach; and a simulated soft actuator as a purely data-driven example, in which LOInK can explicitly generate high-quality solutions, unlike existing generative approaches that require diverse sampling and evaluation of candidate solutions.

</details>


### [72] [VLA-Scope: Shift-Aware Failure Prediction for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21246)
*Kaiwen Zhu,Dongfang Liu,Liangkai Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VLA-Scope 两阶段框架，结合输入分布偏移检测、偏移类别分类与执行历史，在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的分布外（OOD）执行过程中预测失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型将视觉观察和自然语言指令映射为机器人动作，但分布偏移会损害其可靠性；由于模型在 OOD 条件下仍可能成功，仅检测 OOD 输入不足以预测执行失败。

Method: VLA-Scope 分为两阶段：第一阶段利用池化的图像与语言表示检测 OOD 输入并分类其偏移类别；第二阶段针对被标记为 OOD 的输入，结合预测的偏移类别、动作前缀特征和执行进度特征，使用跨偏移类别共享的逻辑回归模型随执行过程更新失败风险。在 OpenVLA 和十个 LIBERO-Spatial 任务上采用留一组交叉验证进行评估。

Result: OOD 检测达到 ROC-AUC 0.9454，偏移分类准确率为 91%；在全部 1,400 条 OOD rollout 上独立于 OOD 门控评估时，执行 60 个动作后失败预测 ROC-AUC 为 0.8497，而无执行进度特征时为 0.7906；同时高于所评估的 ActProbe 和 SAFE-MLP 基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将动作特征与时间聚合的执行步骤表示相结合，可改善输入偏移条件下的执行失败预测。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and natural-language instructions to robotic actions, but distribution shifts can compromise their reliability. Because these models may still succeed under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions, detecting OOD inputs alone is insufficient to predict execution failure. In this paper, we introduce VLA-Scope, a two-stage framework that combines input-shift characterization with execution history to predict failure during OOD rollouts. The first stage uses pooled image and language representations to detect OOD inputs and classify their shift categories. For inputs flagged as OOD, the second stage combines the predicted category, action-prefix features, and execution progress features. A logistic regression model shared across shift categories updates failure risk as execution proceeds. We evaluate the framework with OpenVLA on ten LIBERO-Spatial tasks using leave-one-group-out cross-validation. OOD detection achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.9454, and shift classification achieves 91% accuracy. Evaluated independently of the OOD gate on all 1,400 OOD rollouts, the failure predictor achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.8497 after 60 executed actions, compared with 0.7906 without execution progress features. It also achieves a higher ROC-AUC than the evaluated ActProbe and SAFE-MLP baselines. These results suggest that combining action features with temporally aggregated execution step representations improves failure prediction under input shifts.

</details>


### [73] [KnowDemo: Knowledge-Guided Robot Demonstration Generation from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21229)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Yanxiang Zhan,Mohammad Khoshnazar,Jeroen Schäfer,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KnowDemo，利用人类视频中的结构化操作知识生成多样化机器人演示，通过VLM区分任务需求与演示特定选择，在目标场景中生成并筛选候选，实现多模态行为与sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略学习需大量演示数据，实机采集成本高；现有从人类视频生成演示的方法受限于运动参考适配，行为多样性不足，且对任务需求与场景关系理解不足导致无效候选、生成效率低。

Method: 构建基于VLM的知识提取与推理模块，将物体与动作描述关联到推断的任务条件、演示参考和允许的执行变体，区分任务需求与演示特定选择；将知识解析到目标场景实体与几何，引导候选生成与筛选，再进行运动规划与仿真验证。

Result: 生成演示通过替代接触策略和有效子任务顺序展现多模态行为，并带有结构化执行标签；实验显示相比仅参考配置获得额外验证执行模式，任务引导抓取采样提升候选规划成功率；在仿真数据上微调π0.5模型，在三项任务上实现sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: KnowDemo能有效利用人类视频中的结构化操作知识生成多样化且可执行的机器人演示，提升演示生成效率与策略学习效果，并支持跨任务仿真到真实迁移。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation policies typically requires substantial demonstration data, which are costly to collect on real robots. Recent methods generate robot demonstrations from human videos by adapting recovered motion and validating the resulting trajectories in simulation. However, methods centered on motion-reference adaptation can limit behavioral diversity by retaining the demonstrated contact strategies and subtask orders, while insufficient understanding of task requirements and scene relations can reduce demonstration generation efficiency by generating invalid candidates. To address these limitations, we propose KnowDemo, a framework that uses structured manipulation knowledge from human videos to generate diverse robot demonstrations for a target workspace. To distinguish task requirements from demonstration-specific choices, we develop a knowledge extraction and reasoning module based on a vision-language model (VLM) that associates object and action descriptions with inferred task conditions, demonstration references, and permissible execution variations. To translate this knowledge into executable demonstrations, we resolve the descriptions against target-scene entities and geometry to guide candidate generation and screening before motion planning and simulation. The resulting demonstrations exhibit multimodal behavior through alternative contact strategies and valid subtask orders, with structured execution labels. Experiments demonstrate additional verified execution modes beyond a reference-only configuration and improved candidate planning success through task-guided grasp sampling. To validate the generated data for policy learning, we fine-tune the pretrained $\pi_{0.5}$ model on simulation data, achieving sim-to-real transfer across three tasks. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [74] [FOCAL-VLA: Subtask-Guided Geometry Distillation and Implicit World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21228)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Di Wen,Yanxiang Zhan,Mohammad Khoshnazar,Jeroen Schäfer,Kunyu Peng,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FOCAL-VLA 通过子任务引导的几何蒸馏与隐式世界建模，联合学习当前空间结构与未来交互动态，从而提升 VLA 模型在精细和长时程机器人操作中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型通常直接将当前 2D 观测映射为动作，缺乏足够的空间与时间理解，难以完成精细和长时程操作。近期方法虽引入几何监督和未来状态预测，但往往利用全场景信息，存在冗余，反而干扰模型学习与当前交互相关的几何和动态。

Method: 提出 FOCAL-VLA 框架：一方面，从 VGGT 向 VLA 模型蒸馏几何知识，通过将几何 latent 与子任务相关图像区域的特征对齐，使几何学习聚焦当前子任务；另一方面，引入隐式世界建模，利用当前与未来演示帧的 Track4World 特征，捕捉当前交互的未来 3D 演化。两种互补表征联合指导动作生成，且推理阶段无需运行 VGGT 或 Track4World。

Result: 实验表明，FOCAL-VLA 在仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: FOCAL-VLA 通过子任务聚焦的几何蒸馏与隐式世界建模，有效学习当前空间结构和未来交互动态，为提升 VLA 模型的精细与长时程操作能力提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models built on pretrained vision-language models have demonstrated strong performance across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, VLA models that directly map current 2D observations to actions often lack sufficient spatial and temporal understanding, limiting their performance in precise and long-horizon manipulation. Recent methods enhance VLA models through geometric supervision and future-state prediction across the entire scene. However, these methods can suffer from redundant scene information, distracting the model from learning the geometry and dynamics relevant to the current interaction. To address this issue, we propose FOCAL-VLA, a framework that combines subtask-guided geometry distillation with implicit world modeling to learn representations of current spatial structure and future interaction dynamics. To focus geometric learning on the current subtask, we transfer geometric knowledge from VGGT to the VLA model by aligning geometry latents with features from subtask-relevant image regions. To capture the future 3D evolution of the current interaction, we incorporate implicit world modeling using Track4World features from current and future demonstration frames. The two complementary representations jointly guide action generation without running VGGT or Track4World at inference time. Experiments show that FOCAL-VLA outperforms baselines on both simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [AirSplan: Risk-Aware Motion Planning for Quadrotors in Cluttered 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21226)
*Seth Isaacson,William Hong,Katherine A. Skinner,Ram Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirSplan 系统结合归一化 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景表示与基于可达性的运动规划，利用四旋翼微分平坦性生成连续时间碰撞约束，在复杂场景中实现高成功率避障路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在农业、基础设施巡检与维护等应用中需在复杂场景中严格无碰撞飞行；空中碰撞往往导致机器人损毁。这带来两个挑战：在无真值障碍数据时高保真表示环境，以及规划器需基于该表示生成无碰撞路径。

Method: 提出 AirSplan：采用 3D Gaussian Splatting 的归一化变体编码高保真场景几何；设计新颖的基于可达性的运动规划器，利用四旋翼微分平坦性计算连续时间碰撞约束，并对机器人占位进行紧致过近似。

Result: 在挑战性测试案例中，AirSplan 成功找到路径的比例为 81.2%，显著高于最接近基线方法的 51.2%。

Conclusion: AirSplan 有效应对复杂环境表示与无碰撞运动规划这对互补挑战，相比基线方法显著提升复杂场景下的规划成功率。

Abstract: Quadrotors are increasingly deployed in applications such as agriculture, infrastructure inspection, and maintenance. In each of these applications, the robot must navigate complex scene geometry while remaining strictly collision-free. Unlike in ground domains, even minor collisions for aerial vehicles can result in the loss of the robot. This safety requirement induces a pair of technical challenges. First, the environment must be represented with sufficient fidelity to encode complex structure, even when no ground-truth obstacle data is available. Second, a motion planner must leverage this representation to determine a collision-free path to the goal. This paper proposes a system that addresses these complementary challenges. The proposed method, AirSplan, adopts a normalized variant of 3D Gaussian Splatting that encodes high-fidelity scene geometry. It then applies a novel reachability-based motion planner that leverages the differential flatness of quadrotors to compute continuous-time collision constraints that tightly overapproximate the robot's occupancy. Experiments demonstrate that AirSplan successfully finds a path in 81.2% of challenging test cases, a significant improvement over the nearest baseline method's 51.2%.

</details>


### [76] [SafeStage: Evaluating Safety Before, During, and After Vision-Language-Conditioned Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21223)
*Jinzhu Luo,Qi Zhang,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeStage 是一个按任务生命周期分阶段评估机器人操作安全性的基准，能定位任务成功之外的安全违规发生在何时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多关注任务成功率、孤立物理约束、语义拒绝或已发生的物理损伤，难以揭示闭环操作中安全失败的具体环节。

Method: 提出 SafeStage 生命周期结构化基准，包含 97 个风险场景，划分为初始状态危险、执行期安全、最终状态危险三阶段；采用基于事件与基于状态的检查，并将原生任务成功与阶段特定安全结果独立报告；在统一闭环协议下评估代表性直接动作 VLA 策略和基于世界模型的策略。

Result: 名义任务完成常与安全违规并存，且不同策略在三阶段表现出不同的失败画像。

Conclusion: 通过分离任务成功与安全并定位违规发生阶段，SafeStage 为评估和改进视觉-语言条件机器人操作策略提供了统一诊断测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language-conditioned robot policies integrate perception, language understanding, and control for general-purpose manipulation. However, existing evaluations often focus on task success, isolated physical constraints, semantic refusal, or realized physical damage, providing limited insight into where safety fails during closed-loop manipulation. We introduce SafeStage, a lifecycle-structured benchmark for evaluating manipulation safety before, during, and after task execution. SafeStage contains 97 purpose-built risk scenarios organized into three stages. Initial-State Hazards captures safety-relevant relations that must be resolved before manipulating the target. Execution-Time Safety evaluates unsafe contacts, trajectories, region entries, and object interactions during execution. Final-State Hazards capture unstable or otherwise unsafe conditions remaining after nominal task completion. The benchmark evaluates realized interactions using event-based and state-based checks and reports native task success independently from stage-specific safety outcomes. We evaluate representative direct-action Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies and policies with world-model-based policies under a common closed-loop protocol. Our results demonstrate that nominal task completion frequently coexists with safety violations and that different policies exhibit distinct failure profiles across the three stages. By separating task success from safety and localizing when violations occur, SafeStage provides a unified diagnostic testbed for evaluating and improving vision-language-conditioned robot manipulation policies.

</details>


### [77] [Safe Real-Time Policy Steering via Noise-Space Trajectory Optimization for One-Step Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21220)
*Qingyi Chen,Joseph Ruan,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INSPO 将一步生成式机器人策略的推理时引导建模为策略输入噪声空间中的轨迹优化，在不直接修改动作的情况下满足避碰、朝向保持等部署约束，并实现低运行时的在线控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人策略虽能表达多模态行为，但预训练策略难以直接适应部署时约束，如碰撞避免与朝向维持；现有推理时引导方法依赖迭代扩散或流过程的梯度引导，实时控制中计算开销较高。

Method: INSPO 针对一步生成策略，在策略输入噪声空间中进行轨迹优化：优化输入噪声，并在其诱导的状态轨迹上评估约束，从而搜索策略诱导的行为空间而不直接修改生成动作；同时引入正则项使解保持与策略输入分布一致，并采用基于种群的粒子优化在线求解。

Result: 在 Push-T、Can pick-and-place 和 LIBERO-Spatial 任务上，针对基于状态与图像的任务专用策略以及通用视觉-语言-动作策略进行评估；INSPO 相比 best-of-N 采样和动作投影提升了任务成功率与约束满足率，并与梯度引导生成方法相比具有竞争力且运行时间更低。

Conclusion: INSPO 为一步生成式机器人策略提供了一种高效、实时的推理时引导框架，可在不修改动作生成器的情况下增强部署约束满足能力，并适用于多种任务专用与通用策略。

Abstract: Generative robot policies can represent diverse, multimodal behaviors, but adapting pretrained policies to deployment-time constraints such as collision avoidance and orientation maintenance remains challenging. Existing inference-time steering methods typically apply gradient guidance through iterative diffusion or flow processes, which can be computationally expensive for real-time control. We propose INSPO, which formulates inference-time steering of one-step generative policies as trajectory optimization in the policy's input noise space. By optimizing the input noise while evaluating constraints on the induced state trajectory, INSPO searches the policy-induced behavior space without directly modifying generated actions. The optimization includes a regularization term that encourages solutions to remain consistent with the policy's input distribution and is solved online using population-based particle optimization. We evaluate INSPO on state- and image-based task-specific policies and generalist vision-language-action policies across Push-T, Can pick-and-place, and LIBERO-Spatial. INSPO improves task success and constraint satisfaction over best-of-N sampling and action projection, while comparing favorably with gradient-guided generation at lower runtime.

</details>


### [78] [Fewer Steps, Better Actions: Rethinking Flow-Matching Inference for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21216)
*Zhipeng Tang,Xinda Chen,Weining Rao,Xiao Li,Wenting Tan,Yuning Wang,Xiao Shi,Xiaofang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Coda，将流匹配 VLA 策略的部分迭代预算重新分配给单次学习的终点校正，从而在冻结策略上提升成功率并降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流匹配的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通过反复评估动作专家生成动作块；增加积分步数会提高推理成本，却不一定提升闭环成功率。因此需要更优的质量-延迟权衡方案。

Method: Coda 保持原策略冻结：先以少步数完成从噪声到动作的轨迹；再用轻量级 Transformer 预测一个由演示监督的残差，输入包括候选动作、源噪声和共享的观测前缀缓存。训练时仅训练校正器。

Result: 在 50 个 RoboTwin Easy 任务上，五步 Coda 将成功率从 71.64% 提升至 74.68%，同时相对于默认十步策略将前向延迟降低 30.2%。两步配置达到 71.88% 成功率，并获得 2.12 倍加速。独立 13 任务对照中，在几乎相同延迟下取得 5.69 个百分点提升。相同设计也提升了冻结的官方 SmolVLA，将两步成功率从 60.8% 提高到 69.4%。

Conclusion: 终点校正能改善冻结流匹配策略的质量-延迟权衡，是替代增加积分步数的有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies based on flow matching generate action chunks through repeated evaluations of an action expert. Increasing the number of integration steps raises inference cost, but does not necessarily improve closed-loop success. We propose Coda, which reallocates part of this integration budget to a single learned endpoint correction. A frozen policy first completes a few-step noise-to-action trajectory; a lightweight Transformer then predicts a demonstration-supervised residual using the candidate action, source noise, and shared observation-prefix cache. Only the corrector is trained. On 50 RoboTwin Easy tasks, five-step Coda improves success from 71.64% to 74.68% over the matched five-step baseline, while reducing forward latency by 30.2% relative to the default ten-step policy. A two-step configuration achieves 71.88% success with a 2.12$\times$ speedup. An independent 13-task control shows a 5.69-percentage-point gain at nearly equal latency, supporting correction as an effective alternative to additional integration. The same design also improves frozen official SmolVLA, raising two-step success from 60.8% to 69.4%. These results show that endpoint correction improves the quality-latency trade-off of frozen flow-matching policies.

</details>


### [79] [Visual Navigation Transformer with Pose Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21212)
*Beiming Li,Jaime Romero,Jonathan Diller,Vijay Kumar,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VNT-PA：以相机位姿索引深度关键帧集合作为上下文，并让注意力依赖位姿差而非时间顺序的 Transformer 导航规划器，在 HM3D 点目标导航上取得高成功率与 SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习式导航策略将观测作为按时间排序的历史，位置编码把观测绑定到被看到的时间，导致难以复用先前经过同一环境时获得的经验；复用经验的系统通常又依赖显式地图或拓扑图等表示并基于其规划。

Method: VNT-PA 使用一组由相机位姿索引的深度关键帧作为 Transformer 规划器的上下文，以相机位姿作为位置编码，使注意力取决于关键帧之间的位姿差而非时间顺序；模型通过模仿在真实场景网格上运行的最短路径规划器进行训练，仅用当前位姿和目标位置查询空间上下文来预测动作。

Result: 在 HM3D 验证场景的点目标导航中，VNT-PA 达到 93.3% 成功率和 90.4% SPL，在导航性能和训练效率上优于将相同上下文编码为时间序列或将位姿作为输入特征的基线；由于空间上下文是位姿索引集合，测试时可融合来自不同轨迹的帧，并且在定位噪声下比基于显式地图规划的传统基线退化更平缓。

Conclusion: 结果表明，带有位姿标记的经验可直接作为学习式规划器的环境表示；让注意力依赖位姿差而非时间顺序，可加速训练并提升长时程导航性能。

Abstract: Learned navigation policies typically consume observations as a temporally ordered history, with positional encodings tying each observation to when it was seen, making it difficult to reuse experience from earlier traversals of an environment. Systems that do reuse such experience usually construct an explicit representation, such as a map or a topological graph, and plan on it. We propose VNT-PA (Visual Navigation Transformer with Pose Attention), a transformer planner whose context is a set of depth keyframes indexed by camera pose. With camera poses as positional encoding, attention depends on the pose differences between keyframes rather than on their temporal order. VNT-PA is trained to imitate a shortest-path planner operating on the ground-truth scene mesh, predicting actions by querying the spatial context with only its current pose and the goal position. On point-goal navigation in HM3D validation scenes, VNT-PA reaches 93.3% success and 90.4% success weighted by path length (SPL), outperforming baselines that encode the same context as a temporal sequence or treat pose as an input feature, in both navigation performance and training efficiency. Because the spatial context is a pose-indexed set, frames from different trajectories can be fused at test time. The planner also degrades more gracefully under localization noise than a conventional baseline which plans on explicit maps. These results show that pose-stamped experience can serve directly as the environment representation for a learned planner, and that making attention depend on pose differences, rather than on temporal order, speeds up training and improves long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [80] [Stochastic Neural Signed Swept Volume for Real-time Chance-Constrained Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21211)
*Qingyi Chen,Kevin Zhang,Lucas Chen,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将机器人扫掠体的符号距离函数学习为概率场，用以量化认知不确定性、纳入感知噪声，并集成到机会约束轨迹优化中，实现无碰撞运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无碰撞运动规划既需要来自感知环境的可靠碰撞模型，也需要对连续轨迹上的状态进行验证。为便于处理，多数规划器仅在连续轨迹的离散状态上、针对单一确定化环境模型做碰撞检测，因而在安全性与计算效率之间存在权衡；近似机器人扫掠体的连续碰撞检测方法计算昂贵或过于保守，而数据驱动方法学到的神经扫掠体模型易受近似误差影响，通常只能充当后续碰撞检测器的粗过滤器。

Method: 将扫掠体的符号距离函数学习为概率场，以量化认知不确定性、融入感知噪声，并最终将其集成到机会约束轨迹优化框架中。

Result: 在传感器噪声显著的高维操作任务上验证了该方法，涵盖仿真实验与真实硬件实验。

Conclusion: 该方法使学习到的扫掠体碰撞模型不再局限于粗过滤器角色，为不确定性感知的无碰撞运动规划提供了可行途径，并支持机会约束轨迹优化。

Abstract: Collision-free motion planning requires reliable collision models from sensed environments and validation of states along a continuous trajectory. To make this tractable, most planners check for collision at discrete states along continuous trajectories against a single determinized model of the environment, introducing a trade-off between safety and computational efficiency. While continuous collision checking approaches that approximate the swept volume of the robot exist, they are computationally expensive or overly conservative. Data-driven approaches can learn the swept volume; however, these neural models are susceptible to approximation errors and are therefore often limited to serving as coarse filters for downstream collision checkers. In this work, we propose to learn a signed distance function of the swept volume as a probabilistic field, enabling quantification of epistemic uncertainty, incorporation of perception noise, and eventual integration into a chance-constrained trajectory optimization framework. We demonstrate our approach on challenging high-dimensional manipulation problems with significant sensor noise, both in simulation and on real hardware.

</details>


### [81] [Robust Structureless Monocular Visual Inertial Initialization Exploiting Line Features and Vanishing Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21186)
*Junwan Choi,Woongrae Jo,Dong-Uk Seo,Jinwoo Jeon,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLIM-init：一种无结构单目VIO初始化器，直接利用2D线特征的几何约束，在退化运动下实现鲁棒且准确的视觉惯性初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO初始化对运动激励敏感，退化运动下常呈病态；现有方法要么依赖限制性激励运动以保证可观测性，要么依赖计算昂贵的3D结构重建，难以高效实用部署。

Method: SLIM-init不显式重建3D路标，而是利用跟踪的2D线特征几何约束：以线导出的消失点作为平移不变朝向线索，在低视差或平移主导等退化场景提供旋转约束；引入线对极残差约束平移，并用线法向投影残差改善线性对齐条件数，从而提升初始状态估计的精度与鲁棒性。

Result: 在公开基准和具有挑战性的自定义退化运动序列上，SLIM-init相比当前最优初始化方法在精度和鲁棒性上均有提升；源码已开源。

Conclusion: SLIM-init通过无结构的2D线特征几何约束，避免了显式3D重建和严格激励运动要求，为退化运动下的单目VIO提供了一种高效、鲁棒且实用的初始化方案。

Abstract: Accurate initialization is essential for reliable visual-inertial odometry (VIO), but it is often ill-conditioned under degenerate motions. Existing methods typically require restrictive excitation motions to ensure sufficient observability or rely on computationally expensive 3D structure reconstruction, limiting efficient and practical deployment. To address these limitations, we propose SLIM-init, a structureless monocular VIO initializer that directly exploits geometric constraints from tracked 2D line features without explicit 3D landmark reconstruction. Specifically, SLIM-init leverages line-derived vanishing points (VPs) as translation-invariant orientation cues to provide robust rotation-only constraints under degenerate scenarios such as low-parallax or translation-dominant motions. It further incorporates a line epipolar residual to constrain translation and a line-normal projection residual to improve the conditioning of linear alignment, enhancing the accuracy and robustness of initial state estimation. Extensive experiments on a public benchmark and challenging custom degenerate-motion sequences demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over state-of-the-art initialization methods. The source code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [82] [When to Waddle: A Comparative Study of Bipedal Torso-Stabilization on Low-Friction Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21185)
*Naomi Oke,Ben Gu,George Ortiz,Stacy Ashlyn,Cordelia Pride,Sarah Bergbreiter,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受企鹅摇摆步态启发，研究低摩擦表面上侧向躯干运动和质心位置对双足运动的影响，发现其效果取决于可用摩擦系数，且可通过企鹅式躯干运动调节前进速度、能耗和滑移相关足部运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低摩擦表面限制 stepping 时的可用接触力，挑战双足行走；因此探索侧向躯干运动和质心（COM）配置如何随摩擦变化影响运动，灵感来自企鹅摇摆步态。

Method: 使用五执行器双足机器人，比较直立步态与企鹅式躯干越过支撑腿策略及多种COM位置，在MuJoCo仿真中扫描正弦腿和髋驱动参数，覆盖四个摩擦系数（μ=0.1、0.3、0.5、0.7），并进行硬件实验验证。

Result: 仿真中，低摩擦下躯干越过支撑腿产生更多成功控制器和更高前进速度，高COM配置速度最高。硬件实验在μ=0.12时趋势相同：躯干越过支撑腿提高前进速度并降低运输成本，两种COM比例下高COM均改善这两项指标，同时保持低侧向足部运动。在μ=0.45时COM趋势反转：低COM配置更快更节能，步态策略对前进速度影响小但仍改变侧向足部运动。

Conclusion: 侧向躯干运动和COM位置的影响取决于可用摩擦；通过企鹅式躯干运动可在硬件上调节前进速度、能量消耗和滑移相关足部运动。

Abstract: Low-friction surfaces challenge bipedal locomotion by limiting the contact forces available during stepping. Inspired by penguin waddling, we investigate how lateral torso motion and center of mass (COM) placement affect locomotion as surface friction changes. Using a five-actuator biped, we compare an upright-gait strategy with a penguin-inspired torso-over-stance-leg strategy across multiple COM placements in simulation and hardware. In the 3-D simulator MuJoCo, we sweep through sinusoidal leg and hip actuation parameters across four friction coefficients mu = 0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7. In simulation, torso-over-stance-leg motion produces more successful controllers and higher forward speeds at low friction, with the highest speed occurring for the high-COM configuration. Hardware experiments show the same low-friction speed trend: at mu=0.12, torso-over-stance-leg motion increases forward speed and reduces cost of transport at both tested COM ratios, and the higher COM also improves both measures. The high-COM penguin configuration is the fastest and most energy efficient while maintaining low sideways foot motion. At mu=0.45, the COM trend reverses: the lower-COM configurations are faster and more energy efficient, while gait strategy has little effect on forward speed but still changes sideways foot motion. These results show that the effects of lateral torso motion and COM placement depend on the available friction, and that forward speed, energy use, and slip-related foot motion can be modulated with a penguin-inspired torso motion on hardware.

</details>


### [83] [OpenRoIS: A Community-Driven Open-Source Middleware Implementing the Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework for Physical Robots and Virtual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21178)
*Sebastian Carrera Villalobos,Christopher Nolan Arellano,Arne Hitzmann,Edilson Morais Brito,Akira Utsumi,Yukiko Horikawa,Takahiro Miyashita,Lotfi El Hafi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenRoIS 是 RoIS 框架 2.0 的社区驱动开源中间件实现，提供平台无关的 HRI 服务，可通过互联网控制物理机器人和虚拟代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HRI 服务应用通常针对特定硬件接口编写，更换硬件需重写应用；RoIS 2.0 标准虽定义了平台无关模型，但缺乏维护的实现、SDK 和适配器，难以实际采用。

Method: 提出 OpenRoIS，具体实现 RoIS 2.0。贡献包括：递归引擎架构（单引擎类实现主/子 HRI 引擎角色）、内部五方法组件契约（区别于五个外部 RoIS 接口）、将接口映射到基于 WebSocket 的 JSON-RPC 2.0、单一数据源类型管道生成三种一致语言栈、TypeScript 和 C# 客户端 SDK（含 Web 与 Unity 支持）、Python 适配器 SDK（含 ROS 2 支持）。

Result: 通过通用 RoIS 接口，服务应用可经互联网访问物理机器人和虚拟代理；所有源码、接口类型和文档以 Apache-2.0 许可开源，并在指定 URL 公开开发。

Conclusion: OpenRoIS 以开放、范式中立的实现将 RoIS 2.0 标准从规范推向实践，促进 HRI 服务的平台无关性和实际采用。

Abstract: Service applications for human-robot interaction are commonly written against the hardware-specific interfaces of one platform, so a change of hardware forces a rewrite of the application. The Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework 2.0, standardized by the Object Management Group (OMG), addresses this fragmentation by defining a platform-independent model in which Service Applications interact with Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) Engines through standardized interfaces and hardware-independent symbolic messages. A specification alone, however, does not provide the maintained implementation, Software Development Kits (SDKs), and adapters needed for practical adoption. This paper presents OpenRoIS, a community-driven open-source middleware providing a concrete implementation of the RoIS Framework 2.0. It takes the position that an openly developed, paradigm-neutral implementation is what carries the standard from specification to practice. OpenRoIS contributes a recursive engine architecture in which a single engine class realizes the main and sub HRI Engine roles, an internal five-method component contract distinct from the five external RoIS interfaces, a mapping of those interfaces onto JSON-RPC 2.0 over WebSocket, a single-source-of-truth type pipeline that generates three consistent language stacks, TypeScript and C# client SDKs that include web and Unity support, and a Python adapter SDK that includes ROS 2 support. Through the common RoIS interfaces, a Service Application can address physical robots and virtual agents over the internet. All source code, interface types, and documentation are released under the Apache-2.0 license and openly developed at this https URL .

</details>


### [84] [MA-LIPP: Cooperative Multi-Agent Load-Aware Informative Path Planning for Heterogeneous Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21167)
*Hojune Kim,Guangyao Shi,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多智能体负载感知信息路径规划（MA-LIPP），通过异步“dead drop”协调异构多机器人团队，将高精度采样与高容量运输分工，解决单机器人LIPP中采样与搬运强耦合导致的覆盖受限问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外机器人任务常需将物理样本送回实验室分析，样本累积会增加负载与 traversal 能耗，使路径规划具有负载感知和顺序依赖。单机器人LIPP中，同一机器人必须搬运所有样本，频繁返回depot，严重限制空间覆盖；异构多机器人团队可分工，但需额外解决何时、何地、交接什么以及向谁交接的协调难题。

Method: 将MA-LIPP建模为精确的混合整数二次规划（MIQP），并针对复杂现实应用提出可扩展的成对大邻域搜索（Pairwise LNS）启发式。该方法允许机器人通过异步“dead drop”投放样本，供另一机器人稍后取回，从而无需同步会合即可协作。

Result: 该启发式在95.5%的认证案例中与精确最优解一致；在最多12个机器人的较大实例上，相比顺序基线，加权后验方差降低16.1%–19.8%。

Conclusion: MA-LIPP为协同物理采样任务提供了鲁棒框架，通过异构分工与异步样本交接，缓解单机器人搬运瓶颈，并提升信息采集效率与空间覆盖能力。

Abstract: Field robotics missions often require physical samples to be returned to laboratories for analysis, making path planning inherently load-aware and order-dependent as accumulated samples increase payload and traversal energy costs. In single-robot Load-Aware Informative Path Planning (LIPP), this rigidly couples sensing with hauling: a solitary robot must transport every collected sample, forcing frequent depot returns that severely restrict its spatial coverage. Heterogeneous multi-robot teams can overcome this bottleneck by dividing labor---enabling high-precision samplers to collect while high-capacity carriers handle transport. However, this introduces a complex coordination challenge regarding when, where, what, and to whom handoffs should occur on top of the LIPP problem. To address this tightly coupled problem, we introduce Multi-Agent LIPP (MA-LIPP), which enables teams to cooperate through asynchronous "dead drops," allowing one robot to deposit samples for another to retrieve later without requiring synchronous rendezvous. We formulate MA-LIPP as an exact Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) alongside a scalable Pairwise Large-Neighborhood Search (LNS) heuristic for complex real-world applications. The heuristic matches exact optima in $95.5\%$ of certified cases and reduces weighted posterior variance by $16.1$--$19.8\%$ relative to a sequential baseline on larger instances of up to 12 robots, providing a robust framework for cooperative physical-sampling missions.

</details>


### [85] [Same World, Different Knowledge: When Isolated Audits Misjudge World-Model Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21155)
*Rui Min,Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Sofiane Lachab,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出面向世界模型的信息接口审计，区分保真度缺口（精确输入变为估计值）与可用性缺口（输入缺失），并用固定权重干预衡量预测误差、输入依赖及配对闭环收益，包括重建引入的依赖。在四旋翼MPC中，修复方案优劣取决于部署时模块是否共享故障信息及信息路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个在孤立输入故障下表现更优的修复方案，当部署模块共享故障信息时可能反而更差。因此需要审计世界模型的信息接口，理解重建等操作如何引入依赖并影响闭环控制。

Method: 提出信息接口审计，区分保真度缺口与可用性缺口；采用固定权重干预测量预测误差、输入依赖和配对闭环收益，涵盖重建引入的依赖。在仿真四旋翼模型预测控制中测试耦合、反号的10%质量/推力标定误差、不确定性训练、风重建、仅重建损坏与共享损坏等设置，并用加速度诊断及扰动观测器分析。

Result: 在仿真四旋翼MPC中，耦合、反号10%质量/推力标定误差使物理锚定模型成功率从69%降至8%；不确定性训练恢复至65%。风重建能恢复控制收益，但继承标定依赖。对于正标定偏移，仅重建损坏时不确定性训练的重建更优，而共享损坏时基线更优。加速度诊断揭示重建偏差与标称模型误差之间的补偿，扰动观测器中也观察到类似现象。

Conclusion: 修复方案的选择取决于部署中使用的信息路径；不能仅依据孤立故障下的表现，还需考虑共享故障信息和重建等引入的依赖对闭环性能的影响。

Abstract: A repair favored under an isolated input fault can be inferior when deployed modules share the faulty information. We introduce an information-interface audit for world models, distinguishing fidelity gaps, where exact inputs become estimates, from availability gaps, where inputs are missing. Fixed-weight interventions measure prediction error, input dependence, and paired closed-loop benefit, including dependencies introduced by reconstruction. In simulated quadrotor model predictive control, coupled, opposite-sign 10% mass/thrust calibration errors reduce a physics-anchored model's success from 69% to 8%; uncertainty training restores 65%. Wind reconstruction recovers control benefit but inherits calibration dependence. For a positive calibration offset, reconstruction-only corruption favors uncertainty-trained reconstruction, whereas shared corruption favors the baseline. Acceleration diagnostics reveal compensation between reconstruction bias and nominal-model error, also observed with a disturbance observer. Repair selection therefore depends on the information paths used in deployment.

</details>


### [86] [Diverse and Adaptable Arm Coordination for Octopus-Crawling via Diffusion-Based Uncertainty-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21138)
*Seung Hyun Kim,Heng-Sheng Chang,Kimia Kazemi,Prashant Mehta,Mattia Gazzola*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散的不确定性感知优化（DUO）算法，为模拟肌肉驱动的CyberOctopus学习无示范爬行控制器，首次将扩散式控制用于接触丰富的软多臂机器人仿真，并表明学习到的协调多样性可促进鲁棒适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 章鱼爬行体现了软体机器人利用冗余的潜力，但如何发现并组织多样协调模式以适应动态物理约束仍具挑战；目标是将运动冗余/电机丰余转化为可实用的适应资源。

Method: DUO将多种运动行为嵌入共享控制分布，通过在线黑箱优化发现并保留多样协调模式；采用对称结构策略表示，将径向等价控制器折叠到规范方向扇区；并提出控制编辑技术，使已有控制器无需重训练即可适应新的执行器约束。

Result: 在接触丰富的仿真中，模拟章鱼能够应对动态物理约束并实现无示范爬行控制；策略可保留多样协调模式，并可通过控制编辑适应新执行器限制，验证了协调多样性对鲁棒适应的促进作用。

Conclusion: 学习到的协调多样性使运动冗余/电机丰余成为软多臂机器人适应的实用资源，为软体多臂机器人的控制与适应提供了新思路。

Abstract: Octopus crawling motivates soft robots that exploit redundancy, yet discovering and organizing diverse coordination modes for adaptation remains challenging. To address this, we introduce a Diffusion-based Uncertainty-aware Optimization (DUO) algorithm that learns demonstration-free crawling controllers for a simulated, muscle-actuated CyberOctopus. This work represents the first application of diffusion-based control to soft multi-arm robots in contact-rich simulations. By embedding a variety of locomotion behaviors within a shared control distribution, this approach enables the simulated octopus to navigate dynamic physical constraints, demonstrating that learned coordination diversity inherently facilitates robust adaptation. The main contributions include: (i) a symmetry-structured policy representation that folds radially equivalent controllers into a canonical directional sector, (ii) an online black-box optimization strategy, the DUO algorithm, that discovers and retains diverse coordination modes, and (iii) a control editing technique that adapts existing controllers to novel actuator constraints without retraining. These results show how learned coordination diversity makes motor abundance a practical resource for adaptation in soft multi-arm robots.

</details>


### [87] [SAGE: Safety-Aligned Gradient Enforcement for Human--Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21130)
*Yisen Li,Hao Zhang,Ruize Geng,Yves Tseng,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多人人机协作中可解释决策与安全控制结合所引发的两类多智能体强化学习不匹配，提出SAGE：以SAIL内化反复激活的安全约束，以TALO校正独立演员更新，从而兼顾安全与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人人机协作要求机器人决策可解释、可审计，同时物理交互必须满足安全约束。将可解释决策树策略与CBF滤波结合虽有前景，但在多智能体强化学习中产生两个学习不匹配：安全投影会改变实际作用于环境的动作；耦合提议图会使独立优化的actor更新与团队级更新不一致。

Method: 提出安全对齐梯度强制执行SAGE。SAIL使用可微有限惩罚提议映射，同时保留精确CBF二次规划用于执行，从而保留约束法向灵敏度，以内化反复激活的安全约束。TALO构造团队感知更新参考，并施加Lyapunov半空间校正，以约束独立actor更新。

Result: 在九个仿真场景中，SAGE成功率达71.0%，每千环境步碰撞步数为0.5；与两个个人形机器人及一名人类伙伴的物理实验验证了部署可行性。消融显示：直接CBF滤波使碰撞频率下降98.5%，但成功率从67.3%降至59.3%；SAIL使提议违规降低48.8%、提议-执行校正降低85.2%；TALO使更新一致性差距降低50.8%。

Conclusion: SAGE通过SAIL与TALO分别缓解安全投影与团队更新不匹配，在保持安全约束的同时提升多人人机协作的任务成功率，并具备物理部署可行性。

Abstract: Multi-party human-robot collaboration poses a dual challenge: robot decisions should remain interpretable and auditable, while executed actions must satisfy safety constraints during physical interaction. Combining explainable decision-tree policies with control-barrier-function (CBF) filtering provides a promising architecture but creates two learning mismatches in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Safety projection changes the action applied to the environment, while the coupled proposal graph can misalign independently optimized actor updates with a team-level update. We present safety-aligned gradient enforcement (SAGE) to address both mismatches. Its shield-annealed internalization layer (SAIL) uses a differentiable finite-penalty proposal map while retaining the exact CBF quadratic program for execution, preserving constraint-normal sensitivity to internalize repeatedly active safety constraints. Team-averaged Lyapunov policy optimization (TALO) constructs a team-aware update reference and applies a Lyapunov half-space correction to regulate independent actor updates. Physical experiments with two humanoid robots and a human partner demonstrate deployment feasibility. Across nine simulation scenarios, SAGE achieves a 71.0% success rate with 0.5 collision steps per thousand environment steps. Ablations show that direct CBF filtering reduces collision frequency by 98.5% but decreases success from 67.3% to 59.3%. SAIL reduces proposal violation by 48.8% and proposal-execution correction by 85.2%, while TALO reduces the update-consistency gap by 50.8%.

</details>


### [88] [MetaPusher: Meta Learning and Planning for Nonprehensile Manipulation of Unseen Objects with Rapid Online Adaption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21122)
*Donghyung Lee,Seyedali Golestaneh,Jaskrit Singh,Zhuoyun Zhong,Athanasios Kapoutsis,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MetaPusher，一种面向未见物体的非抓取式操作的元学习与自适应规划框架，无需物体特定先验交互即可在任务执行中快速适应动力学并持续更新规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未见物体的动力学依赖摩擦、质量分布等无法仅从感知推断的潜在物理属性；跨物体先验估计不确定且在 sim-to-real 中会退化。交互适应虽可改进模型，但更新模型可能使原规划轨迹失效，因此需要快速动力学适应与能融合模型演化的规划策略。

Method: MetaPusher 结合元学习动力学模型与自适应 kinodynamic 规划器。元学习模型在任务执行中利用交互快速适应；规划器通过复用并精化已有搜索树来更新长时域计划。二者耦合使操作与适应无需单独数据收集阶段。

Result: 在仿真和 sim-to-real 场景中对未见物体评估，并与微调、主动学习、MPPI 控制及强化学习策略比较。MetaPusher 取得更低预测误差，任务成功率最高提升 20%。

Conclusion: 将元学习驱动的快速动力学适应与基于搜索树复用的自适应规划相结合，可有效支持未见物体的非抓取式操作，并提升 sim-to-real 下的效率与成功率。

Abstract: Manipulating previously unseen objects remains challenging, as their dynamics depend on latent physical properties, such as friction and mass distribution, that cannot be inferred from perception alone. Prior experience across objects can provide an initial estimate of unseen object dynamics, but this estimate remains uncertain and can degrade further during sim-to-real transfer. Adapting the dynamics through interaction can progressively refine the estimation, however, updating the model may invalidate the planned trajectory. Successful and efficient manipulation therefore requires both rapid dynamics adaptation and a planning strategy that can incorporate this evolution. In this work, we introduce MetaPusher, a meta-learning and adaptive planning framework for nonprehensile manipulation of unseen objects without prior object-specific interactions. A meta-learned dynamics model rapidly adapts from interactions during task execution, while an adaptive kinodynamic planner updates long-horizon plans by reusing and refining its existing search tree. This coupling enables manipulation and adaptation without a separate data collection phase. We evaluate MetaPusher on unseen objects in simulation and in sim-to-real scenarios, comparing against fine-tuning and active learning methods, MPPI-based control, and a reinforcement learning policy. It achieves lower prediction error and improves task success rate by up to 20%.

</details>


### [89] [Noctif3R: Feed-Forward Monocular Real-Time SLAM for Photon-Limited Scenes on Embedded Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21114)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aditya Uday Abhang,Kevin Biju Mathew,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文针对黑暗环境中单目RGB相机在低功耗嵌入式平台实时定位的交叉难题，提出SYS：基于低光前馈点图前端与显式匹配门控的单目管线，并给出Jetson AGX Orin嵌入式执行路径；在极低信噪比下比DROID-SLAM、DPV-SLAM等更少输出无信息轨迹，且精度更高，但覆盖更窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在极暗环境从单目RGB实时定位，现有离线低光重建精度高但太慢，实时单目SLAM在低SNR下退化或失败；作者量化了失败模式：在最低9个暗度等级上，DROID-SLAM 9/9、VGGT-SLAM和CUT3R 8/9、pi^3 7/9、DPV-SLAM 4/9返回全长但无运动信息的轨迹。因此需要兼顾实时、低功耗和低光鲁棒性的管线。

Method: SYS采用低光前馈pointmap前端，并加入显式match gate，返回跟踪轨迹并避免无信息轨迹。嵌入式执行路径：在Jetson AGX Orin上，地图、关键帧与后端运行于384像素，跟踪运行于256像素；同时引入两项逐帧位姿求解修正。评估使用可标定、逐位可复现的噪声阶梯，重标注的真实暗光曝光，以及波士顿动力Spot在暗室录制的视频阶梯。

Result: 在最低9个暗度等级中，SYS只返回3条跟踪轨迹且无无信息轨迹（对比基线无信息轨迹数：DROID-SLAM 9，VGGT-SLAM/CUT3R 8，pi^3 7，DPV-SLAM 4）。在能跟踪处误差最低，为无信息上限的24–47%，最强基线为56–73%；覆盖最窄。真实机器人视频中86.5%的交付帧全黑，SYS所有配置在亮起段后停止，而DROID-SLAM和DPV-SLAM对全部1178帧都输出位姿。嵌入式优化带来可复现Pareto改进：场景1吞吐1.28x、误差0.964x；场景2吞吐1.42x、误差0.68x；峰值GPU内存减少47%，每个位姿能耗减少29%。

Conclusion: 在极暗单目实时定位中，盲目输出全长轨迹会掩盖失效；SYS通过match gate与嵌入式优化，在可跟踪时精度更高、不输出无信息结果，并显著降低GPU内存与能耗，但以覆盖率为代价。其贡献在于低光pointmap前端、显式门控和Jetson实时执行路径。

Abstract: Robots carrying out tasks in dark environments need to localize from a single RGB camera, in light so low that the per-pixel signal approaches the sensor's own noise, on a power-constrained onboard computer, in real time. Each of these constraints has matured pipelines, but the intersection does not. Offline low-light reconstruction now recovers structure below -4 dB but is far too slow to run in real time, while the real-time monocular systems a robot can actually carry (DROID-SLAM, DPV-SLAM, etc.) degrade or fail when SNR gets low. We measured how they fail: across the nine lowest darkness levels of our scenes, DROID-SLAM returns a full-length trajectory carrying no information about the camera's motion on all nine, VGGT-SLAM and CUT3R on eight, pi^3 on seven, and DPV-SLAM on four. We present SYS, a monocular pipeline built on a low-light feed-forward pointmap front end with an explicit match gate, which returns three tracked trajectories and no uninformative ones, at the lowest error of any method where it tracks (24-47% of the no-information ceiling against 56-73% for the strongest baseline), and at the narrowest coverage. On a real robot video take in which 86.5% of delivered frames are entirely black, every configuration of ours stops after the lit beginning, while DROID-SLAM and DPV-SLAM each emit a pose for all 1178 frames. Our method contribution is an embedded execution path for the Jetson AGX Orin: running the map, keyframes and backend at 384 pixels with tracking at 256, together with two fixes to the per-frame pose solve, is a replicated Pareto improvement, 1.28x throughput at 0.964x error on one scene and 1.42x at 0.68x on a second, with 47% less peak GPU memory and 29% less energy per pose. We evaluate on a calibrated, bit-exact regenerable noise ladder, on relabelled real-world dark exposures, and on a new dark-room video ladder recorded from a Boston Dynamics Spot robot.

</details>


### [90] [Demonstration Synthesis from a Single Scan via Gaussian Splatting for Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21112)
*Beichen Wang,Yuen-Hei Yeung,V.R. Sridhar Devarakonda,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianFactory 是一个高保真演示数据引擎，仅需一次视频扫描作为人工输入，且生成回路中无需物理引擎，即可批量合成视觉逼真的机器人操作演示，并用于训练扩散策略实现真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练视觉运动策略需要大量与目标环境高度一致的演示数据，但重新采集成本高昂。现有演示合成方法虽能降低成本，仍受限于人工投入大、视觉保真度不足或过度依赖物理仿真器等问题。

Method: GaussianFactory 将场景重建为可编辑的 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 副本，并从场景支持的对象组合任务中采样。对每个任务，在几何重建上纯运动学地规划抓取与轨迹，渲染与目标环境视觉匹配的逼真演示。物理动力学仅在接触力决定结果时进入流程，即在抓取形成阶段，通过一次在交互数据集上预训练的接触模型引入。

Result: 作者在两种设置中实现端到端 scan-to-deployment 流程：用于可复现性的模拟场景，以及配备真实 UR10e 机器人的真实工作空间。仅使用合成演示训练的标准扩散策略分别达到 95.1% 和 84.2% 的成功率。

Conclusion: 该方法证明，仅凭单次视频扫描且不依赖物理引擎生成回路，也能大规模合成高保真机器人演示，并有效训练视觉运动策略完成模拟与真实环境部署。

Abstract: Training a visuomotor policy calls for abundant demonstrations that closely match the target environment, yet collecting them anew remains expensive. Existing demonstration synthesis methods reduce this cost but remain constrained by high manual effort, limited visual fidelity, or heavy reliance on physics simulators. This paper introduces GaussianFactory, a high-fidelity data engine that mass-produces demonstrations with a single video scan as its only human input and no physics engine in the generation loop. Specifically, GaussianFactory reconstructs the scene as an editable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) replica and samples from the object-combination tasks the scene affords. For each task, it plans grasps and trajectories purely kinematically on the geometric reconstruction, rendering photorealistic demonstrations that visually match the target environment. Physical dynamics enter the pipeline only where contact force interactions dictate the outcome---during grasp formation, via a learned contact model pretrained once on an interaction dataset. To evaluate the downstream utility of the synthesized demonstrations, we implement an end-to-end scan-to-deployment workflow in two setups: a simulated scene that stands in for the real world to enable reproducibility, and a real-world workspace with a physical UR10e robot. In each setup, a standard diffusion policy trained solely on the synthesized demonstrations achieves 95.1% and 84.2% success rates, respectively.

</details>


### [91] [Learning Scene-Aware Humanoid Locomotion through 3D Clutter from Immersive Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21107)
*Beichen Wang,Tong Xu,Daniel Kosukhin,Yuen-Hei Yeung,Yuanjie Lu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出MTC（Moving Through Clutter），一个通过演示学习实现场景感知人形机器人运动的框架，使Unitree G1能在密集杂乱的三维环境中无碰撞穿行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人已能在无障碍空间中学习舞蹈、武术等高动态技能，但在密集杂乱环境中的穿越仍研究不足。此类空间具有三维几何约束，要求全身运动与场景几何紧密耦合以实现避障。

Method: MTC采用程序化生成的虚拟现实环境进行沉浸式数据采集，避免昂贵的物理场景搭建；提出场景感知运动重定向算法，将人类演示转换为人形机器人轨迹，并严格保证机器人与场景的间隙以实现无碰撞穿越；随后用这些参考轨迹训练场景感知运动策略，并部署到Unitree G1人形机器人。

Result: 在提出的MTC-Challenge多障碍穿越任务上评估，策略在多种场景中达到70.2%的无碰撞率，并能通过爬行穿过低净空通道、挤过狭窄缝隙等全身技能穿越复杂环境。

Conclusion: MTC展示了从人类演示学习场景感知全身运动以应对杂乱环境穿越的可行性，为人形机器人在几何受限空间中的避障与运动提供了有效框架。

Abstract: While learning from human motions has enabled highly dynamic humanoid skills such as dancing and martial arts in obstacle-free space, traversal through densely cluttered environments remains underexplored. These spaces are three-dimensional and geometrically constrained, requiring scene-aware locomotion that tightly couples whole-body motion with scene geometry for obstacle avoidance. To address these challenges, we present Moving Through Clutter (MTC), a learning-from-demonstration framework for scene-aware humanoid locomotion. To bypass costly physical scene construction, MTC uses procedurally generated Virtual Reality environments for immersive data collection. To transform these human motions into training-ready humanoid motions, we propose a scene-aware motion retargeting algorithm that converts human demonstrations into humanoid trajectories while strictly enforcing robot-scene clearance to guarantee collision-free traversal. These reference trajectories are then used to train a scene-aware locomotion policy that deploys on a Unitree G1 humanoid. Evaluated on our proposed MTC-Challenge for multi-obstacle traversal, the policy demonstrates a 70.2% collision-free rate across diverse scenarios, successfully traversing complex environments through diverse whole-body skills, including crawling through low-clearance passages and squeezing through narrow gaps.

</details>


### [92] [Dynamics-Induced Commitment in Learning-Based Robotic Penalty Kicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21100)
*Ruize Geng,Hao E. Zhang,Yisen Li,Yikai Wang,H. Eric Tseng,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人罚球博弈中策略信息与身体可执行性的耦合。提出DIC-Map，用身体动力学估计续行能力、定位承诺点，并将交互约化为零和博弈；实验发现承诺约在触球前0.29秒，替换估计器将扑救率从0.240提升至0.472。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人对抗游戏中的学习不仅受战略信息限制，还受限于身体能否执行相应动作；因此需要理解博弈层策略与全身控制层动力学之间的耦合。

Method: 构建人形-四足罚球层级系统：博弈级自博弈策略指挥固定足球全身控制器（S-WBC）；人形射门技能由自采动捕数据初始化，四足扑救技能用强化学习获得。提出动力学诱导承诺映射（DIC-Map），估计续行能力、识别终端替代方案首次持续丧失，并检验剩余交互是否可约化为零和博弈。对对称终端替代方案，约化博弈给出最优策略集中度的闭式界，由响应者的延迟价值决定；进一步表明，当响应者通过估计器行动时，相等响应值会消除直接终端分配梯度，仅保留估计器介导的一阶学习通道。

Result: 实验将承诺定位在触球前约0.29 s；仅改变球速即可移动延迟覆盖范围。在四种响应策略中，替换估计器使扑救率从0.240升至0.472；而通过等待获得相近读取精度增益仅使扑救率升至0.246。由于可用覆盖项是观测代理，均衡比较采用后验分析。

Conclusion: 身体动力学造成的承诺与延迟是机器人对抗学习的关键约束；改进响应估计器比单纯等待更有效，DIC-Map为身体约束下的博弈学习分析提供了工具。

Abstract: Learning in robotic games is constrained not only by strategic information but also by what the body can still execute. We study this coupling in a hierarchical humanoid-quadruped penalty system in which game-level self-play policies command fixed soccer whole-body controllers (S-WBCs). The humanoid shooting skill is initialized from self-collected motion-capture data, whereas the quadruped saving skill is learned by reinforcement learning. We introduce dynamics-induced commitment mapping (DIC-Map), a body-grounded analysis that estimates continuation capability, identifies the first persistent loss of a terminal alternative, and tests whether the remaining interaction admits a reduced zero-sum game. For symmetric terminal alternatives, the reduced game yields a closed-form bound on optimal strategy concentration determined by the responder's value of deferring. We further show that, when the responder acts through an estimator, equal response values eliminate the direct terminal-allocation gradient and leave an estimator-mediated first-order learning channel. Experiments locate commitment about 0.29 s before contact, and changing only ball speed shifts deferral coverage. Across four responder policies, replacing the estimator raises save rate from 0.240 to 0.472, whereas a comparable gain in read accuracy obtained by waiting raises it only to 0.246. Posterior analysis is used for the equilibrium comparison because the available coverage terms are observational proxies. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [93] [Dynamic Modeling and LQR Control of a Single Coaxial Drone with 2DOF Thrust Vectoring Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21099)
*Ali Jokar,Amin Talaeizadeh,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种带二自由度摆式推力矢量机构的共轴旋翼无人机，通过LQR与EKF仿真验证，解决滚转/俯仰欠驱动并降低机械复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轴旋翼无人机因能效高、尺寸小备受关注，但滚转和俯仰存在固有欠驱动；斜盘虽可克服该限制，却带来更高机械复杂度，因此需要更简单的替代方案。

Method: 设计二自由度摆锤机构实现主动推力矢量；建立不忽略机体、舵机臂和电机组件惯量贡献的完整拉格朗日动力学模型；在悬停平衡点线性化后设计LQR控制器；考虑执行器动态和传感器噪声进行高保真仿真；使用EKF融合GPS、气压计和IMU进行状态估计。

Result: 仿真验证了所提架构的可行性，EKF能够高精度融合多传感器估计状态，LQR在考虑执行器动态与传感器噪声条件下仍能有效控制。

Conclusion: 该方法对节能、快速共轴无人机具有潜力和可靠性，并提供了一种比斜盘机械复杂度更低的推力矢量替代方案。

Abstract: Coaxial rotor drones have generated considerable interest because of energy efficiency and small size, but they are afflicted with inherent underactuation for roll and pitch control, although systems like swashplates have circumvented this limitation at the cost of greater mechanical complexity. This work presents a novel coaxial drone supplemented by a two-degreesof-freedom pendulum mechanism for active thrust vectoring that offers a less mechanically complicated alternative. We develop a comprehensive Lagrangian dynamic model that does not ignore the inertial contributions of all the components, including body, servo arms, and motor assembly. A Linear Quadratic Regulator(LQR) is designed based on the linearized dynamics around the hover equilibrium. High-fidelity simulations taking actuator dynamics and sensor noise into account validate the proposed architecture. An Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) blends GPS, barometer, and IMU estimates with high accuracy for state estimation. The findings verify the potential and reliability of this approach for power-saving, rapid coaxial UAVs.

</details>


### [94] [Design of Adaptive PID Controller Based On Asynchronous Advantage Actor Critic Learning Method for QuadCopter Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21082)
*Ali Jokar,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将A3C强化学习与PID控制结合用于四旋翼姿态与轨迹跟踪，并利用系统辨识模块预测状态以优化控制策略；仿真表明其参数优化与收敛表现优于A2C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼具有非线性特性和扰动敏感性，传统PID控制器通常不够精细，难以应对复杂控制挑战，因此需要更具自适应性和参数优化能力的控制方法。

Method: 提出A3C-PID控制框架：A3C通过并行智能体和神经网络动态优化PID参数，并引入系统辨识模块预测系统状态以辅助最优控制策略；将该框架与标准A2C模型进行对比。

Result: 仿真结果显示A3C与A2C均能实现精确跟踪，但A3C在损失函数收敛、奖励曲线和损失曲线方面表现明显更好，表明其PID参数优化效果更优。

Conclusion: A3C与PID结合可提升四旋翼控制性能，有效融合强化学习与传统控制，实现更高的适应性和更优的控制效果。

Abstract: Quadcopters offer great utility in many applications, but their nonlinear nature and disturbance sensitivity present great control challenges. Basic PID controllers are generally not sophisticated enough to cope with these complexities. This paper suggests a control system that integrates the Asynchronous Advantage Actor-Critic (A3C) algorithm with a PID controller for quadcopter attitude and trajectory tracking. The A3C controller uses parallel agents to optimize PID parameters dynamically using a neural network. A system identification module for the complementary system makes predictions about system states for optimal control policy. The proposed framework was compared with a standard actor-critic (A2C) model. Simulation results verify that they both track accurately. However, the A3C-based controller converges much more for the loss function, as evidenced by reward figures and loss curves, demonstrating better parameter optimization. This shows that A3C-based approach results in improved performance for the control of quadcopter, effectively integrating reinforcement learning and traditional control to achieve higher adaptability.

</details>


### [95] [PlantShade: Predicting Plant Shadows for Lighting-Aware Robotic Agricultural Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21059)
*Longchao Da,Xiaoou Liu,Xingjian Li,Lirong Xiang,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了涵盖大豆、番茄、甜菜和草莓四种植物、多个生长阶段与复杂观测条件的植物生长与阴影数据集，并提出基于扩散模型且带时间条件控制的生成式阴影模拟方法，以支持农业机器人与植物感知应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物生长和农业生产是国家可持续发展和民生的重要基础，而遮阴是影响植物生长的关键因素。植物阴影模拟具有重要性和内在复杂性，现有研究仍不足，因此需要推动植物感知阴影建模，以提升作物生产力和农业智能化水平。

Method: 首先，构建综合植物生长与阴影数据集，覆盖大豆、番茄、甜菜和草莓四种植物，包含俯视视角、沿圆形轨迹的补充光源、多个生长阶段的动态阴影以及多样观测复杂度。其次，提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟方法，可为未见植物生成逼真阴影，并引入时间条件控制以支持不同时间阶段的灵活阴影模拟。

Result: 所提方法能够为未见过植物生成逼真阴影，并支持感知、光照控制和视角规划等下游机器人任务。作者通过定量和定性评估验证了模型性能，数据集覆盖多物种、多生长阶段和复杂观测条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为植物感知阴影建模提供了基础性研究，对农业和机器人应用具有广泛意义，并有望通过科学农业提升作物生产力，产生更广泛的社会效益。

Abstract: Plant growth and agricultural production form the foundation of a country's sustainable development and directly impact human livelihoods. Recent advances in frontier artificial intelligence have enabled scientific agriculture with strong potential to improve crop productivity. In this paper, we identify the importance and inherent complexity of plant shade simulation, as shading is a critical factor influencing plant growth. To advance this field and promote broader societal benefits, we focus on two main contributions. First, we introduce a comprehensive plant growth and shade dataset covering four plant species, including soybean, tomato, sugarbeet, and strawberry. The dataset includes top-down viewpoints with a supplementary light along a circular trajectory, casting dynamic shadows across multiple growth stages and diverse observation complexities. Second, we propose generative shade simulation based on diffusion models, enabling realistic shade generation for unseen plants and supporting downstream robotic tasks such as perception, lighting control, and view planning. The model incorporates temporal conditioning to facilitate flexible shade simulation across different time stages. We conduct both quantitative and qualitative evaluations to assess model performance. This work provides a foundational study for plant-aware shade modeling and has meaningful implications for broader agricultural and robotic applications.

</details>


### [96] [Constraint-Unified MPC for Over-Actuated Surface Vehicles with Post-Detection Fault Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21046)
*Sebastian Burmester,Ruiheng Jiang,Noa Sendlhofer,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文报道了一个已部署的自主水面航行器编队系统，将轨迹跟踪、推力分配以及各推力器的力与速率限制统一在一个二次规划中求解，并通过外部检测器给出的每推力器二值故障标志实现故障重构。系统已在苏黎世湖现场演出和威尼斯 Time Space Existence 2025 展览中驱动船队运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编排式水上表演要求小型自主水面航行器在每推力器推力与速率限制下精确跟踪路径，并且还要在推力器发生故障后仍能保持跟踪能力。

Method: 将轨迹跟踪、推力分配和每推力器力/速率限制统一建模为关于每推力器命令的单个二次规划问题；故障重构通过来自外部检测器的每推力器一个二值标志进入该规划。

Result: 现场测试中，10分钟定点保持的均方根位置误差为1.6 cm，以0.6 m/s跟踪10 m正方形的误差为4.3 cm。在0.5 m/s下，前推力器失效使误差增至11.6 cm，右舷推力器失效增至11.5 cm；两个推力器同时失效时，仍能以1.25 m的均方根位置误差跟踪0.4 m/s正方形。与作者自己的级联基线相比，统一公式在标称正方形跟踪上达到相同的几厘米精度，并以约一半的推力器力更紧地定点保持。推力器失效后两种架构出现分离：统一控制器保持在参考路径24 cm以内，而级联控制器偏离路径。

Conclusion: 统一二次规划公式在真实演出场景中有效，能在推力器故障下维持较好的轨迹跟踪与定点性能，并优于级联基线。

Abstract: Choreographed aquatic performances require small autonomous surface vehicles to track precise paths under per-thruster force and rate limits, including after thruster failures. We report a deployed system in which trajectory tracking, thrust allocation, and the per-thruster force and rate limits are resolved in a single quadratic program over the per-thruster commands, with fault reconfiguration entering through one binary flag per thruster from an external detector. The system has driven a fleet in live performances on Lake Zürich and at the Time Space Existence 2025 exhibition in Venice. The field campaign measures 1.6 cm root mean square position error in a 10-minute hold and 4.3 cm over a 10 m square at 0.6 m/s. At 0.5 m/s, losing the front thruster increases the error to 11.6 cm, while losing the starboard-side thruster increases it to 11.5 cm. Losing two thrusters simultaneously leaves the craft tracking a 0.4 m/s square with a root mean square position error of 1.25 m. Compared to our own cascaded baseline, the unified formulation tracks the nominal square to the same few centimeters and holds station more tightly with roughly half the thruster force. The architectures separate after a thruster failure, where the unified controller stays within 24 cm of the reference path while the cascade leaves it.

</details>


### [97] [DEXTERA: From a Single Image to Deployable Dexterous Manipulation via Real-to-Sim-to-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21045)
*Jin Wu,Lianjie Yuan,Zeyan Sun,Yuanyuan Lei,Disi A,Bicheng Han,Fangzhou Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEXTERA 是一个自动化的 real-to-sim-to-real 框架，可将单张 RGB 图像转化为可部署的灵巧操作策略，覆盖场景重建、机器人标定、仿真任务生成与多模态策略学习，并支持零样本真机部署与仿真-真实协同训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界灵巧操作数据采集成本高、耗时长；高保真物理仿真虽可扩展数据合成与策略学习，但手工构建可部署数字孪生仍 labor-intensive，且视觉、几何与动力学残差阻碍可靠的 sim-to-real 迁移。

Method: DEXTERA 包含四个统一阶段：(1) 单图场景分解为静态 Gaussian 背景与可交互刚体/铰接资产，并用 VLM 推断物理参数；(2) 度量场景全局对齐、物体规范化与形态平衡的机器人标定；(3) 可扩展仿真任务原语构建、VR 遥操作与以物体为中心轨迹合成；(4) 共享多模态策略接口，同时支持模仿学习与强化学习。

Result: 在 13 个任务-具身对、2 个灵巧机器人平台和 6 种策略架构上评估。DEXTERA 相比生成基线具有更优视觉保真度与 3D 几何重建，跨域轨迹回放验证了强物理交互一致性；纯仿真训练策略可实现可行的零样本真机部署，仿真-真实协同训练将不同策略架构的平均物理策略成功率从 29.2% 提升至 61.9%。

Conclusion: DEXTERA 实现了从单张 RGB 图像到可部署灵巧操作策略的自动化 real-to-sim-to-real 流程，缓解了真实数据采集与 sim-to-real 迁移瓶颈，并展现出跨任务、跨机器人与跨策略架构的泛化能力。

Abstract: Collecting real-world robot data for dexterous manipulation is costly and time-consuming. While high-fidelity physics simulators enable scalable data synthesis and policy learning, constructing deployment-ready digital twins manually remains labor-intensive, and residual visual, geometric, and dynamics gaps hinder reliable sim-to-real transfer. We present DEXTERA, an automated real-to-sim-to-real framework that transforms a single RGB image into deployable policies for dexterous manipulation across four unified stages: (1) single-image scene factorization into a static Gaussian background and interactive rigid or articulated assets with VLM-inferred physical parameters; (2) metric scene global alignment, object canonicalization, and morphology-balanced robot calibration; (3) scalable simulator task primitive construction, VR teleoperation, and object-centric trajectory synthesis; and (4) a shared multimodal policy interface supporting both imitation learning and reinforcement learning. We evaluate DEXTERA across 13 task-embodiment pairs, 2 dexterous robot platforms, and 6 policy architectures. Experimental results demonstrate that DEXTERA achieves superior visual fidelity and 3D geometric reconstruction compared to generative baselines, while cross-domain trajectory replays validate strong physical interaction consistency. Furthermore, simulation-only trained policies enable viable zero-shot real-robot deployment, while simulation-real co-training substantially improves mean physical policy success from 29.2% to 61.9% across diverse policy architectures.

</details>


### [98] [MAPLE-RF: Efficient Probabilistic RF Source Localization in Partially Explored Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21026)
*Haozhe Lei,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究部分探索地图下的单快照射频发射源定位，比较数字孪生射线追踪方法与 MAPLE-RF。实验表明混合地图覆盖训练对两者都关键；数字孪生多数指标更准，而 MAPLE-RF 精度接近且查询成本极低，优于高斯和高斯混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人应用中，尤其 SLAM 探索时通常只有部分地图，难以获得完整环境模型来预测障碍物对射频信号的遮挡与反射，因此需要能在部分已知地图上进行单快照发射源定位并输出位置后验的方法。

Method: 论文比较两类输出发射源位置后验的方法。第一种扩展数字孪生方法：对每个候选位置进行射线追踪，将未探索空间视为自由空间，并在混合地图覆盖上训练。第二种为 MAPLE-RF：将估计的路径到达角和信噪比编码为网格通道，并与地图已知度、占据状态和视距可见性对齐，使用 U-Net 一次前向并行评分所有候选位置，推理时无需模拟传播。

Result: 室内环境射线追踪仿真表明，混合地图覆盖训练对两种方法都必不可少。数字孪生方法在多数单快照指标上更准确；MAPLE-RF 精度接近，但查询成本不依赖传播模型，且比使用通用射线追踪进行全新全网格查询低两个数量级以上。两者均优于高斯和高斯混合基线；在由自身估计引导的探索路径上，融合后的 MAPLE-RF 后验比对比方法在真实源附近放置了更多概率。

Conclusion: 在部分探索地图下，混合覆盖训练是实现单快照射频发射源定位的关键；MAPLE-RF 以与传播模型解耦的极低查询成本达到接近数字孪生射线追踪的精度，并优于基线，适合机器人 SLAM 探索场景。代码和数据将公开。

Abstract: Localizing a radio-frequency (RF) transmitter from received signals often requires a model of the environment to predict how obstacles block and reflect the signal. In many robotic applications, however, only a partial map is available, particularly when a robot localizes the source while exploring with simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). We study single-snapshot transmitter localization on such partially explored maps and compare two approaches that output a posterior over transmitter locations. The first extends a digital-twin method, which ray-traces every candidate location, to partial maps by treating unexplored space as free and training on mixed map coverage. The second, MAPLE-RF, encodes estimated path angles of arrival and signal-to-noise ratios as grid channels aligned with map knownness, occupancy, and line-of-sight visibility, and a U-Net scores all candidate positions in one pass without simulating propagation at inference. Ray-tracing simulations of indoor environments indicate that training on mixed map coverage is essential for both approaches. The digital-twin approach is more accurate on most single-snapshot metrics, while MAPLE-RF comes close at a query cost that does not depend on the propagation model and is more than two orders of magnitude below a fresh full-grid query with general-purpose ray tracing. Both outperform Gaussian and Gaussian-mixture baselines, and on exploration routes guided by its own estimates, fused MAPLE-RF posteriors place more probability near the source than the compared methods. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [99] [Adapting Rigid-Body Dynamics Derivatives for Constraint Embedding Closed-Chain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21024)
*Daniel J. Volpi,Patrick M. Wensing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文将刚体动力学一阶导数算法推广到基于约束嵌入建模的闭链运动系统，并去除关节速度效应局部构型不变的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效动力学导数方法多适用于开链或约束嵌入模型，但假设关节速度效应在局部构型下不变；该假设对约束嵌入闭链及更一般关节类型不成立，而模型预测控制与可微仿真需要更准确的动力学导数。

Method: 在约束嵌入框架下重新推导刚体动力学一阶导数，显式考虑关节速度效应随构型变化产生的附加项，得到适用于闭链和更一般关节类型的自适应导数算法。

Result: 对比传统销轴关节机器人与包含局部闭链的更完整驱动模型：仅建模驱动运动学时附加项计算开销低；当驱动链包含电机转子等额外刚体或考虑非局部闭环时，计算开销更高。

Conclusion: 该方法能更准确地计算约束嵌入驱动子机构的动力学，并可应用于模型预测控制与可微仿真。

Abstract: This paper extends an existing algorithm for the first-order derivatives of rigid-body dynamics to the case of closed-chain kinematic systems modeled using constraint embed- ding. Many standard dynamics algorithms apply to both open- chain and constraint-embedded models, but existing efficient derivative methods assume joint velocity effects are locally config- uration invariant. We remove this assumption and derive adapted algorithms that extend dynamics derivatives to more general joint types, including those arising in constraint-embedded closed- chain models. Our results compare conventional pin-joint robot models with more complete actuation models that capture local closed chains. We show that the additional terms introduced by these generalizations have low computational impact when mod- eling actuation kinematics alone, but can incur higher cost when additional rigid bodies, such as motor rotors, are included in the actuation chain, or when considering non-local loops. Overall, these results enable more accurate dynamics computations for constraint-embedded actuation submechanisms to be adopted in model-predictive control and differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [100] [Catch Me If You Can: Real-Time Feedback Denoising for Responsive VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21022)
*Yiheng Ji,Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Feedback 提出一种双时间尺度架构，将低频扩散规划与高频视觉反馈结合，在动作执行前利用最新观测对扩散去噪最后一步进行轻量校正，从而在保持扩散策略表达能力的同时提升动态环境中的响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型结合预训练视觉语言模型与扩散式动作生成器，能生成时序连贯的动作块，但动作块通常在推理后以开环方式执行，当物体移动、接触变化或场景演化时缺乏实时响应能力。

Method: 提出 VLA-Feedback，一种双时间尺度架构：低频扩散规划与高频视觉反馈相结合。方法不先完整去噪动作块再执行，而是保留最终去噪步骤作为轻量级反馈接口，使每个动作在执行前可根据最新观测进行校正；无需重新运行完整的视觉-语言扩散模型即可实现实时动作纠正。

Result: 在静态 LIBERO 任务上，VLA-Feedback 与 GR00T 表现相当；在动态仿真任务上，平均成功率从 27.5% 提升至 85.0%；在真实机器人任务上，平均成功率从 51% 提升至 73%。

Conclusion: VLA-Feedback 通过将扩散规划的最终去噪步骤作为反馈接口，有效结合低频规划与高频视觉反馈，在保持扩散规划器表达能力的同时实现实时动作校正，显著提升动态仿真与真实机器人任务的成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by combining semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models with expressive action-generation policies. Diffusion-based action generators are particularly effective for modeling temporally coherent action chunks, but these chunks are typically executed open-loop after inference. This limits responsiveness when objects move, contacts change, or the scene evolves during execution. We propose VLA-Feedback, a two-timescale architecture that combines low-frequency diffusion planning with high-frequency visual feedback. Rather than fully denoising an action chunk before execution, VLA-Feedback retains its final denoising step as a lightweight feedback interface, allowing each action to be corrected using the latest observation before it is executed. This design preserves the expressiveness of the diffusion planner while enabling real-time action correction without rerunning the full vision-language diffusion model. VLA-Feedback matched GR00T on static LIBERO tasks while improving average success on dynamic simulation tasks from 27.5% to 85.0%. On real-robot tasks, it improved average success from 51% to 73%. Additional materials can be found on our project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [101] [Towards Effective Visual-Inertial SLAM with Passive-Only Sensors for Low-Cost Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21015)
*Grant Schwidder,David Widhalm,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明，仅使用总价低于1万美元的消费级传感器与开源AUV，也能在无约束6自由度水下环境中实现可用的VI-SLAM导航，为低成本海洋机器人提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高端AUV依赖DVL、USBL等昂贵传感器，阻碍先进海洋机器人从专业实验室走向更广泛研究与爱好者应用；因此需要验证低成本平台能否实现稳健、高质量导航。

Method: 基于同价位开源AUV，仅配备立体相机、MEMS IMU和深度传感器，在完全无约束的6-DOF水下环境中评估这些消费级传感器，分析现成SLAM软件包，并提出传感器融合优化，以应对非系留水下作业的视觉与物理挑战。

Result: 结果表明，低成本消费级传感器方案能够实现可用的SLAM解，使高质量导航不再受工业级硬件的经济门槛限制。

Conclusion: 低成本VI-SLAM可为大众获取，并为严苛实时海洋任务中的性能预期提供基准，推动先进海洋机器人更广泛应用。

Abstract: Improvements to Visual-Inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for low-cost autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are critical for transitioning advanced marine robotics from specialized labs to broader research and hobbyist applications. While high-end AUVs typically rely on expensive sensor suites - such as Doppler Velocity Logs (DVLs) and Ultra-Short Baseline (USBL) systems - this work demonstrates that robust, high-quality navigation is achievable using a sub-$10, 000(USD) platform equipped only with inexpensive consumer-grade sensors. By leveraging a similarly priced, open-source AUV, we evaluate the performance of stereo cameras, Micro-electromechanical System (MEMS)-based IMUs, and depth sensors in a fully unconstrained 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) underwater environment. We analyze the efficacy of off-the-shelf SLAM packages and propose optimizations for sensor fusion to mitigate the visual and physical challenges of untethered underwater operation. Our results prove that a usable SLAM solution can be accessible to the masses, providing a benchmark for expectations in demanding, real-time maritime missions without the financial barrier of industrial-grade hardware.

</details>


### [102] [SPARROW: Survival-POMCP for Adaptive Robot Routing, Observation, and Waiting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21008)
*Hshmat Sahak,Aoran Jiao,Nicholas Rhinehart,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文将临时障碍下的机器人导航建模为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程，提出基于POMCP的信念空间规划器SPARROW，通过在线学习障碍清除生存模型并权衡信息获取价值，减少到达目标时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临时障碍可能阻塞机器人规划路径，机器人需要顺序决策：等待障碍清除、重新规划路线，或先获取更多障碍信息再行动。该问题具有部分可观测性、障碍清除时间不确定性以及替代路径上可能出现新阻塞等挑战。

Method: 将图导航问题形式化为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程；SPARROW基于Partially Observable Monte Carlo Planning，在信念空间搜索通行、观察和有限时长等待动作；用粒子信念维护潜在障碍类别与清除时间；在线学习类别条件生存模型，同时利用清除观测和机器人改道导致的右删失数据；生成模型模拟动作展开过程中的障碍到达与清除；引入学习价值准则，权衡收集带标签生存数据的即时成本与未来导航后悔的期望减少。

Result: 在两个模拟图和多种障碍类别设置中，SPARROW相比OSCAR将平均到达目标时间降低12-26%；在实体移动机器人上，相比OSCAR降低20.5%，并能随环境条件变化选择性地观察、等待和改道。

Conclusion: SPARROW能有效处理临时障碍下的顺序导航问题，通过信念空间规划、在线生存学习和学习价值权衡，在模拟与真实机器人场景中提升导航效率并适应动态环境。

Abstract: Temporary obstacles that may block a robot's planned route create a sequential navigation problem: a robot must decide whether to wait for a blockage to clear, reroute, or acquire more information about the obstacle before acting. We formulate graph navigation among temporary obstacles as a partially observable semi-Markov decision process and introduce SPARROW, a belief-space planner built on Partially Observable Monte Carlo Planning (POMCP). SPARROW searches over traversal, observation, and finite-duration waiting actions while maintaining a particle belief over latent obstacle classes and clearance times. Class-conditioned survival models are learned online from both clearance observations and right-censored encounters where the robot reroutes before clearance is observed. A generative model simulates obstacle arrivals and clearances as each action unfolds, so the planner can account for blockages that may occur along alternative routes. We further introduce a value-of-learning criterion that trades the immediate cost of collecting labelled survival data against its expected reduction in future navigation regret. Across two simulation graphs and multiple obstacle-class settings, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 12-26% relative to OSCAR, a recent survival-based method for the same problem. On a physical mobile robot, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 20.5% relative to OSCAR while selectively observing, waiting, and rerouting as environment conditions change.

</details>


### [103] [Project SCOUT: Interceptor Drone for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21005)
*Azmain Yousuf,Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Lifeng Zhou,David Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCOUT——一种ROS集成的机载感知与控制框架，用于防御型无人机对入侵无人机的实时空中拦截与周界防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未经授权的无人机快速增多，周界防御亟需鲁棒且抗干扰的反无人机系统。

Method: SCOUT在防御无人机上直接完成视觉检测、目标关联、航迹滤波与控制指令生成，不依赖外部感知设施或地面站计算；感知流程结合TensorRT加速的无人机检测、ByteTrack关联与轻量级航迹保持状态机，以抑制目标跳变并在检测短暂退化时维持目标连续性，减少误检或目标切换导致的不稳定控制；通过硬件部署执行平面“守门”拦截策略。

Result: 真实飞行结果表明，SCOUT在实时机载检测速率下，92.2%的帧保持有效目标检测，并验证了视觉跟踪与闭环控制用于无人机周界防御的可行性。

Conclusion: SCOUT展示了基于机载视觉感知与闭环控制的无人机周界防御方案可行，并提供了端到端防御操作的视频演示。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of unauthorized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has created a growing need for robust, jamming-resistant counter-UAV systems for perimeter defense. This paper presents \textbf{SCOUT} (Spatial Computation for Optimized UAV Tracking), a ROS-integrated onboard perception and control framework for real-time aerial defense against incoming UAVs. SCOUT performs visual detection, target association, track filtering, and control command generation directly onboard the defender UAV, without relying on external sensing infrastructure or ground-station computation. To provide stable control inputs, the perception pipeline combines TensorRT-accelerated drone detection with ByteTrack-based association and a lightweight track-retention state machine. The state machine rejects abrupt target jumps and maintains short-term target continuity during temporary detection degradation, reducing unstable control responses caused by false detections or target switching. We evaluate the proposed architecture through an integrated hardware deployment executing a planar ``goalkeeping'' interception strategy. In this setting, the defender UAV tracks the incoming target and adjusts its motion to maintain a blocking configuration near the protected boundary. Real-world flight results show that SCOUT maintains valid target detections for 92.2\% of frames while operating at real-time onboard detection rates, demonstrating the feasibility of visual tracking and closed-loop control for UAV perimeter defense. A video demonstration of the end-to-end perimeter defense operation is available online. this https URL

</details>


### [104] [Do Spinning Radar Doppler Velocity Measurements Improve Vehicle Detection and Tracking?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21000)
*Eric Xie,Daniil Lisus,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转式FMCW雷达提供的逐方位多普勒速度能否提升车辆检测与跟踪性能。作者构建自动标注流程并在Boreas数据集上验证：多普勒去畸变可提升检测，多普勒速度先验可显著提升跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转FMCW雷达因恶劣天气鲁棒性和360°视野在自动驾驶感知中日益流行，扫描雷达还能输出逐方位多普勒速度；但尚不清楚这些多普勒测量是否真正改善旋转雷达的车辆检测与跟踪，且缺少带动态目标真值标签的多普勒旋转雷达数据集。

Method: 检测方面，估计自运动并校正多普勒引起的雷达图像距离畸变，再输入检测网络。跟踪方面，提出逐车辆速度估计方法，并将其作为跟踪器运动模型的先验。数据方面，使用微调的现成LiDAR检测器集成，对Boreas Road Trip数据集全部643 km进行自动标注，再将检测迁移到雷达，选取超过250 km的车辆密集序列作为真值训练数据，并训练和评估两个SOTA检测器。

Result: 多普勒去畸变可使检测平均精度均值mAP最高提升2.37点；多普勒速度先验相比零速度初始化基线，将多目标跟踪准确率MOTA提升13.68点，并达到使用真值速度作为先验时MOTA的99.7%。

Conclusion: 多普勒速度测量能有效改善旋转FMCW雷达的车辆检测与跟踪；自动标注流程为缺少动态标签的多普勒雷达数据提供了可行训练方案，且所提速度先验接近真值速度先验的跟踪效果。

Abstract: Spinning frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars have been gaining popularity in autonomous vehicle perception on account of their robustness to adverse weather conditions and 360° field of view. Recently, scanning radars have also been shown capable of generating per-azimuth Doppler velocity. In this paper, we investigate whether these Doppler velocity measurements improve spinning radar vehicle detection and tracking performance. For detection, we estimate the ego motion and use it to undo the Doppler range distortion of the radar image before passing it to a network. For tracking, we propose a new way to estimate a per-vehicle velocity and use it as a prior for the tracker's motion model. Since Doppler-enabled spinning radar data is not available in any dataset with ground-truth dynamic object labels, our first contribution is an automatic labelling pipeline that uses an ensemble of fine-tuned off-the-shelf lidar detectors to label all 643 km of the Boreas Road Trip dataset. We then transfer detections to radar, and use over 250 km of vehicle-dense sequences as ground-truth training data. By training and evaluating two state-of-the-art detectors, we show that Doppler undistortion can improve detection accuracy by up to $2.37$ points on mean average precision. Furthermore, we show that the Doppler velocity prior can improve tracking accuracy by $13.68$ points on multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA) versus the zero-velocity initialization baseline, while achieving $99.7\%$ of the MOTA obtained using ground-truth velocities as the prior.

</details>


### [105] [PIVOT: Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability for Field Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20983)
*Aoran Jiao,Wenda Zhao,Hshmat Sahak,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PIVOT：一种物理信息引导的视觉语言越野可通行性导航系统，用VLM语义推理增强传统几何规划，并在约6.4 km混合地形闭环试验中将自主率从59.6%提升至97.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地形评估对越野移动机器人安全可靠导航至关重要。传统基于几何的评估计算快，但在非结构化环境中往往过于保守，限制自主导航能力。

Method: PIVOT将基于几何的规划作为常规模式，仅在几何模式找不到路径时触发基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义重规划。为将语义评估物理化，量化VLM预测的通行能量代价、机器人振动和车轮打滑与真实测量值的相关性，并按各模态预测-测量相关性加权，构建统一可通行性分数。

Result: 在约6.4 km混合地形路线上进行五次重复闭环试验，与仅几何导航相比，系统整体自主率从59.6%提高到97.0%，人工干预次数从11次降至3次，平均干预间隔距离（MDBI）从69.2 m提高到412.9 m。

Conclusion: 物理接地的VLM地形评估可显著扩展自主导航能力，突破仅依赖几何信息的局限，同时保留高效几何规划作为常规模式。

Abstract: Terrain assessment is a critical capability for off-road mobile robots, enabling safe and reliable navigation through unstructured and geometrically complex environments. Conventional geometry-based terrain assessment is fast to compute but often overly conservative in unstructured environments. We present PIVOT: a Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability navigation system that augments conventional geometry-based planning with vision-language-model (VLM)-based semantic reasoning for field robots. To physically ground this assessment, we quantify how strongly the VLM's predicted traversal energy cost, robot vibration, and wheel slip correlate with real-world measurements and introduce a unified traversability score that weights each modality by its prediction-measurement correlation. For efficiency, we design a two-level navigation architecture that retains geometry-based planning as the nominal mode and invokes semantic replanning only when that mode fails to find a path. Across five repeated closed-loop trials on a mixed-terrain route totalling around $6.4$ km, the proposed system increases overall autonomy from $59.6\%$ to $97.0\%$, reduces human interventions from $11$ to $3$, and increases the mean distance between interventions (MDBI) from $69.2$ m to $412.9$ m compared with geometry-only navigation. These results demonstrate that physically grounded VLM-based terrain assessment can substantially extend autonomous navigation beyond the limitations of geometry alone, while preserving efficient geometric planning as the nominal mode.

</details>


### [106] [ForeTac-VLA: A Forecasting-Based Tactile-Vision-Language-Action Model for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20980)
*Zhengyu Tao,Xin Li,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ForeTac-VLA，一种基于未来触觉状态预测的触觉-视觉-语言融合模型，用于提升接触丰富场景下的机器人操作鲁棒性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型依赖视觉感知，在接触丰富环境中关键物理交互状态难以从视觉观测；已有触觉增强VLA方法多为反应式，未显式建模接触如何演变。

Method: 将近期触觉观测编码为时序表示，并通过双向交叉注意力与视觉-语言特征融合；使用基于Transformer的预测模块预测多步未来触觉状态，使模型联合推理已观测与预期接触；将融合多模态表示和预测未来触觉状态输入VLA主干以条件化动作生成；训练中采用从真值到预测的课程学习以稳定早期不可靠预测。

Result: 在四项真实世界接触丰富操作任务中平均成功率达95%，比微调VLA模型高36.25个百分点，比最先进触觉增强VLA基线高22个百分点以上；在低光照和视觉杂乱条件下仍保持强性能。

Conclusion: 显式预测未来触觉状态并融合视觉-语言信息，可增强接触丰富操作中的物理 grounding、鲁棒性与动作生成效果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet their reliance on visual perception limits robustness in contact-rich environments, where critical physical interaction states may not be visually observable. Existing tactile-enhanced VLA methods improve physical grounding using observed tactile feedback, but most remain largely reactive rather than explicitly modeling how contact may evolve. Therefore, we propose ForeTac-VLA, a forecasting-based tactile-vision-language fusion model that predicts future tactile states to guide action generation. Specifically, ForeTac-VLA encodes recent tactile observations into temporal representations and integrates them with vision-language features through bidirectional cross-attention.
