Further, a transformer-based forecasting module predicts multi-step future tactile states, enabling the model to reason jointly over observed and anticipated contact. Finally, the fused multimodal representations and predicted future tactile states are fed into the VLA backbone to condition action generation. To stabilize training, a ground-truth-to-prediction curriculum is employed when early forecasts are unreliable. Across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ForeTac-VLA achieves an average success rate of 95%, outperforming the fine-tuned VLA model by 36.25 percentage points and state-of-the-art tactile-enhanced VLA baselines by over 22 percentage points. ForeTac-VLA also maintains strong performance under low-illumination and visually cluttered conditions. Video demonstrations can be found on this https URL

</details>


### [107] [Shake to Learn: Dynamic Interrogation of Hidden Object Physics for Robotic Manipulation with Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20970)
*Wen Sin Lor,Jun Wang,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出“shake-to-learn”机械询问框架：将折纸启发的软机械臂作为物理储备池，抓取物体后施加固定晃动激励，通过记录耦合系统的振铃动态响应，并用轻量线性读出推断视觉不可见的物体物理属性，如隐藏质心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多与机器人操作相关的物理属性无法从视觉中获得，例如密封物体的质心位置或内部内容物；只有通过提升、摇晃或动态扰动等交互，这些隐藏属性才会显现。因此，需要一种利用交互诱导动态响应进行机器人感知与学习的新模态。

Method: 使用折纸启发的软机械臂作为物理储备池计算机。抓取物体后，在基座施加固定摇晃输入，并通过相机跟踪或嵌入式传感器记录系统的振铃响应。由于输入在多次试验中保持不变，隐藏物体属性会通过其对接机器人-物体耦合动力学的影响被编码。随后用轻量线性读出解码这些动力学，以恢复可解释的隐藏物体物理信息。研究还提出一种振铃响应的动态摘要表示以提高预测精度。

Result: 软机械臂储备池完成了三个难度递增的任务：推断物体隐藏质心的方向、推断质心与抓取点之间的距离、以及利用推断出的质心信息引导后续再抓取。动态摘要表示进一步提高了预测准确率。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，晃动学习式机械询问是一种有前景的策略，能使机器人系统将短暂的物理交互转化为关于隐藏物体属性的可操作线索，从而服务于下游操作任务。

Abstract: Many physical properties relevant to robotic manipulation are hidden from vision. A sealed object, for example, may reveal little about its center of mass (COM) or internal contents until it is lifted, shaken, or otherwise dynamically perturbed. This study shows that such interactions can enable a new modality of robotic perception and learning, in which interaction-induced dynamic responses are used to infer object physics that is inaccessible to conventional sensing. We implement this idea using an origami-inspired soft robotic arm that functions as a physical reservoir computer. After grasping an object, the arm is excited by a fixed shaking input at its base, and the resulting ringdown response is recorded through either camera tracking or embedded sensors. Because the input is held constant across trials, hidden object properties, such as the COM position, are encoded through their effect on the dynamics of the coupled robot-object system. A lightweight linear readout can then decode these dynamics to recover interpretable information about the hidden object physics. Using this framework, the soft robotic arm reservoir completed three tasks of increasing difficulty: inferring the orientation of the object's hidden COM, inferring the COM distance from the grasp point, and using the inferred COM information to guide a subsequent regrasp. We further develop a dynamic summary representation of the ringdown response that improves prediction accuracy. Together, these results establish shake-to-learn mechanical interrogation as a promising strategy for robotic systems to convert brief physical interactions into actionable cues about hidden object properties for downstream manipulation.

</details>


### [108] [AeRove: A Compact Bimodal Aerial-Terrestrial Drone with Rapid Bistable Reconfiguration for Close-Range Pipeline Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20965)
*Caleb Polillio,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双模态空地机器人 AeRove，利用螺旋桨护罩作为轮子在管道上滚动，并以弹簧加载双稳态机构在200 ms内切换地面/飞行模式，从而降低功耗并提升近距离管道检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准无人机在近距离管道检测中面临悬停功耗高、气流敏感、螺旋桨尾流干扰气体传感等问题，因此需要一种兼顾连续地面移动与飞行越障能力的紧凑机器人。

Method: AeRove 将螺旋桨护罩用作车轮沿管道滚动，采用弹簧加载双稳态机构实现地面与飞行模式切换，且无需持续执行器功率维持任一状态；收敛型护罩几何提升推力效率。系统以地面滚动进行连续行进，仅将飞行用于越障。开展51 cm直径钢管自主试验，包括直段/弯段导航、越障和模拟检测标记泄漏检测；另进行CO2传感实验，比较栖停与飞行条件，并对比腹部螺旋桨进气与延长探头配置。

Result: 地面滚动相比持续飞行将电流消耗降低14倍（0.7 A vs. 10 A），估计行程从144 m延长至2057 m；自主试验实现直/弯管道导航、越障和模拟泄漏检测。CO2实验中，栖停检测的浓度响应显著大于悬停；飞行时腹部螺旋桨进气配置借助螺旋桨诱导气流，比延长探头实现更快更强的气体检测响应。

Conclusion: AeRove 验证了双模态空地运动与螺旋桨护罩滚动方案在近距离管道检测中的有效性，可降低功耗、扩展行程并改善气体传感性能；代码与设计以 CC-BY 许可证开源。

Abstract: Close-proximity pipeline inspection is challenging for standard drones due to high hovering power consumption, airflow sensitivity, and propeller wash interference with gas sensing. To address this, we present AeRove, a compact bimodal aerial-terrestrial robot. AeRove uses its propeller guards as wheels to roll along pipes and employs a spring-loaded bistable mechanism to reconfigure between ground and flight modes in 200 ms without continuous actuator power to maintain either state. The converging propeller-guard geometry also improves measured thrust efficiency. By reserving flight for obstacle hopping and using ground rolling for continuous traversal, current draw is reduced 14x compared to continuous flight (0.7 A vs. 10 A), extending estimated travel distance from 144 m to 2057 m. Autonomous trials on a 51 cm diameter steel pipe demonstrated navigation on straight and curved sections, obstacle jumping, and leak detection of simulated inspection markers. Separate CO2 sensing experiments evaluated gas-detection performance under perched and aerial operating conditions. Perched inspection produced a substantially larger concentration response than hovering under the tested conditions. During flight, an underbody propeller-intake configuration produced a faster and stronger gas-detection response than an extended probe by leveraging propeller-induced airflow. Code and designs are released under the CC-BY license.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Square Root Gauss-Newton iLQR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21053)
*Maximilian Haas-Heger,Jur van den Berg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高斯-牛顿（加权最小二乘）结构的平方根 iLQR 算法：利用扩展代价-to-go 矩阵的半正定性，使反向传播每步仅需一次 QR 分解，并直接提取反馈增益与 Cholesky 因子，从而提升数值稳定性并简化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: iLQR 是常用的非线性轨迹优化算法，但传统形式在增广拉格朗日框架中处理约束时，大惩罚项会严重恶化数值条件。已有平方根 iLQR 公式要么数值次优，要么算法复杂，因此需要更有效的平方根实现。

Method: 作者指出关键在于代价函数的高斯-牛顿（加权最小二乘）结构：该结构带来的半正定性不仅适用于 Hessian，也扩展到完整的增广代价-to-go 矩阵。由此设计反向传播，使每一步简化为一次 QR 分解，并直接从分解中提取反馈增益和传播的 Cholesky 因子。

Result: 得到一种算法简单且数值更优的平方根 iLQR：反向传播每步只需一次 QR 分解，直接获得反馈增益与 Cholesky 因子，并改善增广拉格朗日框架下因大惩罚导致的病态问题。

Conclusion: 高斯-牛顿/加权最小二乘结构是实现有效平方根 iLQR 的关键；基于该结构可克服既有平方根公式在数值性能和算法复杂度上的不足，提供更简洁、稳定的非线性轨迹优化方法。

Abstract: The iterative Linear Quadratic Regulator (iLQR) is a widely used algorithm for nonlinear trajectory optimization. At each iteration, it solves a local linear-quadratic approximation of the problem via dynamic programming, propagating a quadratic cost-to-go function. If the Hessian of the cost-to-go approximation is positive-semidefinite, one can derive a square root formulation of iLQR that propagates its Cholesky factor instead. This offers significant numerical advantages - much as square root Kalman filters improve upon their conventional counterparts - particularly when iLQR is used within an augmented Lagrangian framework for handling constraints, where large penalties degrade conditioning. Previous square root formulations of iLQR and related algorithms exist, but they are either numerically suboptimal, algorithmically complex, or both. In this paper, we show that the key to an effective square root formulation lies in the Gauss-Newton (weighted least-squares) structure of the cost function: this yields a positive semidefiniteness property that extends beyond the Hessian to the full augmented cost-to-go matrix, and enables a backward pass of remarkable simplicity in which each step reduces to a single QR-decomposition, from which the feedback gain and propagated Cholesky factor are extracted directly.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Benchmarking World Models for Continual Learning on Compositional Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22055)
*Haoyu Zhou,Joe Watson,Anson Lei,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个面向机器人操作中世界模型的组合式持续学习基准，用于分离并评估“复用先验知识”与“学习新任务”两种能力；实验表明模块化世界模型在复用与遗忘之间平衡更好，但仍未完全解决持续世界模型的核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想的世界模型应能跨任务持续学习，适应新环境且不遗忘已学知识。然而，现有对世界模型适应性的度量混淆了两类能力：学习未见任务的速度与容量，以及对已有知识的复用，因为新任务同时包含新颖内容和可复现机制。因此，需要将知识复用从先验经验中隔离出来进行评估。

Method: 作者提出了一个面向机器人操作中世界模型的组合式持续学习基准。具体地，为每个任务课程设计组合任务，使其结合序列中已见任务的若干方面；并沿动作与感知两个轴对组合进行因子化，以分析不同输入模态如何成为知识复用的瓶颈。评估包括在典型持续学习方法下的最先进世界模型，以及一个其动力学主干包含显式可复用组件的模块化世界模型。

Result: 结果显示，模块化方法在知识复用与遗忘之间取得了优于传统持续学习方法的平衡，但没有任何方法能完全解决该问题。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，构建能够在不遗忘的前提下复用知识的持续世界模型仍有明显研究空间；模块化为平衡复用与遗忘提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: A desirable property of a world model is the ability to learn continually across tasks, adapting to new environments without forgetting what the agent has already learnt. In particular, the ability to retain and reuse knowledge obtained from prior experiences underpins an agent's ability to efficiently adapt to novel environments, as the dynamics of the physical world can often be described in recurring mechanisms. However, the world model's measure of adaptation entangles two abilities: the speed and capacity to learn unseen tasks, and the reuse of knowledge already acquired, since incoming tasks carry novel content alongside what recurs. In order to isolate knowledge reuse from prior experiences, we propose a compositional continual learning benchmark for world models in robot manipulation. Specifically, we design each task curriculum with compositional tasks that combine aspects of the tasks seen in the sequence. We further factorise this composition along the axes of action and perception to better understand how different input modalities bottleneck knowledge reuse. We evaluate state-of-the-art world models under canonical continual learning methods, alongside a modular world model whose dynamics backbone contains explicitly reusable components. Results show that modularity balances reuse against forgetting better than conventional methods, but none solve the problem fully, leaving clear room for continual world models built to reuse without forgetting. More details are available on our project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Kinematics: Benchmarking Simulation Fidelity for Muscle-Driven Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21909)
*Ayah G. Ahmad,Claire E. Borden,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了基于SCONE/HyFyDy与MuJoCo/MyoSim的两条运动模仿强化学习管线，并使用同一套人体运动捕捉与EMG数据评估。两者运动学表现相近，但HyFyDy的肌肉激活更接近实验EMG；两者仍需发展以兼顾生理真实性与GPU并行仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动模仿强化学习管线虽能高保真复现人体运动学，但是否准确捕捉产生运动的神经肌肉行为尚不明确。这对机器人辅助设备设计与控制尤为关键，因为肌肉激活模式和代谢成本常被作为优化目标。

Method: 系统比较两条先进MIRL管线：SCONE/HyFyDy强调详细肌肉肌腱建模与生理真实性，MuJoCo/MyoSim强调计算效率与可扩展策略学习。使用共同的人体运动捕捉和肌电（EMG）测量对二者进行比较。

Result: 两条管线均能以相对较高的精度产生相似运动学；但HyFyDy的肌肉激活与实验EMG更一致，平均pooled (RMSE, r)为(0.164, 0.4)，而MuJoCo为(0.344, 0.11)。

Conclusion: HyFyDy因更先进的生理真实性，目前更适合肌肉骨骼建模；但两条管线均需进一步发展，以将生理真实性引入GPU可并行仿真环境，并推进机器人辅助设备设计。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct a systematic comparison of two state-of-the-art motion-imitation reinforcement learning (MIRL) pipelines, one built on SCONE/HyFyDy and one built on MuJoCo/MyoSim. HyFyDy emphasizes physiological realism through detailed musculotendon modeling, while MuJoCo prioritizes computational efficiency and scalable policy learning. While recent work has demonstrated that both pipelines reproduce human kinematics with high fidelity, it remains unclear if they accurately capture the underlying neuromuscular behavior that produced the movement. This limitation is particularly important for robotic assistive-device design and control, where outcome measures such as muscle activation patterns and metabolic cost are often used as optimization targets. To conduct a systematic comparison, our work compares both pipelines using a common set of human motion-capture and electromyography (EMG) measurements. The results find that while both pipelines produce similar kinematics with relative accuracy, the muscle activations from HyFyDy are more aligned with the experimental EMG, as supported by the average pooled (RMSE, r) values for muscle activations from HyFyDy and MuJoCo: (0.164, 0.4) and (0.344, 0.11), respectively. While we conclude that the more advanced physiological realism of HyFyDy currently makes it more suitable for musculoskeletal modeling, both require further development to bring physiological realism to GPU-parallelizable simulation environments and advance robotic assistive device design.

</details>


### [112] [Signal-Centric Remote Sensing via Alternative Preprocessing and Acoustic Processing for ML-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21123)
*Logan Luna,Sirio Jansen-Sánchez,Ilteris Demirkiran,Leo Ghelarducci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用CSV格式处理声纳数据以替代图像化表示，在遥感应用中实现处理时间降低91.18%，并提升机器学习目标检测准确率及SNR、PSNR等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声纳数据处理主要依赖图像表示，需要在自主系统上对图像数据进行预处理，计算和流程负担较大，因此需要探索替代的数据处理方式。

Method: 提出一种面向遥感应用的替代数据处理方法，直接使用逗号分隔值（CSV）格式的声纳数据，而非将其转换为图像表示后再处理。

Result: 实验表明，该替代方法使处理时间减少91.18%，提升了机器学习目标检测的准确率，并提高了SNR、PSNR及其他评价指标。

Conclusion: 基于CSV的数据表示可作为声纳遥感数据处理的有效替代方案，在效率、目标检测精度和信号质量指标上优于传统图像化处理流程。

Abstract: The dominant method of processing sonar data is using image-based representations, requiring the preprocessing of image data on autonomous systems. We propose an alternative data processing method for remote sensing applications via the use of data in Comma-Seperated Value format. Experimentation on our alternative approach shows a reduction of processing time by 91.18%, an improvement in accurate object detection by Machine Learning, and an increase in SNR (Signal-to-noise ratio), PSNR (Peak signal-to-noise ratio), and other evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [113] [Talk to Me, Jarvis: An Open-Source Edge-Deployable Voice Assistant Framework for Autonomous Racecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21109)
*Daniel Henel,Frederik Werner,Alexander Langmann,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出离线语音助手Jarvis，用于自动驾驶高层行为指令，基于领域微调Mistral 7B实现低延迟、高精度的意图识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线托管大语言模型存在网络依赖和推理延迟波动问题，难以满足时间敏感的自动驾驶应用需求。

Method: 开发Jarvis，将语音识别、语音合成与自然语言命令分类集成到轻量本地框架中；核心是基于领域数据微调Mistral 7B的文本到命令分类器，实现低延迟推理，并提供开源实现。

Result: 意图识别准确率达97.63%，平均处理延迟1.39秒，性能优于更大的在线托管模型。

Conclusion: Jarvis适用于需要快速响应的自动驾驶操作，其开源实现可支持后续研究与微调。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have improved their effectiveness as back-end components for voice assistants, particularly in intent understanding and context-aware input classification. However, online-hosted models introduce network dependency and variable inference latency, limiting their suitability for time-critical autonomous driving applications. In this work, we address these issues by developing Jarvis, an offline voice assistant for high-level behavioral commands of autonomous vehicles. Its architecture integrates speech recognition and synthesis with natural language command classification into a lightweight, local framework. Jarvis core component is a text-to-command classifier, built using a domain-specific fine-tuning of the Mistral 7B model, demonstrating low-latency inference. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that our solution outperforms larger online-hosted models, achieving 97.63 % intent recognition accuracy with an average processing latency of 1.39 s, making it well-suited for operations requiring quick response times. To support further research and fine-tuning, we provide an open-source implementation.

</details>


### [114] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASGARD：一种两阶段教师-学生框架，用于提升基于强化学习的无人机控制对动作空间攻击的运行时韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的无人机控制器易受动作空间攻击：攻击者在策略生成动作命令后、执行器执行前篡改动作命令。现有防御多针对策略输入攻击；针对动作空间攻击的方法通常在训练时重训练策略，无法在运行时对受损动作保持韧性。

Method: 提出 ASGARD 两阶段教师-学生流水线。教师阶段：编码器将无人机物理状态与动作攻击相关特权信息结合，生成动作攻击感知隐表示，用于训练强化学习控制策略，并训练监控器向执行器输出修正后的动作命令。学生阶段：编码器与监控器通过监督学习从教师对应模块蒸馏，仅使用无人机物理状态历史，实现在机部署。

Result: 在针对无人机不同动作命令的攻击场景中评估 ASGARD。结果表明，ASGARD 对动作空间攻击具有韧性，即使在攻击下也能完成任务；并能泛化到未见攻击，且对隐蔽攻击仍保持韧性。

Conclusion: ASGARD 通过教师-学生与特权信息蒸馏，使基于强化学习的无人机控制在运行时对动作空间攻击具备韧性，并可泛化至未见及隐蔽攻击。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>
